### [1] [Transient beam loading effects on energy loss during beam abort in high-current storage ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12661)
*Shuhei Obara,Yuji Hosaka,Ryota Saida,Akane Agui,Takao Asaka,Koichi Kan,Choji Saji,Kota Ueshima,Nobuyuki Nishimori*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究首次在纳米辐照同步辐射光源（NanoTerasu）存储环上系统测量了3至400毫安宽电流范围内的束流中止圈数（abort turns），发现高流强下束流因瞬态束流负载（transient beam loading）效应显著加速丢失（从3毫安时的435圈降至400毫安时的187圈），并通过时分辨腔体信号、追踪模拟与解析模型定量复现该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关闭射频腔的束流中止（beam abort）是衍射极限同步辐射光源中广泛使用的机器保护方案，其中从射频关闭到束流丢失的中止圈数是设计保护系统的关键参数，但该参数此前从未在高存储流强下被表征过。

Method: 在3 GeV的NanoTerasu存储环上，对3至400毫安宽电流范围进行了中止圈数测量；结合时分辨腔体拾取信号（time-resolved cavity pickup signals）、追踪模拟（tracking simulations）和解析模型，并利用从稳态腔体-束流响应到中止瞬态的近似扩展进行定量分析。

Result: 测量显示中止圈数具有明确的电流依赖性：束流在高存储电流下丢失显著加快，中止圈数从3毫安时的435圈降至400毫安时的187圈；该行为在高压下并非与束流负载成正比而是趋于饱和；时分辨腔体信号、追踪模拟和解析模型均定量复现了这一趋势。

Conclusion: 瞬态束流负载是高电流第四代存储环中止动力学中的主导机制，机器保护系统设计中需纳入该效应；基于稳态腔体-束流响应到中止瞬态的近似扩展可良好复现实验结果，表明中止圈数可纯数值预测。

Abstract: Beam abort by shutting off the rf cavities is a widely used machine-protection scheme in modern diffraction-limited synchrotron light source storage rings. In this process, the stored beam loses energy turn by turn until it is intercepted by a dedicated absorber. A key parameter in this process is the number of turns after the rf shutdown until the subsequent beam loss, especially for the beam-size blow-up abort scheme. Despite its importance for designing an abort protection system, this quantity has not been characterized at high stored currents. We report measurements of abort turns over a broad current range from 3 to 400 mA in the 3-GeV NanoTerasu storage ring. The results show a clear current dependence: the beam is lost significantly faster at higher stored currents, with the number of turns until beam loss reduced from 435 at 3 mA to 187 at 400 mA. Our results indicate that transient beam loading induced by the aborting beam in empty rf cavities is the primary mechanism responsible for the enhanced energy loss. The number-of-turns behavior is not simply proportional to the beam loading at high current but saturates. Time-resolved cavity pickup signals, together with tracking simulations and analytical modeling, quantitatively reproduce the observed trend. Our experimental results and theoretical modeling demonstrate that transient beam loading significantly influences abort dynamics in high-current fourth-generation storage rings, emphasizing the need to incorporate this effect into machine-protection system design. Our approximate extension of the steady-state cavity-beam response to beam-abort transients reproduces experimental results well, indicating that the number of turns during a beam abort can be predicted purely numerically.

</details>

### [2] [Blind Spots of the Zwanziger Horizon Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12413)
*Daniel G. Tedesco*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文考察了Faddeev-Popov算子失去正定性所定义的第一Gribov视界（Gribov horizon）与Zwanziger视界函数（horizon function）之间的构型对应关系，研究在径向SU(2) hedgehog背景场中，与Gribov视界出现相关的谱方向是否必然能被进入视界函数的源所探测到，包括临界子空间退化的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究第一Gribov视界与Zwanziger视界函数在构型层面（configurationwise）的精确对应关系，特别是源的谱可及性（spectral accessibility）问题，以厘清两种视界描述在谱方向上的内在联系。

Method: 以三维和四维欧氏空间中具有角向对称性的径向SU(2) hedgehog背景为例，利用角对称性约束源空间，同时通过径向剖面控制Faddeev-Popov阈值的有序性；采用变分估计（variational estimates）与有限体积计算（finite-volume calculations）刻画光滑径向剖面的阈值结构；对正则规范BPST背景因全空间零能量行为与视界源的定义域问题单独处理。

Result: 文章通过变分估计和有限体积计算表征了光滑径向背景下的Faddeev-Popov阈值结构，并分类讨论了临界子空间退化时源对谱方向的可及性，识别出需要单独处理的全空间BPST背景中的定义域问题。

Conclusion: 该研究仅限于构型谱性质的分析，不涉及这些背景在Yang-Mills泛函积分中的统计权重（statistical weighting），由此将问题限定在Gribov视界与视界函数之间的几何—谱关系层面。

Abstract: We examine the configurationwise relation between the first Gribov horizon, defined by loss of positivity of the Faddeev-Popov operator, and Zwanziger's horizon function, which probes the inverse operator through background-dependent sources. The analysis focuses on whether the spectral directions associated with the onset of the Gribov horizon are necessarily accessible to the sources entering the horizon function, including situations in which the critical subspace is degenerate. This question is studied for radial SU(2) hedgehog backgrounds in three and four Euclidean dimensions, where angular symmetry constrains the source sector while the radial profile controls the ordering of Faddeev-Popov thresholds. Variational estimates and finite-volume calculations are used to characterize the threshold structure for smooth radial profiles. The regular-gauge BPST background is treated separately because of domain issues associated with zero-energy behavior and the horizon source in the full-space setting. The discussion is restricted to configurationwise spectral properties and does not address the statistical weighting of these backgrounds in the Yang-Mills functional integral.

</details>


### [3] [Topological properties around the Roberge-Weiss transition in $N_f = 2 + 1 + 1$ QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12883)
*Massimo D'Elia,Fabio Siliberto,Kevin Zambello*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限虚化学势下QCD在Roberge-Weiss相变过程中的拓扑性质，采用物理夸克质量的N_f=2+1+1 QCD（stout改进交错费米子），分析了拓扑磁化率和四阶累积量的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究QCD在Roberge-Weiss相变附近的拓扑性质，尤其是拓扑磁化率和相关累积量在不同温度区间下的行为差异。

Method: 采用N_f=2+1+1 QCD，物理夸克质量，stout改进交错费米子（stout improved staggered fermions）离散化，主要使用两个不同的紧致维长度N_t=8和N_t=10进行格点模拟。

Result: T_RW和普适类结果与N_f=2+1情形（略有不同的离散化方案）一致；拓扑磁化率在T≈T_RW以下几乎恒定，在更高温度快速衰减；四阶累积量b_2在T_RW之后迅速与稀薄瞬子气体近似（Dilute Instanton Gas Approximation）的预测相容。

Conclusion: b_2在T_RW附近表现出尖锐的转变，这与纯规范理论（pure gauge theories）行为更相似，而非全QCD沿标准热线的较慢转变，后者在先前研究中被观察到。

Abstract: We investigate the topological properties of QCD across the finite temperature Roberge-Weiss transition, which is found for particular values of the imaginary baryon chemical potential. Our study is conducted for $N_f = 2+1+1$ QCD with physical quark masses, discretized via stout improved staggered fermions and considering mostly two different values of the compactifed dimension, $N_t = 8$ and $N_t = 10$. Results for $T_{RW}$ and for the associated universality class are consistent with those found in the $N_f = 2 + 1$ case with a slightly different discretization. The topological susceptibility appears to be practically constant for $T \lesssim T_{RW}$, then rapidly decaying for higher temperatures. The analysis of the fourth order cumulant of the topological charge distribution, $b_2$, reveals that it is compatible with the prediction of the Dilute Instanton Gas Approximation right after $T_{RW}$, showing thus a sharp transition, which is more similar to what observed in pure gauge theories rather than to full QCD along the standard thermal line, where instead a slower transition was observed in previous studies.

</details>

Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb探测器在√s=7和8 TeV的质子-质子对撞数据（总积分亮度3 fb⁻¹），通过振幅分析（amplitude analysis）对无粲三体衰变B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻的分支比与准两体CP破坏不对称性进行了测量，并采用三种互补方法（等巴近似、K-矩阵形式和准模型无关程序）处理主导的S波（S-wave）贡献，获得了多数中间态最精确的测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无粲三体B衰变（charmless three-body B decay）是研究强子强动力学和CP破坏来源的重要场所，而B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻振幅建模中最具挑战性的问题在于描述主导的K⁺π⁻和π⁺π⁻ S波贡献，因此需要发展互补的分析方法来精确测量各中间态的分支比和CP破坏不对称性，以建立更坚实的基准来检验超越标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的物理。

Method: 研究采用三种互补的振幅建模方法：基于等巴近似（isobar approximation）的物理动机解析模型、K-矩阵形式（K-matrix formalism）以及准模型无关程序（quasi-model-independent procedure），后者同时研究重叠交叉分波（overlapping crossing partial waves）。此外还考虑了π⁺π⁻末态分别通过直接ω(782)衰变或ρ(770)⁰-ω(782)混合两种替代方案来呈现的结果。

Result: 获得了绝大多数中间态迄今最精确的分支比（branching fraction）和CP不对称（CP asymmetry）测量结果；三种独立方法的分析结果彼此一致，为强子强动力学和B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻中CP破坏效应的起源提供了新的认识。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了更坚实的参考基准，可用于干净地检验超越标准模型的、模型无关的新物理（model-independent new physics），并为理解强子动力学和CP破坏机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: The branching fractions and quasi-two-body $C\!P$-violating asymmetries of intermediate states obtained through an amplitude analysis of the charmless three-body decay $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ are reported. The analysis is based on $pp$ collision data at centre-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s}=7$ and $8\,\text{TeV}$ recorded with the LHCb detector, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. The most challenging aspect of the amplitude modelling lies in the description of the dominant $K^+ π^-$ and $π^+ π^-$ S-wave contributions. This is achieved by three complementary approaches based on a physically motivated analytic model built on the isobar approximation, the K-matrix formalism, and a quasi-model-independent procedure in which overlapping crossing partial waves are simultaneously studied. In addition, alternative sets of results are presented, considering the $π^+ π^-$ final state to manifest either through direct $ω(782)$ decays or $ρ(770)^0\textrm{-}ω(782)$ mixing. The most precise measurements of branching fractions and $C\!P$ asymmetries are obtained for the vast majority of intermediate states, establishing firmer reference points against which to cleanly probe model-independent physics beyond the Standard Model. The results from all three approaches agree and provide new insight into strong dynamics and the origin of $C\!P$-violation effects in $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays.

</details>


### [5] [Resolution of outstanding puzzles in $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12613)

Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb探测器2011—2012年采集的3 fb⁻¹ pp碰撞数据，对B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变进行了分波振幅分析。通过放宽关于K*₀(1430)⁰共振与K⁺π⁻标量本底之间关系的幺正性假设，模型与数据的一致性显著改善，并发现了十个中间衰变过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对B→Kππ衰变扇区的研究在S波（S-wave）贡献的建模上存在未解决的关键问题，特别是关于K*₀(1430)⁰共振与缓慢变化的标量部分之间关系的假设需要重新审视。

Method: 基于LHCb探测器在2011—2012年记录的pp碰撞数据（积分亮度为3 fb⁻¹）对B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变进行振幅分析（amplitude analysis），其中关键改进是放宽了关于K*₀(1430)⁰共振与K⁺π⁻中标量分量关系的幺正性（unitarity）假设。

Result: 放宽假设后模型与数据的一致性显著改善；B⁺→K*₀(1430)⁰π⁺的分支比挑战了此前B→K*₀(1430)π衰变主导B→Kππ相空间的实验共识，与QCD因子化（QCD factorisation）而非微扰QCD（perturbative QCD）的预测相符；标量π⁺π⁻振幅可用既有态建模，无需此前分析中的临时性f_X(1300)组分；共发现十个中间衰变过程。

Conclusion: 本分析通过放宽幺正性假设改善了B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻分波振幅模型，其结果逆转了此前测量中支持微扰QCD的结论，转而与QCD因子化预测一致，同时消除了对临时性f_X(1300)组分的依赖。

Abstract: An amplitude analysis of $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays is presented, based on $pp$ collision data recorded with the LHCb detector in 2011--2012, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. Previous studies of the $B \!\to K ππ$ sector have left key unresolved questions concerning the model of the S-wave contributions. A pivotal finding is that relaxing unitarity-based assumptions about the relation between the $K^*_0(1430)^0$ resonance and the slowly varying scalar part in $K^+π^-$ leads to considerably better agreement between the model and data. The $B^+ \!\to K^*_0(1430)^0 π^+$ branching fraction now challenges the experimental consensus that $B \!\to K^*_0(1430) π$ decays dominate the $B \!\to K ππ$ phase space, aligning with the predictions of QCD factorisation rather than perturbative QCD, thus reversing the agreement found in previous measurements. With this increased flexibility, it also becomes possible to model the scalar $π^+ π^-$ amplitude using established states, eliminating the need for the ad-hoc ``$f_X(1300)$'' component included in previous analyses of the $B \!\to Kππ$ sector. These advances facilitate the discovery of ten intermediate decays.

</details>


### [6] [Observation of several sources of $C\!P$ violation in $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12614)

Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb探测器2011-2012年采集的3 fb⁻¹质子-质子碰撞数据，对B⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变进行了振幅分析（amplitude analysis），首次确认了六种CP破坏（CP violation）现象，包括在B⁺ → ρ(770)⁰K⁺衰变中发现准双体CP破坏、在B⁺ → f₂(1270)K⁺衰变中确立振幅级CP破坏，以及在全弹性S波ππ-ππ重散射区和涉及自旋3共振的衰变中首次报告CP破坏证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过B⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变的振幅分析，系统地搜索和量化多种CP破坏现象，以进一步检验标准模型对CP破坏的预言并寻找可能的新物理踪迹。

Method: 该分析基于LHCb探测器在2011-2012年记录的质子-质子碰撞数据，积分亮度为3 fb⁻¹，采用振幅分析方法对B⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变进行全面研究，分别考察准双体衰变、振幅级CP破坏、S波ππ-ππ重散射、自旋3共振以及不同ππ分波间的干涉效应。

Result: 研究发现了六种此前未被确认的CP破坏现象：在B⁺ → ρ(770)⁰K⁺衰变中发现准双体CP破坏；在B⁺ → f₂(1270)K⁺衰变中确立振幅级CP破坏；首次报告全弹性S波ππ-ππ重散射区域的CP破坏证据；首次报告涉及任何自旋3共振衰变的CP破坏证据；在S-P波干涉中观测到显著的CP破坏效应；在S-D波干涉中发现CP破坏证据。

Conclusion: S-P波干涉中观测到的CP破坏和S-D波干涉中的CP破坏证据均须由长程相互作用（long-distance interactions）驱动，这表明这些CP破坏现象涉及超出简单旁观者图景的复杂动力学机制。

Abstract: An amplitude analysis of $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays is presented in which six $C\!P$-violating phenomena are judged to be of significance for the first time. This analysis is based on $pp$ collision data recorded with the LHCb detector in 2011-2012, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. Quasi-two-body $C\!P$ violation in $B^+ \!\to ρ(770)^0 K^+$ decays is discovered, while $C\!P$ violation at amplitude level is established in $B^+ \!\to f_2(1270) K^+$ decays. First evidence for $C\!P$ violation is reported in both the fully elastic S-wave $ππ$-$ππ$ rescattering region and also for any decay involving a spin-3 resonance. Additionally, significant $C\!P$-violation effects are identified in the interference between different $ππ$ partial waves, with observation in S-P wave interference and evidence in S-D wave interference, both of which must be driven by long-distance interactions.

</details>


### [7] [Axion-Like Particle Search with a Hybrid Cherenkov-Scintillation Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12616)
*A. A. Aguilar-Arevalo,S. Biedron,J. Boissevain,M. Borrego,L. Bugel,M. Chavez-Estrada,J. M. Conrad,R. L. Cooper,J. R. Distel,J. C. D'Olivo,E. Dunton,B. Dutta,D. E. Fields,M. Gold,E. Guardincerri,E. C. Huang,N. Kamp,D. Kim,K. Knickerbocker,W. C. Louis,C. F. Macias-Acevedo,R. Mahapatra,J. Mezzetti,J. Mirabal,M. J. Mocko,D. A. Newmark,P. deNiverville,C. O'Connor,V. Pandey,D. Poulson,H. Ray,E. Renner,T. J. Schaub,A. Schneider,M. H. Shaevitz,D. Smith,W. Sondheim,A. M. Szelc,C. Taylor,A. Thompson,W. H. Thompson,R. T. Thornton,M. Tripathi,R. Van Berg,R. G. Van de Water,J. Zettlemoyer*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究首次利用光束倾倒装置（beam-dump facility）上的混合切连科夫-闪烁体（Cherenkov-scintillation）探测器开展轴子类粒子（axion-like particles，ALPs）搜索的概念验证实验，基于CCM（Coherent CAPTAIN-Mills）实验的CCM200探测器，通过构建四个观测量并组合为似然比分类器，在10⁻³ MeV至10 MeV质量范围内未观测到显著超出背景的过剩信号，但实现了优于先前CCM120搜索的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子类粒子是超出标准模型的热门候选粒子，但此前基于CCM120的搜索灵敏度受限。本文旨在利用混合切连科夫-闪烁体探测技术提升背景抑制能力，从而突破现有搜索的灵敏度极限，为未来实验验证物理潜力。

Method: CCM200探测器为10吨液氩光收集探测器，配备200个光电倍增管（PMT），其中80%涂覆波长位移材料、20%保持未涂覆，结合纳秒级时间分辨实现对瞬发切连科夫辐射与液氩闪烁光的鉴别。分析构建四个利用切连科夫辐射和事件拓扑的观测量，并将其组合为似然比分类器以有效抑制稳态背景。

Result: 在轴子质量10⁻³ MeV < m_a < 10 MeV范围内，未观测到超出预期背景的统计显著过剩信号。然而，混合探测方案带来的背景抑制改进使本分析在曝光量更少的条件下仍超越了先前CCM120搜索的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该结果展示了混合切连科夫-闪烁体探测器在束流倾倒装置上搜索ALPs的物理潜力，为未来更大规模或进一步优化配置的类似实验提供了概念验证依据。

Abstract: This analysis presents the first proof-of-concept search for axion-like particles (ALPs) using a hybrid Cherenkov-scintillation detector at a beam-dump facility. The work is based on the Coherent CAPTAIN-Mills (CCM) experiment, a 10-ton liquid argon light collection detector located at Los Alamos National Laboratory. The CCM200 detector is instrumented with 200 photomultiplier tubes (PMTs), providing approximately 50% photocathode coverage. To enable optical discrimination, 80% of the PMTs are coated with a wavelength-shifting material while the remaining 20% are left uncoated. This configuration, combined with nanosecond-scale timing resolution, provides sensitivity to prompt Cherenkov radiation while maintaining efficient detection of liquid argon scintillation light [1, 2]. This analysis constructs four observables that exploit the Cherenkov emission and event topology expected from ALP-induced electromagnetic interactions. These observables are combined into a likelihood-ratio classifier that provides powerful rejection of steady-state backgrounds. No statistically significant excess above the expected background prediction is observed for $10^{-3}~\mathrm{MeV} < m_a < 10~\mathrm{MeV}$. Nevertheless, the improved background rejection enabled by hybrid Cherenkov-scintillation detection allows this analysis to surpass the sensitivity of the previous CCM120 search [3] despite less exposure and demonstrate the physics potential for hybrid Cherenkov-scintillation detectors.

</details>

Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的积分亮度为20.3 fb⁻¹、质心能量为3.773 GeV的数据样本，通过单标记（single-tag）技术从双光子聚变过程e⁺e⁻→e⁺e⁻η′的微分玻恩截面中，对η′介子的类空单虚跃迁形状因子（transition form factor）进行了高精度测量，覆盖Q²∈[0.1, 6.0] GeV²区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: η′介子的跃迁形状因子是理解其内部结构与量子色动力学（QCD）低能行为的重要观测量，但此前在低Q²区域的精确测量尚属空白，因此需要对Q²|F(Q²)|进行更高精度的直接测定。

Method: 采用单标记技术（single-tag technique），仅探测一个散射轻子，从双光子融合过程e⁺e⁻→e⁺e⁻γγ*→e⁺e⁻η′的微分玻恩截面（differential Born cross section）中提取跃迁形状因子F(Q²)。

Result: 测量覆盖Q²∈[0.1, 6.0] GeV²区间，达到前所未有的精度：在Q²<1.5 GeV²区域内不确定性优于3.0%，并首次在Q²<0.3 GeV²区域提供了直接测定结果。

Conclusion: 该测量提供了η′介子单虚类空跃迁形状因子在宽Q²范围内的精密数据，填补了低Q²区域的实验空白，为进一步检验低能有效理论提供了重要实验约束。

Abstract: Using a data sample corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $20.3\ \text{fb}^{-1}$, collected with the BESIII detector at a center-of-mass energy of $3.773\ \text{GeV}$ at the BEPCII collider, we report a precision measurement of the product $Q^2|F(Q^2)|$, where $F(Q^2)$ is the single-virtual space-like transition form factor of the $η'$ meson and $Q^2$ is the squared momentum transfer of the tagged virtual photon. The transition form factor is extracted from the differential Born cross section of the two-photon fusion processes $e^+e^- \to e^+e^-γγ^* \to e^+e^-η^\prime$ using a single-tag technique, where only one scattered lepton is detected. The measurement covers $Q^2 \in [0.1, 6.0]$ GeV$^2$, achieving unprecedented precision, better than $3.0\%$ for $Q^2 < 1.5$ GeV$^2$, and providing the first direct determination at $Q^2 < 0.3$ GeV$^2$.

</details>


### [9] [MANGO: An Autodiff Neutrino Oscillation Engine for Differentiable Analysis Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13429)
*Pierre Granger*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出MANGO（一种中微子梯度振荡器），一个可组合的中微子振荡计算引擎，其中所有计算量对全部输入（包括传播几何、探测器深度和地球壳层密度）均可微。相比传统解析概率公式中不存在的这些量，MANGO以常数倍的额外开销实现敏感度计算，并通过精确灵敏度信号使梯度连续流向探测器响应和似然函数，支持地球断层成像等实验设计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡概率的计算已得到解决，但其导数（derivative）的计算尚未解决。传统解析概率公式无法纳入传播几何、探测器深度和地球壳层密度等输入量，导致实验设计中的敏感度分析存在根本性局限。

Method: MANGO采用可组合的振荡引擎设计，所有输出对全部输入可微；支持多种传播模式（真空、恒密度、分层PREM、任意剖面、绝热太阳传播等）及非标准相互作用（non-standard interactions）、3+N惰性态、退相干和非幺正混合等前沿模型。利用反向模式（reverse-mode）自动微分，其开销取决于输出维度而非输入维度，因此对全部369个密度、电子分数和壳层半径参数的敏感度计算开销不超过6个标准振荡参数。

Result: 计算所有369个地球分层参数（密度、电子分数、壳层半径）的敏感度所需的额外开销仅为前向传递的2.5-3倍恒定量。在风格化的地球断层成像分析中，MANGO以单次传递计算六区径向密度模型的边缘化不确定度，并通过微分逆Fisher矩阵评估其对探测器角分辨率的敏感度。三味和分层地球概率与外部基准（OscProb、NuFast-Earth）在10^-9到10^-5范围内一致，所有前向模型、BSM限制和微分路径均通过精确解析解和有限差分验证。

Conclusion: MANGO通过精确的灵敏度信号使梯度能连续穿过概率阶段、探测器响应、事件加权、分箱和似然函数，为实验设计提供了传统解析概率公式无法实现的设计指标（experimental-design metric），并将敏感度计算成本降至与标准振荡参数相当的水平。

Abstract: Computing neutrino oscillation probabilities is a solved problem; computing their derivatives is not. We present MANGO (MANGO: A Neutrino Gradient Oscillator), a composable oscillation engine in which every computed quantity is differentiable with respect to all inputs, including propagation geometry, detector depth, and individual Earth-shell densities. None of these quantities appear in traditional analytic probability formulas. The engine supports vacuum, constant-density, layered-PREM, arbitrary-profile, and adiabatic solar propagation, alongside front-ends for non-standard interactions, 3+N sterile states, decoherence, and non-unitary mixing. Because reverse-mode cost depends on output rather than input dimension, evaluating sensitivities incurs a constant 2.5-3x forward-pass overhead. As a result, calculating sensitivities for all 369 density, electron-fraction, and shell-radius parameters of a layered Earth costs no more than the 6 standard oscillation parameters. By maintaining exact sensitivity signals, MANGO allows gradients to flow continuously past the probability stage and through detector response, event weighting, binning, and likelihoods. We demonstrate this on a stylized Earth-tomography analysis. In a single pass, MANGO computes the marginalized uncertainty on a six-zone radial density model and, by differentiating through the inverse Fisher matrix, evaluates its sensitivity with respect to detector angular resolution. This provides an experimental-design metric unreachable using traditional analytic probability formulas. Three-flavor and layered-Earth probabilities match external benchmarks (OscProb, NuFast-Earth) to within 10^-9 to 10^-5, while all forward models, BSM limits, and differentiation paths are verified against exact analytic solutions and finite differences.

</details>

### [10] [A Lapse in the Cosmological Constant Problem with Bulk Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12460)
*Justin Khoury,Benjamin Muntz,Antonio Padilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在先前基于紧致额外维各向异性标度（anisotropic scaling）的宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）问题方案基础上，引入 z=1 形变使框架超越超局域（ultra-local）极限，证明了真空能（vacuum energy）消除机制在背景方程层面依然成立，通过 Fiery-Pauli 关系筛选出健康的二次谱，并在五维爱因斯坦引力的协变形式中实现了该机制，同时指出绕紧致方向传播的物质会产生有限的、拓扑敏感的 Casimir 贡献，需额外的谱条件加以抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前提出的基于紧致额外维各向异性标度的方法仅在超局域 z=0 极限下有效，本文旨在将该框架推广到 z=1 形变的非超局域情形，检验真空能消除机制是否依然成立，并寻找一个协变且物理上健康的实现。

Method: 通过在紧致方向引入 z=1 形变恢复动力学，分析背景方程中的真空能消除机制；检查外曲率耦合（extrinsic-curvature couplings）的二次谱，利用 Fierz-Pauli 关系消除标量鬼场（scalar ghost）；基于五维爱因斯坦引力、拓扑形式通量（top-form flux）和类空 khoron 标量场构建协变形式化，其中全局约束来自 khoron 叶空间上的辅助 einbein；最后允许物质在紧致维中传播以考察 Casimir 贡献。

Result: 背景方程层面真空能消除仍然成立；一般的曲率耦合会传播额外的标量鬼场，但 Fierz-Pauli 关系可给出健康的二次谱；所提出的协变形式中，重整化后的物质真空能常数平移从内禀爱因斯坦方程中代数式消去；绕紧致维传播的物质产生有限且拓扑敏感的 Casimir 贡献，机制无法自动消除它们，需施加额外谱条件抑制。

Conclusion: 该机制可推广至 z=1 形变的非超局域框架，并以五维爱因斯坦引力加拓扑通量与 khoron 标量场的协变形式实现；但若要完全消除绕紧致维传播物质产生的 Casimir 贡献，仍需对体自由度施加额外的谱条件。

Abstract: We have previously proposed a new approach to the cosmological constant problem based on anisotropic scaling in a compact extra dimension. In the ultra-local $z=0$ limit, the interplay between a projectable lapse, foliation-preserving diffeomorphisms, and higher-form fluxes renders the gravitational field equations insensitive to radiative corrections to the matter vacuum energy. Here we extend this framework beyond the ultra-local limit by introducing $z=1$ deformations that restore dynamics along the compact direction. We show that vacuum-energy cancellation persists at the level of the background equations, while generic extrinsic-curvature couplings propagate an additional scalar ghost. A healthy quadratic spectrum selects the Fierz-Pauli relation between these couplings. This motivates a particularly simple realization in terms of five-dimensional Einstein gravity, a top-form flux, and a spacelike khoron scalar field that dynamically defines a preferred projectable foliation. In this covariant formulation, the global constraint arises from an auxiliary einbein on the space of khoron leaves, and constant shifts of the renormalized matter vacuum energy cancel algebraically from the intrinsic Einstein equations. Finally, allowing matter to propagate around the compact dimension generates finite, topology-sensitive Casimir contributions that are not automatically removed by the mechanism. Suppressing these contributions requires additional spectral conditions on the propagating bulk degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [11] [Stabilizer complexity and the Python's lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12472)
*Abhirup Bhattacharya,Jatin Narde,Onkar Parrikar,Suprakash Paul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息CFT中具有固定能量边界条件的部分纠缠热（PET）态对应一侧约化密度矩阵的稳定子复杂度（stabilizer complexity），发现其Wigner负性（Wigner negativity）在无python's lunch时复杂度为O(1)，存在python's lunch时呈指数增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解全息量子引力理论中纠缠与量子计算复杂度之间的深层联系，特别是通过稳定子复杂度这一可操作度量来刻画边界子区域约化态经典模拟的难易程度。

Method: 方法基于CFT谱和重算子插入的伪随机性（pseudorandomness）假设，研究PET态相对于给定能量下微正则密度矩阵的Wigner负性，并将该量与非等距（extremal surface）面积建立联系。

Result: 研究发现PET态的Wigner负性等于exp[(A_out - A_min)/(8G_N)]，其中A_out为外极值面积，A_min为最小极值面积；因此无python's lunch时边界子区域约化态稳定子复杂度为O(1)，有python's lunch时呈指数增强。

Conclusion: 该结果表明全息体几何中python's lunch结构的存在与否会显著影响边界量子态的经典模拟复杂度，为引力几何与量子计算复杂度之间的对应关系提供了新的证据。

Abstract: In this note, we study the stabilizer complexity of the reduced density matrix corresponding to one side of a partially entangled thermal (PET) state with fixed energy boundary conditions in a holographic CFT. In particular, we study Wigner negativity, an operationally meaningful magic monotone which can be interpreted as the complexity of classically simulating any quantum circuit preparation of the reduced state on the subregion. Using assumptions on the pseudorandomness of the CFT spectrum and the heavy operator insertion, we observe that the Wigner negativity of the PET state relative to the microcanonical density matrix at the given energy is given by $\exp\left[\frac{1}{8G_N}(A_{\text{out}} - A_{\text{min}})\right]$, where $A_{\text{out}}$ is the area of the outer extremal surface, while $A_{\text{min}}$ is the area of the minimal extremal surface. Thus, the stabilizer complexity of the reduced density matrix on the boundary subregion is $O(1)$ in the absence of a python's lunch, but gets exponentially enhanced in the presence of a python's lunch in the bulk geometry.

</details>


### [12] [Constraints on the $O(n)$ model from a negative number of flavors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12481)
*Daniele Artico,Jasper Roosmale Nepveu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将O(n)模型中味数n作为连续变量处理，利用O(n)与Sp(−n)之间的对偶关系，将谱约束推广到n为负偶数的情况，并通过对具有一般味结构的算子进行不可约表示分解，揭示了微扰论中不同算子标度维度（scaling dimension）的新的简并性，从而无需额外圈图计算即可导出任意φ^k型算子的两圈反常维度（anomalous dimension），并给出重整化群混合矩阵的新非重整化（non-renormalization）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在O(n)模型中，将味数n作为连续变量处理可产生对算子谱的非微扰约束；作者希望利用O(n)与Sp(−n)的对偶关系，将这些约束推广到n的负偶数值，并通过系统的表示论分解来揭示微扰论中算子标度维度之间的隐藏简并性，为高圈反常维度和重整化群结构提供新的理解。

Method: 本文利用O(n)与Sp(−n)之间的对偶关系，将谱约束扩展到n为负偶数的情况；将具有一般味结构的算子在不可约表示（irreducible representations）下进行显式分解；在此基础上利用微扰论中揭示出的算子标度维度之间的新型简并性，从已有结果直接推导任意φ^k型算子的两圈反常维度；并进一步将该机制应用于重整化群混合矩阵的重整化结构分析，在特定算子基中给出非重整化结果。

Result: 研究揭示了在不同算子之间标度维度上存在的新颖简并性，该简并性对任意n值均成立；由此从现有结果中不经额外圈图计算即可导出任意φ^k型算子的两圈反常维度；同一机制还决定了重整化群混合矩阵的特定模式，在特定算子基中产生了新的非重整化结果。

Conclusion: 作者指出该框架可进一步推广到算符乘积展开（OPE）系数之间的关系，以及时空维数连续延拓下由消逝性（evanescence）产生的类似约束，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Treating the number of flavors $n$ as a variable in the $O(n)$ model leads to non-perturbative constraints on the spectrum of operators. Using the duality between $O(n)$ and $Sp(-n)$, we extend these relations to negative even values of $n$ and we make them explicit by decomposing operators with general flavor structure into irreducible representations. In perturbation theory, we exploit this structure to reveal novel degeneracies in the scaling dimensions of different operators, which persist for arbitrary $n$. This allows us to derive the two-loop anomalous dimension of any $φ^k$-type operator from existing results without additional loop calculations. The same mechanism dictates patterns in renormalization group mixing matrices, yielding new non-renormalization results in a specific operator basis. We comment on extending this framework to relations between operator product expansion coefficients and to analogous constraints arising from evanescence under continuation in the number of spacetime dimensions.

</details>


### [13] [Type III von Neumann Algebras are Magical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12512)

*Regina Rusch,Thomas Franosch*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文求解了倾斜余弦势中布朗粒子的福克-普朗克方程（Fokker-Planck equation），推导出包含两个波矢的广义中间散射函数（intermediate scattering function, ISF），借助Bloch定理构建谱理论框架进行数值求解，并通过含时微扰理论获得低阶矩（均方位移、时变扩散系数、偏度、非高斯参数），经布朗动力学（Brownian dynamics）模拟验证，对比了简谐近似与确定性极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间散射函数能够完整刻画系统的时空动力学，但在一维过阻尼布朗粒子被周期性倾斜势驱动的模型中尚缺乏解析与数值结合的完整求解方案。

Method: 利用Bloch定理利用势场周期性，在谱理论框架下数值计算特征函数与特征值以获取ISF和概率密度；采用含时微扰理论（time-dependent perturbation theory）展开ISF并推导低阶矩；通过布朗动力学模拟验证解析结果。

Result: 成功获得广义ISF（含两个波矢以描述周期势中的关联），得到均方位移、时变扩散系数、偏度和非高斯参数等低阶矩，数值结果与布朗动力学模拟一致，并在不同倾斜力（tilting force）强度区间下与简谐近似及确定性极限进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该谱理论方法结合微扰展开能够系统且完整地描述倾斜周期势中布朗粒子的空间-时间关联动力学，为后续实验观测和理论扩展提供了定量框架。

Abstract: We solve the Fokker-Planck equation for a Brownian particle in a tilted cosine potential and derive the intermediate scattering function (ISF), which captures the full spatio-temporal dynamics of the system. The model consists of a single overdamped Brownian particle in one dimension. We derive a generalized ISF comprising two wave vectors to describe correlations in the periodic potential. Exploiting the periodicity via Bloch's theorem, we formulate the problem within a spectral-theoretical framework and numerically compute the corresponding eigenfunctions and eigenvalues, from which we obtain the ISF and the probability density. Using time-dependent perturbation theory, we expand the ISF and derive low-order moments, including the mean-square displacement, time-dependent diffusivity, skewness, and the non-Gaussian parameter. Our analytical results are validated by Brownian-dynamics simulations and analyzed focussing on different regimes of the tilting force. The results are compared to a harmonic approximation and the deterministic limit.

</details>


### [30] [Local and quasilocal conservation laws of three-state IRF cellular automata and their quantum deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13080)
*Tomaž Prosen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用拼块-矩阵乘积方法（patch-matrix-product methods）研究了两种可逆三态“周围面对相互作用”（interaction-round-a-face）元胞自动机（cellular automata），即物种保持（species-preserving）和物种翻转（species-flipping）规则，以及介于两者之间的相干量子形变（coherent quantum deformation）。研究发现了准局域守恒律（quasi-local conservation laws）族、精确的Euler速度下界$\sqrt{3/23}$，以及量子形变中的精确不变态（scar候选态）和准局域准模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Klobas和Prosen于2022年引入了两种可逆三态周围面对相互作用元胞自动机规则并给出了外推的Euler速度值，但对其守恒律结构、Euler速度下界的严格性以及量子形变下的性质尚未充分理解，本文旨在通过拼块-矩阵乘积方法系统地刻画这些系统的代数结构与动力学性质。

Method: 采用拼块-矩阵乘积方法，对两种经典规则构建显式平移不变（translationally invariant）ansatz，发现其共享一个辅助维度为三（auxiliary-dimension-three）的单参数准局域守恒律族；通过取对数导数（logarithmic derivative）构造分析性辅助维度为二的守恒族，并在其闭包上计算非零Euler速度；对量子形变利用相同代数结构研究不变态与准模态。

Result: 两种经典规则共享的守恒律族中，唯一的非零Euler速度（Euler velocities）为$\pm\sqrt{3/23}$，从而$\sqrt{3/23}$（约0.361）是任何更大守恒扇区中最大Euler速度的严格下界，与Klobas和Prosen外推值一致。物种保持规则额外拥有交错生成族（staggered generating family），其对数导数构成无穷长的严格局域荷塔（tower of strictly local charges）。量子形变中获得精确低键维不变态（scar候选）、指数长寿命准局域准模态（quasimodes），以及投影掉三个基本局域荷后产生非零Mazur界（Mazur bound）的准局域对角荷（diagonal quasi-local charges）。

Conclusion: 本文结果严格证实了$\sqrt{3/23}$作为最大Euler速度下界的有效性，强烈支持物种翻转规则中已知声学活跃荷（sound-active charges）的完备性；同时展示了物种保持规则的局域荷塔结构，并揭示量子形变中保留的代数结构可产生精确不变态与非零Mazur界等非平衡动力学特征。

Abstract: Using patch-matrix-product methods, we study two reversible three-state interaction-round-a-face cellular automata introduced by Klobas and Prosen [J. Phys. A 55, 094003 (2022)] - the species-preserving and species-flipping rules - and a coherent quantum deformation interpolating between them. Within an explicit translationally invariant ansatz, the two classical rules share a one-parameter family of quasi-local conservation laws with an auxiliary-dimension-three realization. Every regular member is also the first centered logarithmic derivative of an analytic auxiliary-dimension-two family passing through the identity observable. On the closed span of this family and the three elementary local charges, the only nonzero Euler velocities are $\pm\sqrt{3/23}$. Consequently, $\sqrt{3/23}$ is a rigorous lower bound on the maximal Euler speed in any larger conserved sector. For the species-flipping rule, this value agrees with the reported extrapolated value $0.361$ of Klobas and Prosen, strongly supporting completeness of the known sound-active charges. The species-preserving rule admits, in addition, a staggered generating family whose logarithmic derivatives form an infinite tower of strictly local charges. In the quantum deformation, the same algebraic structures yield exact low-bond-dimension invariant states (scar candidates), exponentially long-lived quasi-local quasimodes, and diagonal quasi-local charges that produce a nonzero Mazur bound after projection away from the three elementary local charges.

</details>


### [31] [Thermodynamics of Learning: A Typed Four-Component Accounting of Memory, Fit, and Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12791)
*Akihito Sudo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为有限状态学习设备（finite-state learning devices）建立了一套类型化记账框架，将设备在训练侧与运行侧的价值分离为四个分量，并通过分离性定理、资本化记账等式和保值对齐域三个板块给出精准的理论刻画，阐明被记录信息与未来任务价值并非同一数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限学习设备所记录的信息与其在未来任务中实际具有的价值并不相同，这一核心观察构成了本文的出发点。现有理论缺乏对训练侧适配与未来运行价值之间差异的定量刻画，需要一种能将二者分离并各自记账的形式化框架。

Method: 作者建立了一个类型化记账框架，将有限状态学习设备分解为四个独立分量：训练侧适配泛函Φfit、记录相关存量JD=I(M;D)、更新侧搜索账本σM以及运行资本价值V(M;T,b)；后者定义为有信息协议类与删除记忆读取端口后从头重新优化的盲协议类之间的功差。在此基础上，作者分别从分离性（Separation）、资本化记账（Capitalization ledger）和保值（Value retention）三个方向推导精确恒等式与不等式界。

Result: （I）在分离性方面，对每个n存在一个设备族，使记录相关性与世界相关性以n ln 2增长而资本增益恰为零；在flat*区中无数据更新不会增加V。（II）在资本化记账方面，由flat*提取恒等式和普适记账恒等式给出在(F5')稳定及无丢弃记录相关性条件下，资本化效率ηcap≤1及其等号成立的充要条件。（III）在保值方面，给出了两层对齐域：一个无需记录侧独立性假设的值与侧信息调整记录适配I(M';D|Y)之间的精确汇率，以及联合侧信息中性与(M,D)⊥Y假设下的原始记录的汇率，其边界由一次性密码本（one-time-pad）见证明确刻画。

Conclusion: 这些结果是关于有限设备在任务分布漂移下价值保留的陈述，而非统计泛化理论。本文提供了将训练侧记录与其运行价值分离的精确记账机制，并给出了资本化效率的上界与保值交换率的完整刻画。

Abstract: What a finite learning device has recorded and what will hold value for it on future tasks are not the same quantity. We develop a typed accounting for finite-state learning devices that separates four components: a training-side fit functional $Φ_{\mathrm{fit}}$, the record-correlation stock $J_{D}=I(M;D)$, an update-side search ledger $σ_{M}$, and an operational capital value $V(M;T,b)$. This value is the work gap between an informed protocol class and a blind class obtained by deleting the memory-read port and re-optimizing from scratch. (I) Separation: for every $n$, there is a device family on which record correlation and world correlation grow by $n\ln 2$ while the capital gain is exactly zero. In the $\mathrm{flat}^{*}$ regime, data-free updates never increase $V$. (II) Capitalization ledger: an exact $\mathrm{flat}^{*}$ extraction identity and a universal ledger identity give, for (F5$'$)-stable $M$-local updates under a no-discarded-record-correlation condition (f), the bound $η_{\mathrm{cap}}\le 1$ for the capitalization efficiency $η_{\mathrm{cap}}=ΔV/(k T\,σ_{M})$, together with necessary and sufficient conditions for equality. (III) Value retention: for the retention gap $L_{\mathrm{gen}}$ and retention ratio $ρ_{\mathrm{gen}}$ (the former carries no sign constraint; the latter is defined for positive training-side value and is not confined to $[0,1]$) we give a two-layer alignment domain: an exact exchange rate between value and the side-information-adjusted record fit $I(M';D\mid Y)$ without any record-side-information independence assumption, and a raw record-stock exchange rate under a joint side-information neutrality condition $(M,D)\perp Y$, whose boundary is marked by an explicit one-time-pad witness. These are statements about finite-device value retention under task-distribution shift, not a theory of statistical generalization.

</details>


### [32] [Equivariant learning of a transferable three-dimensional classical density functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13506)
*Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何直接从三维平衡密度场中学习经典密度泛函理论（classical density functional theory）的核心泛函，无需自由能或化学势标签，同时保持空间对称性与变分一致性。学习得到的单一泛函可跨温度、系统尺寸和统计系综迁移，并准确恢复结构因子、状态方程、液-汽共存及界面展宽等未作为训练目标的性质，还可预测复杂三维几何中胶体间溶剂耗尽桥的力以及互通螺旋孔中的吸附行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体的集体行为强烈依赖于热力学条件、界面和限制条件，但预测每种新状态通常需进行单独的原子模拟。经典密度泛函理论提供了可复用的变分描述框架，但其核心的超额自由能泛函一般未知，已有的学习式近似主要局限于平面或低维情形。

Method: 直接从三维平衡密度场中学习超额自由能泛函，在训练过程中保持空间对称性（spatial symmetry）与变分一致性（variational consistency），且无需自由能或化学势标签作为监督信号。

Result: 单一学习到的泛函可跨温度、系统尺寸和统计系综迁移，能恢复结构因子（structure factor）、状态方程（equation of state）、液-汽共存（liquid–vapor coexistence）及界面展宽（interfacial broadening），这些均未作为训练目标。在三维复杂几何中，还可预测胶体间溶剂耗尽桥形成与破裂的非单调力，以及互通螺旋（gyroid）孔隙中的吸附行为。

Conclusion: 平衡密度数据可转化为具有可迁移性的热力学生成器（transferable thermodynamic generator），将微观液体结构连接至响应、相行为和集体现象，为预测液体新状态提供了一种无需逐例原子模拟的通用途径。

Abstract: Liquids exhibit collective behavior that depends sensitively on thermodynamic conditions, interfaces and confinement, yet predicting each new state commonly requires a separate atomistic simulation. Classical density functional theory offers a reusable variational description, but its central excess free-energy functional is generally unknown, and learned approximations have largely remained restricted to planar or lower-dimensional settings. Here we show that this functional can be learned directly from fully three-dimensional equilibrium density fields while preserving spatial symmetry and variational consistency, without free-energy or chemical-potential labels. A single learned functional transfers across temperatures, system sizes and statistical ensembles, and recovers structure factors, the equation of state, liquid--vapor coexistence and interfacial broadening, none of which are used as training targets. Applied to complex three-dimensional geometries, it predicts the non-monotonic force associated with formation and rupture of a solvent-depleted bridge between colloids and adsorption in an interconnected gyroid pore. These results demonstrate that equilibrium density data can be converted into a transferable thermodynamic generator connecting microscopic liquid structure to response, phase behavior and collective phenomena.

</details>

### [33] [Floquet Quasiparticle Poisoning of Frozonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12454)
*Haoyu Guo,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了周期性驱动（periodic driving）抑制通量子超导电路（fluxonium superconducting circuit）的约瑟夫森非线性后，在冻结点（freezing point）附近形成的“冻结子”（frozonium）电路中，准粒子（quasiparticle）诱导的耗散并不会被普遍压制。作者利用Floquet框架分析了驱动辅助的库珀对破裂与已有准粒子隧穿过程，发现高频下对生成受能隙破裂阈值控制，低频下多光子共振会产生显著速率增强，且准粒子隧穿表现出由谐波Floquet-Magnus谱组织的连通共振结构。结果表明，合适的运行区域需在动力学冻结与准粒子损耗之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动可在特定冻结点处抑制通量子电路的约瑟夫森非线性，产生近似谐波的Floquet谱，但动力学冻结行为是否同时抑制准粒子诱导的耗散尚不清楚，本文旨在系统分析该问题。

Method: 作者采用Floquet框架形式化描述冻结子（frozonium）中的准粒子过程，分别分析驱动辅助的库珀对破裂和已有准粒子的隧穿行为，并结合Floquet-Magnus谐波谱研究共振结构与避免交叉现象。

Result: 在高驱动频率下，库珀对生成受能隙破裂阈值控制；在较低频率下，多光子共振会导致显著的速率增强。准粒子隧穿表现出由谐波Floquet-Magnus谱组织的连通共振结构，共振杂化产生特征性的避免交叉（avoided crossings）。

Conclusion: 动力学冻结并不能普遍抑制准粒子诱导的耗散，合适的实验运行区域必须在动力学冻结与准粒子损耗之间取得平衡；该研究为识别远离有害共振的实验驱动参数提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Periodic driving can suppress the Josephson nonlinearity of a fluxonium superconducting circuit, producing a nearly harmonic Floquet spectrum at isolated freezing points [K. Lewellen et al., Newton 2, 100434 (2026)]. Here we show that this dynamically frozen behavior does not generically suppress quasiparticle-induced dissipation in the resulting frozonium circuit. We formulate quasiparticle processes in the frozonium using a Floquet framework and analyze both drive-assisted Cooper-pair breaking and tunneling of pre-existing quasiparticles. Pair generation is controlled by gap-breaking thresholds at high drive frequencies, while multiphoton resonances produce pronounced rate enhancements at lower frequencies. Quasiparticle tunneling exhibits connected resonance structures organized by the harmonic Floquet-Magnus spectrum near the freezing point, with resonant hybridization generating characteristic avoided crossings. Our results show that suitable operating regimes must balance dynamical freezing against quasiparticle loss and provide a framework for identifying experimental drive parameters away from harmful resonances.

</details>


### [34] [Finite-momentum coupling of Higgs and Bardasis--Schrieffer modes in superconductors with competing pairing channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12461)
*Samuel Awelewa,Yafis Barlas,Maxim Dzero*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了超导体中希格斯（Higgs）模与 Bardasis–Schrieffer（BS）激子之间的耦合机制与交叉回避（avoided crossing）条件。作者在扩展至领先 $1/\varepsilon_F$ 修正的 Nambu–Keldysh 准经典框架中，证明有限动量与粒子–空穴不对称性可产生闭合形式的直接耦合；但该耦合是否导致能级回避交叉由运动学（kinematics）而非耦合强度决定。在干净极限下，两种色散方向相反、永不简并；仅在中间散射强度（intermediate scattering）的无序极限下才可能出现回避交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有竞争配对通道的超导体中，s 波道的希格斯模（Higgs mode）与 d 波道的 BS 激子（Bardasis–Schrieffer exciton）是两种明确的亚能隙激发，但它们的混合在零动量下受点群对称性禁止、在任意动量下受粒子–空穴对称性禁止。作者旨在阐明这两种对称性禁止是否可被打破，以及混合效应能否导致可观测的实验信号。

Method: 作者采用扩展至领先 $1/\varepsilon_F$ 修正的 Nambu–Keldysh 准经典框架（quasiclassical framework），并纳入自洽屏蔽库仑势（self-consistently screened Coulomb potential），推导出有限动量与粒子–空穴不对称性共同产生的直接耦合项，并给出闭合形式表达式。随后分别在干净极限和弱无序极限（exact dirty-limit results）下分析了两种激发态的色散关系及其交叉条件。

Result: 有限动量与粒子–空穴不对称性确实能打破原有的对称性禁止，产生直接耦合（以闭合形式给出）。但干净极限下，希格斯模并非亚能隙极点而是锚定于配对断裂边缘（pair-breaking edge）的共振，其在 $(v_Fq)^2$ 中以系数 1 色散，而 BS 束缚模色散更慢，两者分支分离而永不简并，因此不产生回避交叉。无序极限下，无序使振幅共振脱离边缘并反转其色散，可在中间散射强度下与 BS 模产生回避交叉，耦合大小决定混合分支的劈裂。

Conclusion: 希格斯模与 BS 激子之间的耦合是否产生可探测的回避交叉由运动学而非耦合强度决定；干净极限下两者永远无法简并，而适度的无序可逆转色散以实现混合。该发现对超导中集体模式的实验探测（如光谱学手段）具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: In superconductors with competing pairing channels, two well defined excitations exist below the pair-breaking edge: the Higgs mode of the condensed $s$-wave channel and the Bardasis--Schrieffer (BS) exciton of the subdominant $d$-wave channel. Their mixing is doubly forbidden --- by point-group symmetry at zero momentum and because the two reside in the amplitude and phase sectors of the order parameter respectively, by particle--hole symmetry at every momentum. Working in a Nambu--Keldysh quasiclassical framework extended to leading $1/\varepsilon_F$ corrections and including the self-consistently screened Coulomb potential, we show that finite momentum combined with particle--hole asymmetry generates a direct coupling which we obtain in closed form. Whether this coupling produces an avoided crossing is decided, however, not by its magnitude but by kinematics. In the clean limit the Higgs is not a sub-gap pole but a resonance pinned to the pair-breaking edge, which disperses with coefficient unity in $(v_Fq)^2$, while the bound BS mode disperses more slowly: the two branches therefore separate rather than converge and never become degenerate. The obstruction is specific to the clean limit: exact dirty-limit results show that disorder detaches the amplitude resonance from the edge and reverses its dispersion, which can result in an avoided crossing with the BS mode at intermediate scattering. In that regime, the coupling computed here would set the splitting between the hybridized branches. We discuss the experimental implications of these results.

</details>


### [35] [The nature of the "pseudogap" in the insulating phase of highly disordered superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12508)
*Mark Nikolaevsky,Abhisek Samanta,Nandini Trivedi,Aviad Frydman*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过超高电阻平面隧穿谱实验与吸引哈伯德模型的量子蒙特卡洛模拟相结合，在无序非晶态氧化铟薄膜的超导体-绝缘体转变（superconductor-insulator transition）深绝缘区域中探测单粒子激发谱，实验与理论在俘获库珀对（Cooper pair）绝缘态方面取得定量一致，证实了局域化库珀对在绝缘相中存续并导致赝隙（pseudogap）与绝缘隙共源的物理图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管理论预测局域库珀对可在绝缘相深处存续，但由于高绝缘薄膜的隧穿测量需要超高电阻结和皮安（picoampere）级电流灵敏度，直接谱学确认在实验上长期不可及，亟需新的实验手段加以验证。

With increasing temperature, the gap fills rather than closes, while coherence peaks are suppressed and spectral weight redistributes to energies far exceeding the gap scale. Such large deformations are promising from the point of view of the detection of continuous gravitational waves by current and future detectors, and motivate further work on more realistic stellar models.

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型（diffusion model）的解析延拓（analytic continuation）框架，用于从虚时关联函数（iTCF）重建实频域谱。与传统回归方法不同的是，该框架学习给定iTCF下所有一致谱的分布，从而能够量化不确定性和评估每个反演问题的内在难度。作者引入不确定性伪体积（uncertainty pseudo-volume）作为硬度度量，并在液态仲氢的路径积分分子动力学模拟数据上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析延拓涉及的逆拉普拉斯变换是病态问题（ill-posed problem），存在大量谱与同一iTCF相容。传统的回归型神经网络方法只能输出单一解（大致相当于真实解空间上的平均），无法捕捉合理的功率谱（power spectrum）的完整分布，缺乏对不确定性的量化能力。

Abstract: Inverse problems are ubiquitous in physics, chemistry, and engineering, arising when reconstructing hidden quantities from indirect measurements. A key example is the analytic continuation of imaginary-time correlation functions (iTCFs) to the real-frequency domain. This process requires an inverse Laplace transform, which is inherently ill-posed and highly sensitive to small input variations. Recent neural network (NN)-based methods have shown promising results by learning mappings from imaginary-time to real-frequency spectra, often outperforming traditional techniques such as maximum entropy. However, because the problem is ill-posed, many spectra fit the same iTCF. Regression-based approaches output a single solution, which approximates an average over the true solution space, and therefore fail to capture the full distribution of plausible power spectra. To address this issue, we introduce a diffusion-based framework for analytic continuation that learns the distribution of spectra consistent with a given iTCF. It offers two key advantages. First, it quantifies uncertainty directly from the learned distribution. Second, by analyzing the spread and structure of this distribution, we can quantitatively assess the intrinsic hardness of each inversion problem. We measure this hardness with a new metric, the uncertainty pseudo-volume. Applying the framework to an iTCF from a path-integral molecular dynamics simulation of liquid parahydrogen, we obtain the self-diffusion coefficient with an error bar and flag a secondary high-frequency peak as a possible spurious artifact. In contrast to previous attempts at uncertainty quantification, our generative approach rests on a concrete probabilistic basis, providing a more theoretically grounded measure of confidence in the reconstructed power spectra.

</details>

Abstract: Quasi-hydrodynamics describes systems with quasi-conserved degrees of freedom, namely observables that relax on timescales that are finite but parametrically longer than microscopic relaxation times. Examples include kinetic chemistry and linear viscoelasticity. Here, we develop a rigorous effective-field-theory framework for linear quasi-hydrodynamics from kinetic-type theories. Starting from any linearized, causal kinetic-like theory endowed with slow degrees of freedom, we show that the exact dynamics of conserved and quasi-conserved observables admits a systematic expansion in the fast relaxation timescale. At zeroth order, the resulting equations form a causal, symmetric-hyperbolic theory belonging to the appropriate transient-hydrodynamic universality class, establishing Israel-Stewart-like dynamics as the universal description of slow relaxation modes. Higher-order corrections can be computed systematically and inherit universal symmetry, Onsager, positivity, and causality constraints from the underlying microscopic theory.

Abstract: Energy and charge transfer in condensed matter systems are often described in terms of quasiparticles. The order of accuracy of the results in terms of the electron-to-nucleus mass ratio $ε$, an adiabatic small parameter, is usually not known. To quantify energy transfer with controlled accuracy in $ε$, we use adiabatic perturbation theory to derive a formula for the rate of change of the nuclear kinetic energy. In the special case that the nuclei are treated classically, the energy transfer formula reduces to the usual formula for the rate of work done on a classical particle but the mass of the particle is enhanced by the factor $M_0^{-1}(M_0+εM_1)$, where $M_1$ is a nonadiabatic correction to the bare nuclear mass tensor $M_0$. In this work, we study these coupled dynamics for alkali metals (Li, Na, K, Rb, Cs), alkaline Earth metals (Mg, Ca, Sr, Ba), and chloride through the lens of their dependence on ion isotopic mass. Results are validated against previous measurements of the isotopic mass-dependence of ion diffusion coefficients in water and previous ab initio calculations of ion high-frequency dynamics in water. We find that the vibrational power spectra of ions in water consistently exhibit either two or three peaks, i.e., ions have several rattling frequencies within their solvations shells as previously reported for a subset of the species examined here. These frequencies have different sensititivies to isotopic mass that may serve as signatures of ion solvation processes (such as the tendency of ions to orient their first-shell water molecules) and that also may relate to Hofmeister-like effects including the relative affinity of different metals for ribonucleic acid (RNA).

### [49] [Spectral Localization Principle for Entanglement Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13449)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠提取（entanglement harvesting）是量子场论与量子信息交叉领域的核心问题，但此前缺乏统一的物理原理来解释从量子场中可提取的纠缠量。作者旨在提出并验证一个统一原则，使纠缠提取机制仅由场有效谱密度的局域化程度决定。

Method: 作者构建了一个解析可解的模型：两个量子比特（qubit）耦合到一漏腔单模腔（leaky single-mode cavity），该腔再耦合到连续电磁浴（electromagnetic bath）。通过推导最大可提取并发度的闭合表达式Cmax(Q)，并对高Q与低Q极限分别进行分析，同时将Q与逆参与比（inverse participation ratio, IPR）建立联系，实现了理论框架的量化。

Result: 导出了最大并发度的闭合表达式Cmax(Q)=2e^{-π/(2Q)}(1+e^{-π/(2Q)})/(1+3e^{-π/Q})。高Q极限下并发度渐近为1-π²/(16Q²)，表明纠缠对腔损耗具有鲁棒性；低Q极限下其指数衰减至零，与连续场的不可逆储层（irreversible reservoir）特性一致。最大并发度与IPR之间存在形式对应关系，类似于安德森局域化（Anderson localization）中的电导—参与比关系。

Conclusion: Q是控制从确定性门基纠缠（Q→∞）到真空纠缠提取（Q→0）交叉行为的唯一无量纲参数。该框架通过对局域探测器可获得的真空关联（vacuum correlations）比例进行量化，实现了Reeh-Schlieder定理的可操作化，且预测的Cmax(Q)曲线原则上可在超导电路QED（circuit QED）实验中直接观测。

Abstract: We propose a unified physical principle for entanglement harvesting: the entanglement that two localized detectors can extract from a quantum field is determined solely by how localized the field's effective spectral density is. We demonstrate this in an analytically solvable model of two qubits coupled to a leaky single-mode cavity, which in turn couples to a continuous electromagnetic bath, and derive the maximum harvestable concurrence in closed form, $\mathcal{C}_{\max}(Q)=2e^{-π/(2Q)}(1+e^{-π/(2Q)})/(1+3e^{-π/Q})$, where $Q\equiv|Δ|/κ$ is the ratio of the qubit-cavity detuning $Δ$ to the cavity linewidth $κ$. In the high-$Q$ limit, $\mathcal{C}_{\max}\simeq1-π^{2}/(16Q^{2})$, so the entanglement is robust against cavity loss; in the low-$Q$ limit it decays exponentially to zero, consistent with the irreversible-reservoir character of a continuous field, where maximal entanglement is unattainable. Since $Q$ is proportional to the inverse participation ratio (IPR) of the effective spectral density, it is the single dimensionless parameter governing the crossover from deterministic gate-based entanglement ($Q\to\infty$) to vacuum harvesting ($Q\to0$). Our framework operationalizes the Reeh-Schlieder theorem by quantifying the fraction of vacuum correlations accessible to localized detectors. It also reveals a formal correspondence of the maximal concurrence with the IPR, analogous to the conductivity-participation-ratio relation in Anderson localization. The predicted $\mathcal{C}_{\max}(Q)$ curve is, in principle, directly observable in superconducting circuit QED experiments.

</details>

In the XXX limit, these states exhibit exact plateaus in both entanglement and stabilizer Rényi entropy and correspond to coherent superpositions of long-range valence-bond solid (VBS) states. The pulse-engineered models generate nearly identical quantum resources in both regimes, revealing a partial decoupling between quantum chaos and quantum-resource generation. Abstract: Free-space quantum communication provides a flexible complement to fiber-based quantum networks, but its point-to-point capacity is fundamentally limited by diffraction, atmospheric extinction and beam wandering induced by turbulence. In this work, we study the end-to-end performance of large-scale free-space quantum networks connecting randomly distributed fixed or mobile users, modelled as Waxman random graphs. We derive the mean network capacity, edge consumption and connectivity phase transitions for both single-path and multi-path (flooding) routing. We also study router-centered star networks, deriving the full distribution of end-to-end capacities as a function of the router's coverage radius. We then consider how performance may be improved by reinforcing free-space networks with a small number of optimally placed fiber-based backbone nodes. We prove that any optimal backbone configuration must correspond to a capacity-maximizing Voronoi tessellation of the network region, and show that this can be efficiently approximated by a centroidal Voronoi tessellation via Lloyd's algorithm, with backbone nodes connected according to a Delaunay triangulation. Numerical results show that even a modest number of backbone nodes substantially improves end-to-end capacity and reduces edge consumption for both mobile and fixed users.

Abstract: A core characteristic of dissipative non-Hermitian topology is that the relaxation dynamics tracks the non-Bloch bulk-boundary correspondence, rendering an algebraic decay in the gapless regime and an exponential falloff in the gapped phase, so that local observables directly diagnose the topology.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介观尺度自组装为可编程微系统的设计提供了一条途径，然而如何通过局部相互作用规则来控制集体折叠行为以及序列-结构关系，仍是亟待探究的开放问题。作者希望构建一个可控的介观实验平台，以模拟类似于生物分子系统（biomolecular systems）中出现的集体折叠和复杂序列-结构关系。

Method: 作者构建了由浮动单体组成的柔性链，其中单体具有弯曲分支，可对液体界面（liquid interface）施加向上或向下的形变，对应有效的正或负毛细电荷（capillary charges）；这些几何编码的形变沿链产生局部的吸引或排斥相互作用。作者通过调节毛细序列（capillary sequence）获得不同折叠构型，数值映射折叠景观，并通过机械扰动实验考察亚稳态之间的转变。

Result: 短链的折叠主要由最近邻相互作用主导，产生明确的直线、之字形和环状等结构；随着链长增加，非相邻片段靠近并相互作用，折叠景观变得复杂，亚稳态数量随链长指数增长。数值映射显示该复杂性，实验证实机械扰动能使链在亚稳态构型之间转换。

Conclusion: 毛细序列不仅编码目标几何结构，还控制折叠景观的复杂性以及折叠结构的简并度（degeneracy）和突变鲁棒性（mutational robustness）。这些结果确立了毛细链作为一个可控的介观平台，用于研究局部相互作用规则如何产生集体折叠，其序列-结构关系与生物分子系统情景类似。

Abstract: Mesoscale self-assembly provides a route toward the design of programmable microsystems. Here, we construct flexible chains of floating monomers whose curved branches impose upward or downward deformations of the liquid interface, corresponding to effective positive or negative capillary charges. These geometrically encoded deformations generate local attractive or repulsive interactions along the chain. By tuning the capillary sequence, we obtain distinct folded configurations, including straight lines, zigzag patterns, and loops. For short chains, folding is largely governed by nearest-neighbor interactions and leads to well-defined structures. As the chain length increases and non-neighboring segments come into proximity and interact, however, the folding landscape becomes increasingly complex, with multiple metastable states whose number grows exponentially with chain length. We map these landscapes numerically and demonstrate experimentally that mechanical agitation allows the chains to transition between metastable configurations. Beyond encoding a target geometry, the capillary sequence therefore controls the complexity of the folding landscape as well as the degeneracy and mutational robustness of folded structures. These results establish capillary chains as a controllable mesoscale platform for investigating how local interaction rules give rise to collective folding and complex sequence-to-structure relationships reminiscent of those encountered in biomolecular systems.

</details>

Computing the graded $\operatorname{Ext}$ pairing explicitly, we deduce the self-adjointness of the genus two Macdonald operators and the orthogonality of genus $2$ Macdonald polynomials with respect to the certain skew-bilinear form.

Method: 作者此前已针对SL(2n)群建立了朗兰兹对偶两种情形之间的桥梁，本文将其推广到SL(2n+1)群的情形。由于两组群在表示理论（representation theory）上存在明显差异，需分别处理。

Conclusion: 通过将不变微分算子纳入朗兰兹纲领的框架，本文推进了朗兰兹对偶两种情形之间的桥梁建设，并验证了该联系在SL(2n+1)群上的适用性。

Abstract: Recently we started building a bridge between two cases of Langlands duality. The latter is one of the most influential topics in mathematical research. It has many different appearances and influential subtopics. Yet there is a topic that until now seems unrelated to the Langlands program. That is the topic of invariant differential operators. That is strange since both items are deeply rooted in Harish-Chandra's representation theory of semisimple Lie groups.
  We started with the case of the group ~$SL(2n)$. In the present paper we deal with the group $SL(2n+1)$. The two mentioned groups are similar, but their representation theories is rather different.

</details>

Abstract: We describe turbulent hydrodynamics of superfluids in two spatial dimensions via the dynamics of point-like vortices coupled to an emergent 2+1 dimensional $U(1)$ gauge field. The cascade of superfluid kinetic energy is equivalently described by a cascade of dual electric field energies. We study superfluid turbulence using the equations of motion of the dual gauge theory in the presence of a drive and dissipation. In the limit that the vortices are point-like, the dual equations of motion directly yield the hydrodynamical equations of the superfluid. We obtain a turbulent cascade consistent with Kolmogorov's scaling law for two dimensional fluid turbulence. We observe clustering of like-signed vortices and compute the kinetic energy flux to show that the turbulent regime exhibits an inverse energy cascade.

</details>

*Chao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过无符号问题的量子蒙特卡洛（sign-problem-free quantum Monte Carlo）方法，研究软核玻色-哈伯德模型中掺杂条纹固体的超固态行为，发现空穴侧形成锁定共度数条纹固体而超流刚度消失，粒子侧则形成具有有限压缩率和超流刚度的条纹超固体，且该超固体依赖于格点软度（onsite softness）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂晶格固体中，额外缺陷能否在保持有序背景不熔化的情况下形成相干态是一个开放问题。超固体（supersolid）同时兼具密度序与相位相干，研究掺杂对条纹固体超固体行为的影响有助于理解缺陷相干性与有序背景的关系。

Method: 采用各向同性跳跃的软核玻色-哈伯德模型，外加人为设计的非轴对称偶极相互作用 $V_{ij}=V_2(x_{ij}^2-y_{ij}^2)/r_{ij}^5+W_6/r_{ij}^6$，其中符号可变的 $d_{x^2-y^2}$ 分量选定固定 $(q,0)$ 条纹通道，$W_6/r^6$ 核心稳定短程吸引分支。使用无符号问题量子蒙特卡洛方法配合虫洞算法（worm algorithm）进行数值模拟。

Result: 半填充条纹母体对掺杂呈现不对称响应：空穴侧形成锁定共度（commensurate）条纹固体，超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）为零；粒子侧则形成条纹超固体，具有有限压缩率 $κ>0$、有限超流刚度 $ρ_s>0$ 和增强的双占据（double occupancy）$D$。当保持离壳核不变而增大 $U/t$ 趋近硬核极限时，粒子侧超固体因双占据类缺陷被投影而消失。

Conclusion: 工程化的偶极核选择了固定的 $(q,0)$ 条纹通道，而格点软度决定了相位相干的缺陷扇区。这说明超固体的形成依赖于格点软度提供的双占据通道，硬核极限下粒子侧超固体消失。

Abstract: A supersolid combines density order with phase coherence, and doped lattice solids ask whether added defects can become coherent without melting the ordered background. We study a soft-core Bose-Hubbard model with isotropic hopping and an engineered non-axisymmetric dipolar interaction, \(V_{ij}=V_2(x_{ij}^2-y_{ij}^2)/r_{ij}^5+W_6/r_{ij}^6\), where the sign-changing \(d_{x^2-y^2}\) component selects a fixed \((q,0)\) stripe channel and the \(W_6/r^6\) core stabilizes the short-distance attractive branch. Using sign-problem-free quantum Monte Carlo method with worm algorithm, we find that the half-filled stripe parent responds asymmetrically to doping: the hole side forms locked commensurate stripe solids with vanishing superfluid stiffness, whereas the particle side forms a stripe supersolid with finite compressibility \(κ>0\), finite superfluid stiffness \(ρ_s>0\), and enhanced double occupancy \(D\). Keeping the same off-site kernel while increasing \(U/t\) toward the hard-core limit shows that the particle-side supersolid disappears once doublon-like defects are projected out. Thus the engineered dipolar kernel selects the fixed \((q,0)\) stripe channel, while onsite softness selects the phase-coherent defect sector.

</details>

### [62] [Neural Quadratic Forms: A Unified Minimal Model for Sudden Learning and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13335)
*Liu Ziyin,Yizhou Xu,Tomaso Poggio,Isaac Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，即使代价函数是光滑的，梯度下降训练的神经网络仍会出现阶段性学习：代价函数长时间保持平台后突然下降。通过对称性论证，作者证明网络的层是可通过可交换单元的和，据此推出关于训练初期零权重附近的普适展开形式，其所有架构细节被归结为单个"结构矩阵" $A(x)$。训练动力学在序参量 $M=WW^{\top}$ 上闭合，并在数据矩阵共享本征基时简化为 Lotka–Volterra 方程，从而统一解释了平台和幂律两种训练损失行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者观察到神经网络在光滑代价函数下的训练中会出现不连续的阶段性学习行为，同时训练损失又表现为光滑幂律，且不同微观结构的架构中都存在这两种行为的变体，暗示存在少数相关的集体变量（collective variables）。本文动机是通过对称性原理确定这些集体变量的本质，并建立统一框架以解释多样架构中的共同训练动力学。

Method: 作者从网络的层是"可交换单元之和"这一对称性出发，结合光滑性条件以及单元在原点处梯度为零的假设，推导出训练初始近零权重附近展开的普适前导形式，即二次型 $\mathrm{Tr}[WW^{\top}A(x)]$，其中架构细节全部封装在"结构矩阵" $A(x)$ 中。对感知机（perceptron）、注意力层（attention layer）、专家混合（mixture of experts）和卷积（convolution）分别计算 $A$，论证其统一为一个模型。训练动力学在序参量 $M=WW^{\top}$ 上闭合，在数据矩阵共享本征基时约化为 Lotka–Volterra 方程。

Result: 理论预测被数值实验证实：不同训练方法和架构下均观察到两种行为的出现。当初始权重越小，模式开启时间的间隔越大，平台表现为光滑流的奇异极限；当大量模式无法分辨时，相同事件在训练时间上合并为幂律行为，其指数由理论给出。

Conclusion: 对称性原理将看似异质的神经架构统一到同一套训练动力学框架中，通过单个序参量和 Lotka–Volterra 方程同时解释了平台式不连续下降与幂律式光滑下降两种训练损失行为，两者的区别取决于初始权重大小和模式可分辨性。

Abstract: Neural networks trained by gradient descent on a smooth cost function can nevertheless learn in steps: the cost holds on long plateaus and then drops abruptly. Meanwhile, training losses instead follow smooth power laws. Variants of both behaviors occur in architectures with very different microscopic structures, which is the signature of a few relevant collective variables. We show that a symmetry fixes what those variables are: a network layer is a sum over interchangeable units, so relabeling the units leaves it unchanged; given smoothness and the condition that a unit's gradient vanish at the origin, symmetry then enforces a universal leading form for the expansion about the near-zero weights present at the start of training, the quadratic $\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]$, in which every architectural detail is confined to a single ``structure matrix" $A(x)$ that we compute for each architecture. Perceptrons, attention layers, mixtures of experts, and convolutions become one model at different $A$. Its training dynamics then close on the ``order parameter" $M=WW^{\top}$ and, whenever the data matrices share an eigenbasis, reduce to a Lotka--Volterra equation whose modes switch on one after another. The smaller the initial weights, the further apart the switch-on times, and the plateaus appear as a singular limit of a smooth flow; when many modes are unresolved the same events merge into a power law in training time whose exponent the theory predicts. We confirm both numerically across training methods and architectures.

</details>

### [63] [Graph-theoretic design of lasing networks for physical vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13097)
*Paul Obernolte,Jakub Dranczewski,Yixiu Yin,Tobias Farchy,Wai Kit Ng,Tobias Simonsen,Elias Großhauser,Alexis Arnaudon,Robert L. Peach,Mauricio Barahona,Riccardo Sapienza,Jack C. Gartside,T. V. Raziman*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在抽象图空间中高效搜索物理神经网络拓扑的方法。以随机激光网络为范例，建立了图论指标→非线性激光物理→视觉性能的三层定量关联，并利用图指标驱动进化算法，实现了比物理模拟快3000倍的设计优化，同时显著提升图像分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理神经网络通过物质的内在非线性动力学进行学习，但其设计优化面临巨大挑战：复杂的多体物理计算昂贵，而大规模实验优化难以实现制造。因此，高维网络拓扑空间无法被有效直接搜索，亟需一种更高效的优化途径。

Method: 作者以随机激光网络（由相互连接的纳米级波导组成，承载强耦合激光模式）为物理视觉系统范例，建立了一个定量的三层关联：简单的图论指标（graph-theoretic metrics）预测非线性激光物理，后者进而预测视觉性能。在通过物理模拟验证该关联后，利用图指标驱动进化算法（evolutionary algorithm）来生成网络拓扑。

Result: 图优化生成的网络拓扑在计算成本上相比物理模拟实现了3000倍的加速，并且在模拟图像分类任务中显著提高了分类精度，优于随机设计的网络。

Conclusion: 由于该框架作用于网络拓扑而非基底特有的物理机制，方法有望迁移至其他基于网络的物理学习系统，为复杂强相互作用物理神经网络的方向性设计与优化提供高效路径。

Abstract: Physical neural networks perform learning through the intrinsic nonlinear dynamics of matter. Optimising their design presents a considerable challenge: complex many-body physics can provide powerful computation, but are expensive to simulate and large experimental optimisation runs are impractical to fabricate. Hence, the high-dimensional space of possible network topologies cannot be effectively directly searched. Here, we show that this search can be efficiently performed in an abstract graph space that is vastly cheaper to explore. Using random lasing networks -- composed of interconnected nanoscale waveguides and hosting strongly coupled lasing modes -- as an exemplar physical vision system, we establish a quantitative three-layer link: simple graph-theoretic metrics predict the nonlinear lasing physics, which in turn predicts vision performance. After validating this relationship using physical simulations, we exploit it to drive an evolutionary algorithm using graph metrics, producing network topologies that outperform random designs at a fraction of the computational cost (3000$\times$ speed-up compared to physical simulation). On simulated image-classification tasks, graph-optimised networks substantially improve classification accuracy. As our framework operates on network topology rather than substrate-specific physics, we anticipate it can transfer to other network-based physical learning systems, providing an efficient route for the directed design and optimisation of complex, strongly-interacting physical neural networks.

</details>

### [64] [The "Moiré Capacitor Effect" and Stabilization of Fractional Chern Insulators in Rhombohedral Graphene Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12452)
*Nicolas Regnault,Heqiu Li,Yves H. Kwan,B. Andrei Bernevig,Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“莫尔电容器效应”（moire capacitor effect）的新机制，通过将价带电荷密度静电印迹到导带上以增强莫尔势（moire potential），从而稳定菱面体石墨烯-六方氮化硼（rhombohedral graphene-hBN）超晶格中的分数量子霍尔绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）。基于多带精确对角化计算，作者在填充数ν=2/3处确认了FCIs的出现，并首次为该体系在准直样品中的稳定性提供了自洽理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管莫尔势对菱面体石墨烯-hBN超晶格中FCIs的必要性已在实验中得到证实，但其起源和重要性的理论解释仍然缺失。

Method: 作者推导了莫尔电容器效应的解析形式，提出了一个母态理论（parent state theory），并利用多带精确对角化（multi-band exact diagonalization）计算在ν=2/3填充下验证FCIs的出现。

Result: 在莫尔电容器效应存在时，于ν=2/3处确认了FCIs的出现；该FCI态通过带间涨落（inter-band fluctuations）稳定，而在无莫尔势情形下带间混合会导致态坍缩。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为准直样品中该态的稳定性提供了一致的理论解释，并解释了为什么在非准直样品中该态缺失。

Abstract: While the necessity of a moiré potential for fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) in rhombohedral graphene-hBN superlattices, first predicted in Ref. 1, is now grounded in experiments, a theory of its origin and importance remains at large. We present a mechanism---the moiré capacitor effect---that enhances the moiré potential by electrostatically imprinting the valence charge density onto the conduction bands. Built on general principles of symmetry and locality, the SBP wave function provides a broadly applicable variational representation for challenging interacting fermionic systems.

### [71] [Correlation versus Causation in Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12770)

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发现，在共形场论（CFT）中，静态响应率（static susceptibility，即"causation"）的衰减可比关联函数（correlation function）快多达十五个$x$幂次，这一机制源于时间导数场无法贡献于静态响应。该机制在更高维CFT中确保前导因果响应来自初级算符（primary），并可用于识别DMRG中此前难以分辨的算符，同时它还解释了$(1+1)$维无隙对称性保护拓扑相中的边缘模局域化现象。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联函数的空间衰减可揭示标度维度（scaling dimension），但作者发现另一种对象——静态响应率（即causation）——具有更丰富的衰减行为，且此行为此前未被系统理解，值得深入研究。

Method: 作者通过维数分析（dimensional analysis）对比CFT中关联函数与causation的预期衰减指数，并揭示一个一般机制：时间导数场（time-derivative field）无法对静态响应产生贡献，从而导致causation衰减更快。他们将该机制应用于更高维CFT，并借助密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）进行数值验证。

Result: 在$(2+1)$维临界Ising模型中，利用该机制识别出此前未分辨的角落初级算符（corner primary，$\Delta\approx 8.8$）和重磁线缺陷初级算符（heavy magnetic line defect primary，$\Delta\approx 4.6$）。此外，在$(1+1)$维无隙SPT相中，该机制解释了异常小的边缘模劈裂（edge-mode splitting），并据此构造了劈裂小至$1/L^{18}$和$1/L^{25}$的自旋链。

Conclusion: 因果响应（causation）提供了一种比关联函数更强大的标度分析工具，其机制不仅统一了静态响应衰减与边缘模局域化两个看似不同的物理现象，还为数值方法（如DMRG）识别高维算符提供了新途径。

Abstract: Correlation functions $\langle O_1(x) O_2(0) \rangle$ reveal scaling dimensions through spatial decay. We instead consider static susceptibility, the change in $\langle O_1(x) \rangle$ from perturbing the Hamiltonian by $O_2(0)$, which we term causation for short. In a conformal field theory (CFT), dimensional analysis predicts decay of $|x|^{-2Δ}$ for correlation and $|x|^{-2Δ+1}$ for causation. Yet we find causation can decay up to fifteen additional orders in $x$ through a general mechanism, which we trace to time-derivative fields being unable to contribute to static response. In higher-dimensional CFTs, this mechanism ensures leading causation arises from primaries, even when descendants dominate correlation, which we leverage with DMRG to identify a previously unresolved corner primary of $Δ\approx 8.8$ and a heavy magnetic line defect primary of $Δ\approx 4.6$ in the $(2+1)$D critical Ising model. Moreover, the same mechanism governs edge-mode localization in $(1+1)$D gapless symmetry-protected topological phases, explaining previously observed anomalously small edge-mode splittings and guiding our construction of spin chains with splittings as small as $1/L^{18}$ and $1/L^{25}$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 通过量子与经典自旋极限之间的连续插值，可考察相应相图是否同样连续连接；不同屏蔽场景（欠屏蔽与过屏蔽）展现出迥异的拓扑特征与对称性行为，其中过屏蔽情形下的自发对称性破缺导致富有物理内涵的量子-经典相图。

Abstract: The gapped Kondo effect describes the screening of an $S=\frac12$ impurity spin locally coupled via an antiferromagnetic exchange interaction to a conduction-electron system exhibiting a finite hard gap. Using a combination of a Lanczos transformation and a self-consistent configuration-interaction scheme, we numerically investigate the local phase diagram. Furthermore, we show that the different phases can be characterized by several topological invariants: the conventional momentum-space Chern number of the underlying two-dimensional host system, corresponding to a Chern insulator; the B-space Chern number, defined by coupling the impurity spin to a fictitious local magnetic field $\boldsymbol B$, in the limit $B \to 0$; and the S-space Chern number, defined for a classical impurity spin, i.e., a vector of fixed length. The classical-spin limit is obtained for $B \to \infty$. By varying the field strength, we can therefore continuously interpolate between quantum-impurity-spin and classical-impurity-spin Hamiltonians and investigate whether the corresponding phase diagrams are likewise continuously connected. The gapped underscreened Kondo effect is studied for an impurity spin $S>\frac12$ as well as in the classical-spin limit approached via $B \to \infty$ or $S \to \infty$. Different variants of scattering theory are employed to interpret the resulting phases. Finally, the gapped two-channel overscreened Kondo effect, realized by coupling a quantum impurity spin equally to the local electron spins of both orbitals within a unit cell, is shown to be characterized by spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking. This leads to a highly nontrivial quantum-classical phase diagram.

### [73] [Magnetism in antiperovskite (Li$_2$\textit{M})\textit{Ch}O (\textit{M} = Fe, Mn, Co; \textit{Ch} = S, Se) diluted magnets with fixed 1/3 filling: the key role of magnetic anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13111)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究在固定1/3填充率（接近渗流阈值）下，磁性亚晶格中自旋大小、磁各向异性与轨道构型等因素如何影响磁有序的演化。该体系提供了一个独特平台来研究强稀释磁性晶格中的磁交换作用。

Method: 通过制备一系列富锂反钙钛矿（antiperovskite）材料（Li₂M）ChO（M = Fe, Co, Mn；Ch = Se, S），利用过渡金属与锂离子在X位点的随机分布构建强稀释磁性亚晶格，并通过磁化率测量及电子自旋共振（ESR）等手段表征磁性质。

Conclusion: 结论表明，在磁性稀释的1/3填充X位点晶格中，长程反铁磁有序依赖于过渡金属的本征性质（尤其是磁各向异性），而非结构细节。显著高于T_N的短程磁关联意味着体系中存在较强的磁耦合，且实际有序温度被磁稀释效应强烈抑制。

Abstract: We report the magnetic properties of a series of lithium-rich antiperovskites (Li$_2M$)$Ch$O ($M$ = Fe, Co, Mn and $Ch$ = Se, S) where transition metal and lithium ions are randomly distributed on the X-sites of the X$_3$BA structure, thereby forming a strongly diluted magnetic sublattice. Our study hence enables us to investigate the evolution of magnetic order at fixed 1/3-filling -- which is in the vicinity but slightly above the percolation threshold -- upon variation of the spin size, the magnetic anisotropy, and the orbital configuration. The data imply the absence of a distinct Curie-Weiss behavior up to 350~K but show rather large and weakly temperature-dependent magnetic susceptibility. We observe clear signatures of long-range antiferromagnetic order evolving in the 1/3-filled and strongly diluted magnetic X-site lattice with increasing Néel temperatures from $T_{\rm{N}}\simeq 30$~K in (Li$_2$Mn)$Ch$O to $\simeq 50$~K in (Li$_2$Fe)$Ch$O and $70-90$~K in (Li$_2$Co)$Ch$O. Except for $M$ = Co, the chalcogenide has no sizable effect on $T_{\rm N}$. We conclude significant magnetic coupling and short-range magnetic correlations at well above $T_{\rm N}$ which is in line with the observation of a broad electron spin resonance signal at room temperature. The actual ordering temperatures are strongly diminished by magnetic dilution. While structural parameters such as the tolerance factor and bonding angles do not strongly affect $T_{\rm N}$, a key parameter is the magnetic anisotropy of the transition metals.

</details>


### [74] [Accelerating a Strong-Coupling Non-Equilibrium Steady-State Impurity Solver using (Quantics) Tensor Trains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13146)
*Bastian Schindler,Martin Eckstein*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并比较了四种不同的被积函数参数化方案，用于通过张量交叉插值（tensor cross interpolation）构建低秩（quantics）张量训练（tensor-train）表示，从而高效计算强耦合展开中的高阶图解修正。文中在平衡与非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT）计算中验证了所提方法，并扩展到含延迟密度-密度相互作用的杂质模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合展开中纳入高阶图解修正的主要瓶颈在于高维、时间有序积分（time-ordered integrals）的求值，因其计算成本极高。解决这一高维积分求值难题对于推进非平衡量子杂质计算至关重要。

Method: 提出了四种被积函数参数化方案，利用张量交叉插值获得低秩（quantics）张量训练表示；特别强调了一种quantics时间差（time-difference）公式化，其中所需的延迟卷积（retarded convolutions）直接在quantics张量训练形式中完成。

Result: 通过受控高斯基准测试分析了各方法的精度、键维数（bond dimensions）和计算标度；在自洽平衡与非平衡DMFT计算中验证了最有前景的方案，并演示了强耦合展开至第三阶的计算；将其扩展到含延迟密度-密度相互作用的杂质模型，并在非平衡扩展DMFT（extended DMFT）中应用。

Conclusion: 结果表明张量交叉插值大幅降低了高阶图解求值的成本，为非平衡量子杂质计算提供了一个受控且可系统性改进的计算框架。

Abstract: Including higher order diagrammatic corrections to the strong-coupling expansion is mainly limited by the evaluation of high-dimensional, time-ordered integrals. In this work we present and compare four different parametrizations of the integrands in order to obtain a low-rank (quantics) tensor-train representation using tensor cross interpolation. Particular emphasis is placed on a quantics time-difference formulation in which the required retarded convolutions are performed directly in quantics tensor-train form. Using controlled Gaussian benchmarks, we analyze the accuracy, bond dimensions, and computational scaling of the different approaches. We then validate the most promising formulations in self-consistent equilibrium and nonequilibrium DMFT calculations and demonstrate calculations up to the third order in the strong-coupling expansion. Finally, we extend the solver to impurity models with retarded density-density interactions and apply it within nonequilibrium extended DMFT. Our results show that tensor cross interpolation substantially reduces the cost of evaluating higher-order diagrams and provides a controlled, systematically improvable framework for nonequilibrium quantum impurity calculations.

</details>


### [75] [Emergent Symmetry-Protected Topological Phases via Polyakov Confinement in Quantum Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13238)
*Li-Wei He,Shun-Li Yu,Jian-Xin Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大尺度变分蒙特卡洛模拟证实，Polyakov禁闭机制可作为动力学引擎，将Dirac自旋液体转变为对称性保护的拓扑（SPT）态，实现了自旋子的量子自旋霍尔效应类比，并揭示了测量自旋泵浦响应可直接编码母Dirac锥的Berry曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代凝聚态物理的核心问题之一在于理解量子自旋系统中涌现（emergent）的分裂化激发（fractionalized excitations）与规范结构（gauge structures），但如何理解这些奇异自由度重新禁闭（reconfine）为新的物质相仍是根本性挑战。

Method: 作者从具有自旋依赖规范通量（spin-dependent gauge fluxes）的Dirac自旋液体出发，论证物理希尔伯特空间固有的单占据约束（single-occupancy constraint）会触发磁单极子凝聚（monopole condensation），从而动态禁闭体态自旋子但保留无能隙边缘模式。在含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction）的三角晶格反铁磁体上采用大尺度变分蒙特卡洛模拟（variational Monte Carlo simulation）提供微观证据。

Result: 模拟结果证实了受限SPT相的存在，表现为Wilson环（Wilson loop）的特征面积律（area law）以及消失的拓扑纠缠熵（topological entanglement entropy）。此外，识别出在磁场下可测量的自旋泵浦（spin-pump）响应，该响应直接编码母Dirac锥（parent Dirac cones）的Berry曲率。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一条此前未被探索的通向SPT物理的路径，将规范理论（gauge theory）、量子磁性（quantum magnetism）与拓扑物质（topological matter）三个领域联系起来，并提供了可实验验证的自旋泵浦响应作为该相的直接探测手段。

Abstract: A main theme in modern condensed matter physics is the emergence of fractionalized excitations and gauge structures from quantum spin systems. However, understanding how these exotic degrees of freedom reconfine into new phases of matter remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we demonstrate that Polyakov's confinement mechanism-a foundation of compact gauge theory-can serve as a dynamical engine to transform a Dirac spin liquid into a symmetry-protected topological (SPT) state. Starting with a Dirac spin liquid with spin-dependent gauge fluxes, we show that the single-occupancy constraint inherent to the physical Hilbert space triggers monopole condensation, dynamically confining bulk spinons while preserving gapless edge modes-realizing a spinon analog of the quantum spin Hall effect. Using a large-scale variational Monte Carlo simulation on a triangular antiferromagnet with an additional Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, we provide microscopic evidence for this confined SPT phase, including a characteristic area law for the Wilson loop and vanishing topological entanglement entropy. Furthermore, we identify a measurable spin-pump response under magnetic fields, which directly encodes the Berry curvature of the parent Dirac cones. Our results reveal a previously unexplored pathway to SPT physics, bridging the fields of gauge theory, quantum magnetism, and topological matter.

</details>


### [76] [Imaginary-time correlations in time-sliced stochastic series expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13477)
*Ryan Flynn,Adam Iaizzi,Sibin Yang,Ying-Jer Kao,Anders W. Sandvik*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在随机级数展开（SSE）框架内提出了一种高效且通用的算法，用于计算对角和非对角算符的虚时关联函数（imaginary-time correlation functions），该方法基于对SSE算符串的离散虚时切片，在无离散化误差的网格点上提供关联函数，并通过一维横场Ising模型和XXZ自旋链的基准测试验证了其正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法结合数值解析延拓（analytic continuation）技术可以从虚时关联函数中提取实频动力学性质，然而对于在计算基中非对角的算符，QMC高效计算虚时关联函数可能具有挑战性。因此需要一种能够同时高效处理对角和非对角算符的通用算法。

Method: 算法在随机级数展开框架内实现，通过对SSE算符串进行离散虚时切片（discrete imaginary-time slicing），在明确定义的虚时时间点网格上提供关联函数，且不引入离散化误差。对于非对角算符，推导了可直接集成到现有SSE有向环（directed-loop）或团簇（cluster）更新方案中的估计量，仅带来极小的计算开销。

Result: 在二维之外的一维横场Ising模型（使用团簇更新采样）和XXZ自旋链（使用有向环采样）上进行基准测试，结果表明与小系统精确对角化（exact diagonalization）结果仅有统计误差范围内的高度一致；此外，对更大系统的研究验证了该方法的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法为在SSE框架下高效计算对角和非对角算符的虚时关联函数提供了一种通用解决方案，兼具无离散化误差和低计算开销的优点，可广泛应用于量子多体系统的动力学性质研究。

Abstract: Combined with numerical analytic continuation techniques, quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) methods enable the extraction of real-frequency dynamical properties from imaginary-time correlation functions. However, the efficient computation of imaginary-time correlation functions by QMC simulations can (depending on the particular model used) be challenging, particularly for operators that are off-diagonal in the computational basis. In this work, we present an efficient and general algorithm within the stochastic series expansion (SSE) framework for evaluating imaginary-time correlation functions of both diagonal and off-diagonal operators. The algorithm builds on a discrete imaginary-time slicing of the SSE operator string, which provides correlation functions on a grid of well-defined imaginary-time points with no discretization error. For off-diagonal operators, we derive estimators that integrate directly into the existing SSE directed-loop or cluster updating schemes, introducing only minimal computational overhead. We benchmark the method on the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model (sampling with cluster updates) and XXZ spin chain (using directed-loop sampling), demonstrating excellent agreement (with only statistical errors) with exact diagonalization of small systems. We also study larger systems to demonstrate efficiency.

</details>
