### [77] [Origin of effective non-Fourier heat conduction phenomena in heterogeneous materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13336)
*Róbert Kovács*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过空间体积平均（spatial volume averaging）方法，从双组分系统的微观结构出发推导出超越傅里叶定律（Fourier's law）的连续介质热方程。研究证明了异质介质（heterogeneous media）天然具有过扩散（over-diffusive）特性，推导出的热方程具备热力学相容性，并在金属泡沫、碳泡沫、岩石和金属有机框架等实验数据上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非傅里叶热传导（non-Fourier heat conduction）的现象学模型往往缺乏严格的微观结构基础，导致在模拟复杂异质材料时出现歧义，因此需要建立具有微观结构支撑的连续介质热传导方程。

Method: 采用空间体积平均方法对双组分系统进行推导，从微观材料特性和结构出发解析地推导出超越傅里叶定律的连续热方程，并证明静态与动态热扩散率（thermal diffusivity）直接来源于不同材料属性。同时分析了有限样本边界引入的高阶空间非局域性（non-locality）。

Result: 解析证明了异质介质天然具有过扩散特性，得到的非傅里叶热方程具有热力学相容性，微观结构来源使得非傅里叶输运系数（transport coefficients）可被计算；有限样本边界会产生高阶空间非局域性，从而解释了过扩散的尺寸依赖性。模型在金属泡沫、碳泡沫、岩石和金属有机框架等实验数据上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为异质材料的非傅里叶热传导提供了一个具有明确微观结构基础的连续介质框架，能解释过扩散现象的尺寸依赖效应，并可用于非傅里叶输运系数的计算。

Abstract: Phenomenological models of non-Fourier heat conduction often lack a strict microstructural foundation, leading to ambiguities when modeling complex heterogeneous materials. In this study, we derive a continuum heat equation beyond Fourier's law using spatial volume averaging for a two-component system. We analytically prove that the experimentally observed static and dynamic thermal diffusivity arise directly from the distinct material properties, concluding that heterogeneous media are inherently over-diffusive. The resulting heat equation is thermodynamically compatible, and the microstructural origin allows the calculation of non-Fourier transport coefficients. Furthermore, we demonstrate that finite-sample boundaries introduce higher-order spatial non-localities, thereby explaining the size dependence of over-diffusion. We validate the model against experimental data across metal and carbon foams, rocks, and metal-organic frameworks.

</details>

### [78] [Mechanisms of Nanoscroll Formation and Particle Encapsulation in Janus MXenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12439)
*Sasan Rezaee,Fatemeh Mohammad Dezashibi,Ould el Moctar,Hossein Darban*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模反应分子动力学模拟，研究了二维Janus MXene材料卷曲形成纳米卷（nanoscroll）的原子级机制，揭示了晶格诱导应变产生的弯曲力矩是卷曲的主要驱动力，并发现纳米卷在包裹纳米颗粒时可形成局部可调层间距的核壳复合材料，但伴随的H2释放会形成纳米气泡，可能缩短电池寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Janus MXene向纳米卷的形貌转变可解锁非常规性能，尽管实验上已验证可规模化合成的路线，但纳米卷形成的原子级机制仍缺乏理解，亟需从理论层面阐明其稳定性、驱动力与几何控制因素。

Method: 采用大规模反应分子动力学（reactive molecular dynamics, RMD）模拟，并以密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）及实验的结构与弹性性质进行验证；对具有不同表面端基（bare -b、-O、-OH）的三类Janus MXene (Tx)Ti2C(Ty)进行模拟，包括方形与无限宽薄片，长度从10 nm到超过120 nm。

Abstract: Erbium ion is among the most promising solid-state single photon emitters and spin-photon interfaces for quantum networks, emitting directly in the telecom C-band in many host semiconductors.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Electronic excitation induced by nuclear motion is a key energy dissipation channel in chemical dynamics at metal surfaces. A theoretical formalism is presented to evaluate tensorial and configuration-dependent electronic friction memory kernels from first principles. </details>

Conclusion: 这些发现为在软半导体（soft semiconductors）中同时优化电荷和离子传输提供了一种推广的微观机制，表明电子-声子耦合与离子迁移可由同一电子结构框架统一理解。

TL;DR: 本文通过嵌套采样（nested sampling）模拟结合机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）和GPU加速，在从头算（first-principles）预测精度水平上获得了金属表面演化与化学环境/温度之间的耦合关系。该方法成功预测了Cu(100)表面早期氧化中实验观察到的复杂(2√2×√2)R45°-O缺行重构（missing-row reconstruction），并实现了对温度依赖的表面演化及有序-无序转变的详细表征。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属表面会因化学环境而发生结构、组分和形态变化，为了调控表面在特定应用中的功能与稳定性，需要理解表面演化如何与外部条件耦合。传统方法难以在全局配置空间（configuration space）中进行无先验知识（prior knowledge）的采样，从而无法直接获得任意温度下的系综平均（ensemble average）。

Conclusion: 嵌套采样模拟能够在从头算预测精度下获得金属表面演化与外界条件之间的耦合关系，为在任意温度下无先验地获取热力学性质提供了有效途径，从而成为表面科学与材料设计中的有力工具。

Method: 采用路径积分分子动力学模拟，涵盖经典核极限，基于经过密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算验证的高效紧束缚（tight-binding）哈密顿量；在广泛的温度与静水压范围（涵盖拉伸与压缩状态）内系统地计算弹性常数与体模量，并与无缺陷晶体进行比较。

Result: 硅空位显著重整化了弹性常数C11、C12、C44及体模量；核量子运动——源于零点运动（zero-point motion）——在低温下产生额外的弹性软化效应；缺陷还将3C-SiC的机械稳定域缩小，在缺陷浓度为0.016/格点时使最大可承受拉伸压力降低约4 GPa。

Conclusion: 研究表明点缺陷与量子晶格涨落之间存在相互影响，二者共同决定SiC的弹性行为。这一发现为极端环境结构应用与缺陷基量子技术提供了微观层面的理解。

Abstract: We investigate how silicon vacancies modify the elastic response and mechanical stability of cubic 3C-SiC. Our approach employs path-integral molecular dynamics simulations, including the classical-nuclei limit, based on an efficient tight-binding Hamiltonian, whose accuracy is validated against density-functional-theory calculations. This framework enables a quantitative assessment of nuclear quantum effects arising from zero-point motion. Across a broad range of temperatures and hydrostatic pressures, spanning both tensile and compressive regimes, silicon vacancies are found to substantially renormalize the elastic constants $C_{11}$, $C_{12}$, and $C_{44}$, as well as the bulk modulus, relative to the defect-free crystal. Inclusion of nuclear quantum motion produces an additional softening of these elastic properties, particularly at low temperatures, demonstrating that quantum fluctuations make a measurable contribution to the mechanical response of defective SiC. Vacancies also affect the mechanical stability domain of 3C-SiC, lowering the maximum sustainable tensile pressure by approximately 4 GPa for a defect concentration of 0.016 per lattice site. These results reveal an interplay between point defects and quantum lattice fluctuations in determining the elastic behavior of SiC, providing microscopic insight relevant for both extreme-environment structural applications and defect-based quantum technologies.

</details>


### [86] [Dissipationless Photovoltaic Spin Hall Effect from Spin-current Vorticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12968)
*Longjun Xiang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出自旋电流涡度（spin-current vorticity, SCV）在直流电场下可使光激发电子偏转，从而产生无耗散光伏自旋霍尔效应（photovoltaic spin Hall effect, PSHE）。该效应分别由贝里曲率（Berry curvature）和量子度量（quantum metric）调控圆偏振与线偏振光激发，依据时间反演对称性的奇偶性产生两类可切换的PSHE，并在单层WTe₂和d波交错磁（altermagnetic）模型中分别得到验证，由此确立SCV为理解无耗散非线性自旋霍尔输运的统一概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电流涡度（SCV）已被证明能够产生线性磁自旋霍尔效应，但其在非线性自旋霍尔输运中的作用此前未得到充分探索。

Method: 作者理论分析直流电场下SCV对光激发电子的偏转效应，结合贝里曲率（时间反演奇）与量子度量（时间反演偶）分别表征圆偏振和线偏振光激发，并考察它们与SCV（时间反演奇）组合后的时间反演对称性，同时采用单层WTe₂和d波交错磁模型进行示例验证。

Result: 发现两类PSHE：时间反演偶（T-even）PSHE可通过切换光手性反转自旋电流方向（在单层WTe₂中验证），时间反演奇（T-odd）PSHE可在Néel矢量反转时改变符号（在d波交错磁模型中演示）。此外，SCV偶极子同时支配Drude型和内禀型非线性自旋霍尔效应。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了两种可切换的自旋霍尔机制，并将SCV确立为统一理解无耗散非线性自旋霍尔输运的核心概念。

Abstract: Spin-current vorticity (SCV) can generate the linear magnetic spin Hall effect [\href{https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-018-0853-0}{Nature \textbf{565}, 627 (2019)}], yet its role in nonlinear spin Hall transport has been much less explored. Here, we show that, under a dc electric field, SCV can deflect optically excited electrons to drive a dissipationless photovoltaic spin Hall effect (PSHE), in which optical excitation by circularly and linearly polarized light is governed by the Berry curvature and quantum metric, respectively. Because the Berry curvature is $\mathcal{T}$-odd whereas the quantum metric is $\mathcal{T}$-even, their respective combinations with the $\mathcal{T}$-odd SCV give rise to $\mathcal{T}$-even and $\mathcal{T}$-odd PSHEs, where $\mathcal{T}$ denotes time-reversal symmetry. Remarkably, we find that the spin current of the $\mathcal{T}$-even PSHE can be reversed by switching the light helicity, as illustrated in monolayer WTe$_2$. By contrast, the $\mathcal{T}$-odd PSHE in altermagnets changes sign upon Néel-vector reversal, as demonstrated in a $d$-wave altermagnetic model. Beyond the PSHE, we show that the SCV dipole governs both the Drude and intrinsic nonlinear spin Hall effects proposed recently. Our results reveal two switchable spin Hall mechanisms and establish SCV as a unifying concept for understanding dissipationless nonlinear spin Hall transport.

</details>


### [87] [From Molecular Design to Optical Anisotropy: Orientation Control in BODIPY Langmuir-Blodgett Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12978)
*Lilia Huynh,Jason Bessonnet,Lucas Fr{é}d{é}ric,C{é}line Fiorini-Debuisschert,Simon Vassant,H{é}l{è}ne Fensterbank,Emmanuel Allard,David Kreher,Fabrice Charra,Nicolas Fabre*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超薄膜内分子取向是控制表面光子学和光电子学领域光学与电子性质的关键因素，理解并调控分子的空间与取向分布对功能性薄膜设计至关重要。

Method: 采用Langmuir–Blodgett沉积法构建两种BODIPY衍生物的超薄膜；通过超光谱成像（hyperspectral imaging）、光致发光辐射模式分析（photoluminescence radiation pattern analysis）和入射角分辨吸收光谱（incidence-angle-resolved absorption spectroscopy）联合测定纳米尺度的分子空间及取向分布，并提出一个新的理论模型进行验证。

Conclusion: 分子工程（molecular engineering）深刻影响界面处超分子有序性和各向异性光学性质，为设计单分子层级别功能性薄膜和先进光学器件提供了稳健策略。

Abstract: Molecular orientation within ultrathin films is a critical factor in controlling their optical and electronic properties for surface-based photonics and optoelectronics. In this study, we examine two amphiphilic boron-dipyrromethene (BODIPY) derivatives, distinguished by the number of hydrophobic alkyl chains, and investigate their organization and optical response using Langmuir--Blodgett deposition. The spatial and orientational distribution of molecules at the nanoscale is determined by combining hyperspectral imaging, photoluminescence radiation pattern analysis, and incidence-angle-resolved absorption spectroscopy. We evidence, both experimentally and based on a new and original theoretical model, the formation of organized monolayers exhibiting either in-plane or perpendicular transition dipole alignment, depending on the molecular symmetry. These results underscore the deep impact of molecular engineering on supramolecular order and anisotropic optical properties at interfaces, providing a robust strategy for the design of functional thin films down to the monolayer level for advanced optical devices.

</details>


### [88] [Machine-learned interatomic potential for sputtering of tungsten-boron surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13038)
*Alexandre Bergero,Jesper Byggmästar,Fredric Granberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习（machine learning）构建了钨-硼（W-B）体系的原子间势（interatomic potential），用于溅射（sputtering）和硼沉积（deposition）研究，发现表面构型、取向和成分均影响溅射行为，且硼沉积会形成致密硼层。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硼化（boronization）是在托卡马克（Tokamak）聚变反应堆中通过在钨表面沉积硼来减少等离子体污染的关键技术，但硼原子与钨表面的相互作用机制以及恶劣环境对表面的影响尚未完全理解，部分原因是缺乏精确的原子级模拟和原子间势。

Method: 开发了一个机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential），训练数据来自密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT），该势函数能够支持精确的大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，用于研究硼在钨表面的沉积行为以及辐照条件下钨和硼的溅射过程。

Result: 研究发现表面构型/取向和表面成分均影响溅射行为；在钨表面沉积硼会形成致密的硼层；所开发的势函数对表面和体相性质均表现出良好的准确性。

Conclusion: 所开发的机器学习势函数可用于混合W-B体系的模拟，为理解硼化过程及其在聚变堆环境中的表现提供了可靠的原子级模拟工具。

Abstract: Boronization, where boron is deposited onto tungsten surfaces, is a key technique to reduce plasma contamination, such as oxygen in Tokamak fusion reactors. The exact interaction between the boron atoms and the tungsten surface, and the effect of the harsh environment on these surfaces are however not fully understood, partially due to the lack of accurate atomistic simulations and interatomic potentials. Here, we develop a machine-learned interatomic potential for sputtering studies of W-B structures and deposition of boron onto tungsten surfaces. The machine-learned potential is trained to density functional theory data and allows accurate large-scale molecular dynamics simulations. Our aim is to understand how boron behaves when deposited on tungsten and how tungsten and boron are sputtered under irradiation. We observe that both the surface configuration/orientation and the surface composition affect the sputtering, and that depositing boron onto tungsten surfaces produces a dense boron layer. The developed potential shows good accuracy for both surface and bulk properties and can be used for simulations of mixed W and B systems.

</details>


### [89] [Engineering Chirality in Halide Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13053)
*Juan Delgado-Alvarez,Javier Castillo-Seoane,Jorge Budagosky,Lidia Contreras-Bernal,Maria Alcaire,Xabier Garcia-Casas,Jose Feria,Vanda Godinho,Ana Borras,Angel Barranco,Juan R. Sanchez-Valencia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过掠射角沉积与可控衬底旋转相结合的方法，在晶体生长过程中直接引入卤化物钙钛矿（halide perovskites）的结构手性（structural chirality），实现了生长控制的晶体扭转（crystallographic torsion），产生巨大且可调的手性光学响应，并通过气相转化将手性传递至多种钙钛矿组分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卤化物钙钛矿中的手性控制为圆偏振光子学、自旋选择性电子学和量子信息技术提供新机遇，但现有手性实现方式（手性分子构筑单元或外部光子结构）难以满足自旋相关功能对材料内部结构对称性破缺的需求，亟需探索材料自身生长过程中的手性来源。

Method: 将掠射角沉积（glancing angle deposition）与受控衬底旋转相结合，在PbI2纳米结构的晶体生长过程中直接引入晶体扭转；通过X射线织构分析表征晶体取向随生长的旋转，并利用气相转化（vapour-phase conversion）将手性传递至多种卤化物钙钛矿组分。

Result: 衬底旋转在保持c轴取向的同时使晶体取向在生长过程中逐步旋转，形成具有巨幅且可调手性光学响应的扭转织构，椭圆率（ellipticity）达19°，吸收不对称因子（absorption dissymmetry factor）接近0.6；经由气相转化后，多种卤化物钙钛矿组分表现出圆偏振发光（circularly polarized luminescence），其不对称因子g_lum高达0.23。

Conclusion: 生长控制的晶体扭转被确立为卤化物钙钛矿中一种此前未被探索的手性来源，为材料本征手性的实现提供了全新路径。

Abstract: The ability to control chirality in halide perovskites offers new opportunities for circularly polarized photonics, spin-selective electronics, and quantum information technologies. Chirality in halide perovskites is commonly achieved through chiral molecular building blocks or externally imposed photonic architectures. Although both approaches can generate strong chiroptical responses, many spin-dependent functionalities require structural symmetry breaking embedded within the material itself. Here we show that chirality can emerge directly during crystal growth. By combining glancing angle deposition with controlled substrate rotation, we generate highly textured PbI2 nanostructures with growth-controlled crystallographic torsion. X-ray texture analysis reveals that substrate rotation progressively rotates the crystal orientation during growth while preserving the c-axis orientation, resulting in a twisted texture with giant and tuneable chiroptical responses, including ellipticities of 19° and absorption dissymmetry factors approaching 0.6. The chirality programmed during growth is transferred through vapour-phase conversion into multiple halide perovskite compositions, resulting in circularly polarized luminescence with glum values up to 0.23. These findings establish growth-controlled crystallographic torsion as a previously unexplored origin of chirality in halide perovskites.

</details>


### [90] [Giant exciton effects and magneto-excitonic coupling in V4S9X4 2D magnetic semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13093)
*Yingjie Wei,Fan Zhang,Ying Zhao,Lixin Zhou,Yan Su,Yu Guo,Jijun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出通过团簇组装（cluster assembly）策略设计V4S9X4 (X = F, Cl, Br, I)单层材料，该二维半导体同时兼具本征铁磁性（居里温度高达507.6 K）和巨大激子束缚能（如V4S9Br4为1.85 eV），突破了传统半导体中铁磁性与激子束缚能相互排斥的瓶颈，有望推动室温自旋光子（spin-photonic）器件的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温自旋光电器件需要同时具备强铁磁性和巨大激子束缚能，然而在传统半导体中，磁局域化会屏蔽激子，导致这两种性质相互排斥。作者旨在寻找一种能够同时实现这两种性质的材料设计范式。

Method: 采用层级化（hierarchical）设计策略：团簇内的局域态同时承载局域磁矩和强电子-空穴相互作用，而团簇间的耦合则介导长程铁磁性。研究对象为V4S9X4 (X = F, Cl, Br, I)单层，以V4S9Br4作为原型系统进行激子性质分析。

Result: 这些二维半导体表现出本征铁磁性，居里温度（Curie temperature）最高达507.6 K。V4S9Br4单层具有1.85 eV的巨大激子束缚能，其最低激子为暗态（dark state, DI），辐射寿命（radiative lifetime）为1.20 ns，而第一个亮激子（bright exciton, BI）的辐射衰减仅86.87 ps。此外，在铁磁与反铁磁序之间切换可在保持巨大束缚能的同时宽范围调控激子寿命。

Conclusion: 团簇组装被确立为一种强有力的范式，可用于设计室温下工作的下一代自旋光子器件和量子信息器件。

Abstract: Room-temperature spin-optoelectronic devices require a combination of robust ferromagnetism and giant exciton binding, a pairing mutually exclusive in conventional semiconductors due to magnetic localization that screens excitons. Cluster-assembled V4S9X4 (X = F, Cl, Br and I) monolayers overcome this bottleneck via a hierarchical design, that is, intra-cluster localized states host both local magnetic moments and strong electron-hole interactions, while inter-cluster coupling mediates long-range ferromagnetism. Remarkably, these two-dimensional semiconductors exhibit intrinsic ferromagnetism with Curie temperature up to 507.6 K. As a prototype, V4S9Br4 monolayer possesses a giant exciton binding energy of 1.85 eV. Its lowest exciton is a dark state (DI) with a radiative lifetime of 1.20 ns, whereas the first bright exciton (BI) exhibits an ultrafast radiative decay of 86.87 ps. This stark lifetime contrast enables simultaneous ultrafast optical response and long-lived spin information storage. Most notably, switching between ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic order allows for wide-range tuning of exciton lifetime, with the giant binding energy remaining nearly intact. Our findings establish cluster assembly as a powerful paradigm for designing next-generation spin-photonic and quantum information devices operating at room temperature.

</details>


### [91] [Thermal transport in crystals: from the quantum Dyson equation to mesoscopic phonon hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13339)
*Enrico Di Lucente,Michele Simoncelli,Nicola Marzari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统性地连接了量子声子（phonon）Dyson与Kadanoff-Baym方程、半经典玻尔兹曼输运（Boltzmann transport）形式以及介观黏性热方程三个理论层级，展示了Guyer-Krumhansl和双相滞后（dual-phase-lag）方程作为其特殊极限的涌现过程，并指出这些方程可预测负热阻、稳态热回流和涡旋等非扩散热输运现象。作者进一步将黏性热方程重整为Helmholtz与双调和方程进行解析求解，以对比声子流体与其他流体系统的宏观行为，并于结尾提出将声子水动力学工具推广至其他准粒子的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介电、非磁性晶体中的热输运由量子化晶格振动（声子）介导，温度梯度驱动下声子产生漂移与相互作用，这一现象可在从全量子描述到半经典再到介观连续介质等多个理论层级上描述。该工作的动机在于严谨梳理各理论层级之间连接所依赖的步骤与近似，从而统一理解非扩散（non-diffusive）与流体动力学（hydrodynamic）热输运现象。

Method: 作者采用多尺度理论综合方法：一方面明确量子声子Dyson方程与Kadanoff-Baym方程到半经典玻尔兹曼输运形式的推导路径，另一方面阐述通过粗粒化（coarse-graining）过程得到介观黏性热方程的步骤。此外，将黏性热方程转化为Helmholtz方程与双调和方程并解析求解，以分析声子流体的宏观行为。

Result: 研究表明Guyer-Krumhansl方程和双相滞后方程分别作为黏性热方程在线性各向同性能带与无黏极限下的特例涌现。这些框架不仅能预测Poiseuille流和第二声（second sound），还可预测负热阻、稳态热回流和涡旋等更奇特效应。通过将理论框架与第一性原理（first-principles）模拟结合，可连接微观声子物理与可观察的非扩散热输运现象。

Conclusion: 文章以声子流体与水动力学系统的对比分析（聚焦可压缩性与涡度）阐明其宏观行为的相似性与差异，并勾勒出将描述声子水动力学的理论工具推广至其他准粒子（quasiparticle）的路线图，为固体中集体量子输运现象的未来研究提供方向。

Abstract: Thermal transport in dielectric, non-magnetic crystals is mediated by quantized lattice vibrations, which drift and interact when driven out of equilibrium by a temperature gradient. This phenomenon can be described at multiple theoretical levels, ranging from fully quantum descriptions to semiclassical and mesoscopic continuum approaches. This review rigorously discusses the theoretical steps and approximations connecting these levels, bridging quantum phonon Dyson and Kadanoff-Baym equations and semiclassical Boltzmann transport formalism, and discussing the coarse-graining procedures that yield mesoscopic viscous heat equations for non-diffusive, hydrodynamic heat transport in devices. We show how the Guyer-Krumhansl and dual-phase-lag equations emerge as special linear-isotropic-band and inviscid limits of the viscous heat equations, respectively; most importantly, we demonstrate that these equations predict not only Poiseuille flow and second sound, but also more exotic effects such as negative thermal resistance, steady-state thermal backflow and vortices. We highlight how combining these frameworks with first-principles simulations connects microscopic phonon physics to observable non-diffusive heat-transport phenomena and guides their detection, amplification, and control. We recast the viscous heat equations in terms of Helmholtz and biharmonic equations solved analytically, and use this to discuss similarities and differences between the macroscopic behavior of the phonon fluid and other hydrodynamic systems, such as classical and electron fluids, focusing on compressibility, vorticity, and their influence on phonon hydrodynamics. We conclude with a roadmap to generalize the tools used to describe phonon hydrodynamics to other quasiparticles, motivating future advances in collective quantum transport phenomena in solids.

</details>


### [92] [Comparison of mechanical properties of Ag/W1-xTixB2.5 and pure silver coatings deposited by PLD/HIPIMS method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13421)
*Katarzyna Zielińska,Mateusz Włoczewski,Rafał Psiuk,Jacek Hoffman,Ewa Wojtiuk,Piotr Bazarnik,Tomasz Mościcki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition, PLD）和高压脉冲磁控溅射（high-power pulsed magnetron sputtering, HiPIMS）分别制备银（Ag）层与WB2.5、W0.76Ti0.24B2.5涂层，构成Ag/WB2.5和Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5双层结构。测试表明银层在不显著改变硬度的前提下提升材料磨损性能，其中Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5兼具优异附着力、耐蚀性和耐久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属硼化物（transition metal borides）因高硬度和化学、热稳定性受到关注，而银（silver）具有优良导电导热性、耐磨性和抗菌性。将二者结合有望获得兼具超硬特性和增强功能性的双层材料，但相关双层涂层的性能对比研究尚不充分。

Method: 以脉冲激光沉积（PLD）制备银涂层，以高压脉冲磁控溅射（HiPIMS）制备WB2.5和W0.76Ti0.24B2.5涂层；采用纳米压痕（nanoindentation）测试力学性能、划痕测试（scratch test）评估附着力、往复磨损测试评估耐磨性；通过Tribotouch模拟人手擦拭，并在0.9% NaCl环境中进行腐蚀测试。

Result: 银层在所有样品中均提高了材料的耐磨性且未显著改变硬度；Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5对基体表现出良好附着力；经36000次模拟擦拭循环后该涂层仅发生宏观不可见的轻微变形，循环次数超过纯银膜的三倍；在0.9% NaCl环境中，Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5双层涂层腐蚀性能接近纯银，具有良好耐蚀性。

Conclusion: Ag/WB2.5与Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5双层涂层成功兼顾了过渡金属硼化物的高硬度与银的功能性优势，其中Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5在附着力、耐久性和耐腐蚀性方面表现尤为突出，具有较大的实用潜力。

Abstract: Transition metal borides are attracting increasing interest due to their unique properties. They are not only characterised by very high hardness, but also considerable chemical and thermal stability. Silver, on the other hand, is a good material for increasing electrical and thermal conductivity, wear resistance and has antibacterial properties due to its biological characteristics. Combining these two materials can provide superhard bilayers with increased functional properties. In this study, it was decided to synthesise Ag/WB2.5, Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5 coatings and compare their properties to the individual components. The silver coating was produced by pulsed laser deposition (PLD), while the WB2.5 and W0.76Ti0.24B2.5 coatings were formed by high-power pulsed magnetron sputtering (HiPIMS). To determine the mechanical properties, nanoindentation tests, adhesion of the coatings by scratch -test and wear resistance by abrasion in reciprocating motion were tested. In all cases, the silver film contributed to an increase in the wear resistance of the materials without major changes in the hardness results of the materials. In addition, the Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5 film showed very good adhesion to the substrate. Human hand wiping simulator was also carried out using - Tribotouch. After 36 000 cycles Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5 coating was slightly deformed, which was not visible macroscopically. This result is more than three times greater than for the pure silver film. It was also decided to carry out corrosion tests in an environment of 0.9% NaCl. The Ag/W0.76Ti0.24B2.5 bilayer has very good corrosion resistance, similar to pure silver.

</details>


### [93] [Landau theory and exchange instabilities in Mn$_5$Si$_3$: A case against altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13483)
*K. D. Belashchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过朗道理论（Landau theory）、第一性原理计算和蒙特卡罗模拟（Monte Carlo simulation），系统分析了Mn₅Si₃薄膜中两种磁有序相——体相共线反铁磁相（AFM2）和假定的交替磁（altermagnetic）相——的结构与稳定性。研究表明体相M点反铁磁失稳是自然涌现的主导不稳定性，而γ点的交替磁模在适度应变下显著弱化甚至被抑制，因此Mn₅Si₃薄膜中交替磁相很可能无法在不引入额外物理机制的情况下稳定存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn₅Si₃薄膜因其金属性、异常输运性质和假定d波交换劈裂图案，被视为最重要的交替磁候选材料之一。然而其交替磁结构的零传播矢量子假设与体相在M星的反铁磁序（AFM2）相矛盾，研究者希望厘清这两种磁结构之间的竞争关系与稳定性条件。

Method: 采用三种互补方法：朗道理论分析两种磁有序相（AFM2和γ点E₂g模）的对称分类与序参量结构；基于第一性原理计算顺磁无序局域矩态的交换失稳谱；运用蒙特卡罗模拟经典海森堡模型验证有序温度和多自旋模的选择。

Result: AFM2在朗道理论中被识别为M星单臂上对称性保护的、反演偶/置换奇模；γ点E₂g模属于同晶胞序的共线分支，两者均需高阶项来选定相。第一性原理计算正确找到M星处的领先交换不稳定性，海森堡模型模拟以合理温度进入正交3M相；γ点E₂g交替磁模显著更弱，且被Mn₅Si₃薄膜中代表性的外延应变进一步抑制。

Conclusion: 研究成果为体相M点不稳定性提供了自然解释，但强烈不利于在适度应变的类体相Mn₅Si₃薄膜中将传播矢量从M点迁移至γ点的假设，表明相应交替磁相在不引入额外物理机制（如新的耦合或杂质效应）的情况下难以稳定成立。

Abstract: Thin-film Mn$_5$Si$_3$ is one of the most studied altermagnetic candidates thanks to its metallicity, demonstrated anomalous transport properties, and assumed $d$-wave exchange splitting pattern enabling spin-polarized transport and various spintronic applications. Its postulated altermagnetic structure has zero propagation vector, in contrast to the collinear antiferromagnetic bulk phase (AFM2) which orders at the $M$ star. In this work, the two phases are analyzed using Landau theories, first-principles calculations of the paramagnetic instabilities, and Monte Carlo simulations. AFM2 appears in a Landau theory as a symmetry-protected inversion-even, permutation-odd mode at a single arm of the $M$ star. At $Γ$, the same intracell ordering pattern belongs to the collinear branch of an $E_{2g}$ order parameter. In both cases, higher-order terms are required for the phase selection. First-principles calculations for the paramagnetic, disordered-local-moment state correctly identify the leading exchange instability at the $M$ star, and the resulting classical Heisenberg model orders at a reasonable temperature into the orthogonal $3M$ phase favored by single-site entropy. The $Γ$-point $E_{2g}$ mode, whose Landau theory contains the altermagnetic sector, is substantially weaker and further suppressed by epitaxial strain representative of Mn$_5$Si$_3$ films exhibiting anomalous transport. The same strain reduces the leading magnetic exchange scale. These results provide a natural explanation for the bulk $M$-point instability but strongly disfavor the postulated relocation of the propagation vector from $M$ to $Γ$ in a moderately strained bulklike Mn$_5$Si$_3$ film, suggesting that the corresponding altermagnetic phase is unlikely to be stabilized without additional physics.

</details>

### [94] [Beyond persuasion: Mobile electoral interfaces in polarized societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13184)
*Nuno Crokidakis,Celia Anteneodo*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个极简动力学模型，将选民划分为两个稳定的党派集团（partisan bloc）和一个流动选民群体（mobile fraction），通过类费米概率规则（Fermi-like probabilistic rule）描述流动选民的分配，从而刻画极化选举中的宏观响应与政治冲击敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统选举动力学模型强调观点变化与社会影响，但当代极化社会中，稳定党派集团与选战性流动人口并存，直接说服（persuasion）机制难以解释此类现象。作者旨在提出一个不依赖对立阵营间直接说服的极简模型，以捕捉极化选举的动态本质。

Method: 将选民人口划分为两个稳定的党派集团和一个流动部分，且抑制极化集团间的直接转换；流动选民通过基于排斥不对称性（rejection asymmetries）的类费米概率规则在两个阵营之间分配。通过解析计算分析选举对政治冲击的响应特性。

Result: 解析计算表明，选举对政治冲击的敏感性（electoral susceptibility）与流动选举界面的稳态规模成正比，即选举流动性成为控制极化系统对外部扰动宏观响应的关键量。模型自然预测了从冻结极化到高响应选举状态的连续流动性区域（continuum of mobility regimes）。

Conclusion: 在强极化选举中，总体选举变化主要由流动选举界面（mobile electoral interface）上的波动驱动，而非大规模的意识形态转变。该模型为理解极化社会中的选举动态提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Many traditional models of electoral dynamics emphasize opinion change, social influence and persuasion mechanisms. In contrast, contemporary polarized societies often exhibit relatively stable partisan blocs coexisting with an electorally relevant mobile population. We propose a minimal dynamical model in which electoral competition is governed not by direct persuasion between opposing blocs, but by the dynamics and allocation of a mobile electoral interface. The electorate is partitioned into two stable partisan blocs and a mobile fraction, while direct transitions between the polarized blocs are strongly suppressed. Mobile voters are allocated between the competing blocs through a Fermi-like probabilistic rule governed by rejection asymmetries. Analytical calculations show that the electoral susceptibility to political shocks is proportional to the stationary size of the mobile electoral interface, identifying electoral mobility as the key quantity controlling the macroscopic response of polarized systems to external perturbations. The model naturally predicts a continuum of mobility regimes, ranging from frozen polarization to highly responsive electoral states characterized by a broad mobile interface. These results suggest that, in strongly polarized elections, aggregate electoral changes may be governed primarily by fluctuations at the mobile electoral interface rather than by large-scale ideological conversion.

</details>

### [95] [Tangential and normal traces for extension domains with non-Lipschitz boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12930)
*Romain Cervera,Patrick Ciarlet,Anna Rozanova-Pierrat,Alexander Teplyaev*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将经典的Lipschitz域上的向量值切向与法向迹理论推广到非Lipschitz的H¹-拓展域（H¹-extension domains），涵盖如Koch雪花等具有分形边界的域，并基于迹算子满射性与迹空间Hilbert结构定义了法向迹算子与切向迹算子，同时将Costabel的Maxwell方程双线性形式方法推广至非Lipschitz域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的向量值迹理论建立在Lipschitz域上，但许多实际问题（如含分形边界的域）并不满足Lipschitz条件，需要在更一般的H¹-拓展域框架下发展对应的迹理论，以支持Maxwell方程与Hodge-Dirac算子等偏微分方程边值问题的求解。

Method: 以H¹(Ω)元上迹算子的满射性和相应迹空间的Hilbert结构为基础定义法向与切向迹算子；法向迹算子定义在Hdiv上，利用广义Stokes公式在二维与三维空间中于Hcurl和Hc¹(Ω)上引入切向迹算子。借鉴Buffa、Costabel和Sheen (2002)对Lipschitz域的处理方式，定义两个抽象切向边界空间作为迹算子的像，建立其Hilbert结构，并构造一个抽象的旋转算子（rotation operator）以替代基于法向量的几何旋转。此外，将Costabel (1991)的强制性双线性形式方法推广到非Lipschitz域，处理Hodge-Dirac算子与Green公式，并求解rotv rotv +1算子的边值问题。

Result: 建立了非Lipschitz H¹-拓展域上的广义向量值切向与法向迹理论，构造了具有Hilbert结构的抽象切向边界空间及连接二者的抽象旋转算子，并成功将Costabel的强制性双线性形式方法推广到该框架，支持Hodge-Dirac算子与Green公式的处理及相应边值问题的求解。

Conclusion: 所构建的理论框架为分形边界等非Lipschitz域上的向量场迹分析提供了严格基础，扩大了Maxwell方程及其他相关偏微分方程边值问题的可解域类。

Abstract: We generalize the classical vector-valued tangential and normal trace theory on Lipschitz domains to the setting of non-Lipschitz $H^1$-extension domains, which includes domains with fractal boundaries such as the Koch snowflake. We define and study these operators based on the surjectivity of the trace operator for elements of $H^1(Ω)$ and the Hilbert structure of the associated trace space. The normal trace operator is defined on $\Hdiv$ in $\mathbb{R}^n$. A generalized Stokes formula allows us to introduce the tangential trace operator on $\Hcurl$ and $\Hc^1(Ω)$ in two and three dimensions. Following the approach of Buffa, Costabel and Sheen (2002) for Lipschitz domains, we define two abstract tangential boundary spaces as images of these trace operators, establish their Hilbert structure, and construct an abstract rotation operator linking them, in place of the geometric rotation based on the normal vector. We also extend Costabel's (1991) coercive bilinear form approach for Maxwell's equations to non-Lipschitz domains, yielding a theory that supports the treatment of the Hodge-Dirac operator and Green's formulas and enables the solution of boundary-value problems for the $\rotv \rotv +1$ operator within the $H^1$-extension domains framework.

</details>


### [96] [Semiglobal uniqueness for the Lorentzian Calderón problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13116)
*Lauri Oksanen,Rakesh,Mikko Salo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在半全局（semiglobal）设定下证明了洛伦兹Calderón问题的唯一性，即对闵可夫斯基度量（Minkowski metric）的潜在大扰动与小扰动进行比较。证明采用简化数据，求解了一个形式确定（formally determined）版本的问题，不依赖控制论论证或微局部分析方法，而是基于畸变平面波（distorted plane waves）和加权$L^2$估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹Calderón问题是几何逆问题的重要变体，涉及从边界数据恢复时空度量。传统方法依赖于控制论论证或通过微局部分析、高频解归约为几何逆问题，这些方法在洛伦兹情形下存在局限，因此需要探索新的证明途径。

Method: 本文在形式确定问题框架下使用减少量的数据，采用畸变平面波和加权$L^2$估计作为核心分析工具，替代了传统方法中的控制论论证或微局部分析归约。

Result: 在半全局设定下证明了洛伦兹Calderón问题的唯一性，即可将一个潜在的大扰动与一个小扰动区分开来。

Conclusion: 本文展示了无需控制论方法或微局部分析归约即可证明洛伦兹Calderón问题唯一性的可能性，为这类逆问题提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: We prove uniqueness in the Lorentzian Calderón problem in a semiglobal setting, comparing a potentially large perturbation of the Minkowski metric against a small one. Our proof uses a reduced amount of data, solving a formally determined version of the problem. In contrast to traditional approaches, it employs neither control-theoretic arguments nor a reduction to a geometric inverse problem via microlocal methods or high-frequency solutions. Instead, it relies on distorted plane waves and weighted $L^2$-estimates.

</details>


### [97] [Global classical solutions by transport noise for reaction-diffusion systems with entropy dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13332)
*Antonio Agresti,Michael Kniely,Bao Quoc Tang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明，对复杂平衡化学反应网络（complex balanced chemical reaction networks），选择恰当且具有物理动机的输运噪声（transport noise）可使系统在任意高概率下存在全局唯一的强解，且解在空间上是经典的；该类噪声还能以任意指定的指数速率增强空间波动的耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学反应网络（chemical reaction networks）所对应的反应-扩散系统在确定性框架下是否存在全局经典解仍是一个重要未解问题，尤其当反应具有高次多项式增长时尤为困难。作者希望通过引入物理上合理的噪声机制来克服确定性框架下的这一困难。

Method: 证明方法结合了三个工具：缩放极限论证（scaling-limit arguments）、极大L^p(L^q)正则性（maximal L^p(L^q)-regularity）以及熵-熵耗散估计（entropy-entropy dissipation estimates），用以处理噪声扰动下反应-扩散系统的全局适定性。

Result: 证明了引入适当输运噪声后，复杂平衡化学反应网络具有任意高概率的全局时间强解（unique strong solutions global in time with arbitrarily high probability），解路径属于C^θ_t C^∞_x（对所有θ<1/2），在空间上是经典的；此外，合适的输运噪声能以任意指定的指数速率增强空间波动的耗散。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，尽管确定性框架下全局经典解的存在性仍然开放，引入具有物理意义的噪声扰动可以绕开这一障碍，为化学反应网络系统的长期行为分析提供了稳健的概率性框架。

Abstract: The existence of global classical solutions for reaction-diffusion systems arising from chemical reaction networks remains a major open problem in the deterministic setting, especially for reactions with high polynomial growth. We prove that a suitably chosen, physically motivated transport noise yields unique strong solutions for complex balanced chemical reaction networks that are global in time with arbitrarily high probability. These solutions possess paths in $C^θ_t C^{\infty}_x$ for all $θ<1/2$ and are, in particular, classical in space. Furthermore, we show that a suitable transport noise can enhance the dissipation of spatial fluctuations at an arbitrarily prescribed exponential rate. Our proofs rely on a combination of scaling-limit arguments, maximal $L^p(L^q)$-regularity, and entropy-entropy dissipation estimates.

</details>


### [98] [Stochastic Nonlinear Waves in all the Right Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12369)
*C. H. S. Hamster*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文综述了文献中用于描述反应-扩散方程（reaction-diffusion equations）中行波（travelling waves）相位的不同定义，并针对一维随机环境下的适用性进行了分析。新结果是对Nagumo方程自洽等时相位的显式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机扰动下描述行波的相位时，不同文献采用了多种相位定义，但这些定义在随机设定中的有效性和适用性尚未被系统梳理和比较。作者希望通过概览这些定义，为随机一维情形下的相位描述选择提供理论依据。

Method: 作者对文献中不同类型的相位描述进行了系统性概览，并专门针对一维随机设定下各相位定义的适用性展开讨论。此外，对Nagumo方程给出了自洽等时相位（autonomous isochronal phase）的显式计算，这是论文的新结果。

Result: 论文呈现了对行波相位描述方法的综述性分析，并获得了Nagumo方程自洽等时位相的显式解析结果，这是一项此前未被文献报道的新贡献。

Conclusion: 不同相位定义在随机一维设定下各有优劣，理解其适用范围有助于在随机反应-扩散系统中正确描述行波动力学。Nagumo方程的自洽等时相位显式表达式为后续随机分析提供了基础工具。

Abstract: I present an overview of different phases used in the literature to describe the phase of travelling waves in reaction-diffusion equations. The discussion is tailored to understand the effectiveness of these different phase descriptions in a stochastic setting in 1D. The explicit computation of the autonomous isochronal phase for the Nagumo equation is a new result.

</details>
