### [99] [Effects of Quantum Spin-Connection Foam in the Solar System, Galaxies, and the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12404)
*Igor V. Kanatchikov,Valery A. Kholodnyi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文论证了广义相对论的前正则量子化（precanonical quantization）所描述的量子自旋连接泡沫（quantum spin-connection foam）效应可能在微小宇宙学常数和低加速度下牛顿动力学修正中已被观测到，后者表现为星系平坦旋转曲线。通过引入维度上必要出现的基本小加速度尺度 $a_* = 8\pi G\hbar\varkappa$，研究将其与标准模型纯杨-米尔斯扇区的质量隙强子尺度相关联，从而得到与 MOND 中 Milgromian 加速度尺度相容的估计值，且 $a_*^2$ 与宇宙学常数的联系给出了后者的现实数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力理论中，量子引力效应通常被认为在远低于可观测尺度的能标下才显著，而星系旋转曲线和宇宙学常数等问题仍缺乏统一的微观理论解释。本文旨在探索前正则量子引力框架下自旋连接泡沫效应是否已在现有天文和实验室观测中留下可辨识的痕迹，从而为量子引力提供观测验证途径。

Method: 论文从广义相对论的前正则量子化出发，推导出考虑基本小加速度尺度 $a_* = 8\pi G\hbar\varkappa$ 的牛顿势修正（newtonian potential modification），其中 $\varkappa$ 为具有逆空间体积量纲的维度参数。作者通过建立 $\varkappa$ 与标准模型杨-米尔斯纯扇区质量隙强子尺度之间的联系来估计 $a_*$ 的值，并将 $a_*^2$ 与宇宙学常数关联。在非相对论近似下，从前正则量子引力的最简单解计算的均值场加速度（mean-field acceleration）出发，推导出 Milgromian 修正牛顿动力学（MOND）及一个理论上有区别的插值函数。

Result: 研究所获 $a_*$ 的估计值与 MOND 中的 Milgromian 加速度尺度相容，通过 $a_*^2$ 与宇宙学常数的连接得到的宇宙学常数数值也与现实观测一致。论文还指出自旋连接泡沫修正的牛顿动力学效应可能在太阳系乃至实验室实验中可观测。

Conclusion: 量子引力可能已在宏观引力现象中留下可直接观测的痕迹，而非仅限于普朗克尺度。前正则量子引力框架下的自旋连接泡沫可为星系旋转曲线与宇宙学常数问题提供统一的微观依据，且其效应有望在太阳系和实验室尺度被进一步检验。

Abstract: We argue that effects of the quantum spin-connection foam, which describes quantum gravity according to the precanonical quantization of General Relativity, may already be observed in the form of the small cosmological constant and a modification of Newtonian dynamics at small accelerations, manifested in the flat rotation curves of galaxies. We obtain a modification of the Newtonian potential that takes into account the existence of a fundamental small acceleration scale, $a_* = 8πG\hbar\varkappa$, where $\varkappa$ is a parameter with the dimensions of inverse spatial volume that appears on dimensional grounds. The connection between $\varkappa$ and the hadronic scale of the mass gap in the pure Yang-Mills sector of the Standard Model leads to an estimated value of $a_*$ compatible with the Milgromian acceleration scale in MOND. The connection between $a_*^2$ and the cosmological constant leads to a realistic value of the latter. Milgromian MOND, together with a theoretically distinct interpolating function, is derived under the assumption that classical dynamics is modified by the mean-field acceleration calculated from the simplest solution of precanonical quantum gravity in the nonrelativistic approximation. We also indicate that the effects of Newtonian dynamics modified by the spin-connection foam may be observable in the Solar System and even in laboratory experiments.

</details>


### [100] [On Super-Hyperbolic Wormhole Geometries and their Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12412)
*Subrat Parida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个统一几何与相对论框架，用于描述一类由经典悬链面的超双曲变形构造的新型可穿越虫洞，该框架整合了局部曲率参数κ与基于Mittag-Leffler的超双曲珠形变形函数，并在一般红移函数下导出了形状函数、曲率张量、物质源、连接条件及精确的薄膜运动方程和稳定性判据，为广义相对论中的奇异几何研究提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）的构造通常面临物质源约束与几何稳定性两大难题。本文旨在通过引入新的变形几何族，在保持喉部正则性的同时建立数学自洽且物理可行的框架，从而拓展广义相对论中虫洞理论的适用边界。

Method: 从经典悬链面（catenoid）出发，引入超双曲变形（super-hyperbolic deformation）构造新几何族，其嵌入形式包含局部曲率参数κ以确保喉部正则，并采用基于Mittag-Leffler函数的超双曲面变形函数𝔖Cα(v)，其中变形参数α>1控制喉部之外的径向起点与远场增长，并在α→1⁺极限下连续逼近经典悬链面几何。在允许非恒定红移函数Φ(r)的条件下，系统解析地推导了形状函数、曲率张量、物质源以及连接条件，并得出了精确的薄膜（thin-shell）运动方程与稳定性判据。

Result: 本文获得了所有关键几何与物质量的封闭解析表达式，包括形状函数、曲率张量和物质源，并最终导出精确的薄膜方程。该框架确立了在广义相对论中构造局部稳定可穿越虫洞的明确条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为虫洞理论在广义相对论中的拓展提供了数学一致且物理可行的框架，为探索奇异几何以及辨识可穿越构型保持局部稳定的条件开辟了新的途径。

Abstract: In this study, we developed a unified geometric and relativistic framework for a new family of traversable wormholes constructed from a super-hyperbolic deformation of the classical catenoid, whose geometric embedding combines an explicit local curvature parameter $κ$, which secures a regular throat, with the Mittag-Leffler-based super-hyperboloid deformation function ${\mathfrak{S}C}_α (v)$, where the deformation parameter $α>1$ governs the radial onset and far-field growth of the embedding beyond the throat, continuously approaching the classical catenoidal geometry in the boundary limit $α\rightarrow1^{+}$. Allowing a non-constant redshift $Φ(r)$, we systematically evaluate closed analytical expressions for the shape function, curvature tensors, matter sources, and junction conditions, culminating in an exact thin-shell equation of motion and stability criterion. This work establishes a mathematically consistent and physically viable framework for the extension of wormhole theory, providing a new avenue for exploring exotic geometries within general relativity and identifying the conditions under which traversable configurations remain locally stable.

</details>


### [101] [Rainbow McVittie Horizons in an Expanding Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12417)
*Amani Ashour,Ahmed Farag Ali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在“重力彩虹”（Gravity's Rainbow）框架下推导了空间平坦McVittie时空的弗里德曼动力学与表观视界热力学，构建了不自洽吸积的完美流体扇区，并利用宇宙学观测数据（32个宇宙计时器测量和1580条Pantheon+光变曲线）约束低能彩虹形变，得到H0=68.75±2.63 km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹等参数约束，验证了小型晚期彩虹修正的观测可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于检验“重力彩虹”理论对宇宙学动力学的修正是否与现有观测数据相容，特别是在晚期宇宙中引入彩虹修正后能否解释哈勃常数（Hubble constant）等参数，并判断广义相对论极限是否仍处于参数偏好区域内。

Method: 首先在空间平坦McVittie时空下推导重力彩虹框架中的爱因斯坦方程、捕陷视界（trapping horizon）、表面重力（surface gravity）、Misner–Sharp能量、统一第一定律和Clausius关系，构成封闭热力学系统并重现弗里德曼动力学；随后引入低能彩虹形变，结合Planck物质密度先验，利用32个宇宙计时器测量和1580条Pantheon+光变曲线（含完整协方差矩阵）进行拟合约束。

Result: 拟合得到哈勃常数H0=68.75±2.63 km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹、物质密度参数Ωm=0.3157±0.0070、彩虹形变参数ε=0.0106^{+0.0234}_{-0.0231}；结果确立了小型晚期彩虹修正的观测可行性，且广义相对论极限位于参数偏好区域内。

Conclusion: 本文构建的彩虹McVittie热力学系统在理论上自洽，观测数据支持小的晚期彩虹修正，并表明广义相对论极限仍与数据一致，证实了该框架作为晚期宇宙动力学修正方案的观测有效性。

Abstract: We derive the Friedmann dynamics and apparent-horizon thermodynamics of a spatially flat McVittie spacetime in Gravity's Rainbow and construct a consistent non-accreting perfect-fluid sector with an effective rainbow Hubble rate. The Einstein equations, trapping horizon, surface gravity, Misner--Sharp energy, unified first law, and Clausius relation form a closed thermodynamic system reproducing the Friedmann dynamics. We then introduce a low-energy rainbow deformation and constrain it using 32 cosmic-chronometer measurements and 1580 Pantheon+ light curves with the full covariance matrix. Combined with the Planck matter-density prior, the fit gives $H_0=68.75\pm2.63~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, $Ω_m=0.3157\pm0.0070$, and $ε=0.0106^{+0.0234}_{-0.0231}$. The resulting constraints establish the observational viability of small late-time rainbow corrections and place the general-relativistic limit within the preferred parameter region.

</details>


### [102] [Phase-plane formulation of weak gravitational deflection in static spherical spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12423)
*Reggie C. Pantig,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相平面（phase-plane）表述来描述静态球对称黑洞（如史瓦西时空）对光的引力偏折，通过振幅与内禀相位表示轨道，利用拉格朗日反演（Lagrange inversion）得到弱偏折系数的全阶显式公式，并阐明系数中交替有理数与π依赖结构的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弱偏折计算通常需要微扰零测地线并定位位移后的出射渐近线，而本文旨在发展一种更简洁的相平面框架，直接通过振幅和内禀相位描述轨道，从而系统性地生成全阶弱偏折系数。

Method: 将轨道表示为振幅与内禀相位的函数，偏折角由相位在固定渐近端点间推进所积累的物理方位角超额决定。对于史瓦西时空，精确径向一阶积分将振幅演化约化为三次代数关系，物理分支通过单一逆代数映射给出局部相位因子；再利用拉格朗日反演以M/b幂次展开，得到全阶弱偏折系数公式。

Result: 每个弱偏折系数可分离为代数相位贡献与普适三角矩（trigonometric moment），解释了史瓦西级数中交替出现的有理数与π依赖结构；与精确径向散射积分及常规轨道微扰的独立比较均复现了标准弱偏折系数。相映射的分支奇异点与临界光子轨道重合，决定了弱偏折展开的收敛范围。

Conclusion: 该相平面框架可自然推广到一般静态球对称几何，而史瓦西三次型仅是更广泛代数类中的一个特例。这一表述不仅统一了弱偏折系数的生成方式，还揭示了其代数结构与收敛性的内在起源。

Abstract: This paper develops a phase-plane formulation of gravitational light deflection by static and spherically symmetric black holes, with Schwarzschild spacetime as the principal case. Instead of perturbing the null trajectory and locating the displaced outgoing asymptote, we represent the orbit through an amplitude and an intrinsic phase. The bending angle then follows from the excess physical azimuth accumulated while the intrinsic phase advances between two fixed asymptotic endpoints. For Schwarzschild spacetime, the exact radial first integral reduces the amplitude evolution to a cubic algebraic relation. Its physical branch generates the local phase factor through a single inverse algebraic map. Lagrange inversion then yields an explicit all-order weak-deflection coefficient formula in powers of the invariant ratio \(M/b\). Each coefficient separates into an algebraic phase contribution and a universal trigonometric moment, which explains the alternating rational and \(π\)-dependent structure of the Schwarzschild series. Independent comparison with the exact radial scattering integral and conventional orbit perturbation reproduces the standard weak-bending coefficients. The same phase framework extends to general static spherical geometries, while the Schwarzschild cubic represents an especially simple member of a broader algebraic class. The branch singularity of the phase map also coincides with the critical photon orbit and governs the convergence of the weak-deflection expansion.

</details>


### [103] [From a Sharp Thin-Shell Obstruction to a Smooth Positive-Density Initial-Data Embedding of a Virialized Halo in Lambda-FLRW Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12433)
*Seokcheon Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在经典广义相对论框架内，比较了均匀Λ-FLRW环境中正超额维里化晕的尖锐类时连接与光滑有限宽度类空初始数据嵌入方案，并用有限宽度、背景相对欠密度取代零宽度源，构造出满足哈密顿和动量约束的共形平坦、常均曲率ADM初始切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖锐Israel匹配在维里边界处会产生负表面层，这是对尖锐构造所省略的环境补偿的分布表示；作者希望用有限宽度的物理来源替代这一零宽度奇异源，消除残余分布层。

Method: 在经典GR框架内，将尖锐时间类连接替换为有限宽度、背景相对欠密度（其局部静止系能量密度保持为正），构造共形平坦、常均曲率（constant-mean-curvature）的ADM初始数据切片，验证其满足哈密顿和动量约束，并通过分辨率细化检验残差收敛；在有限半径处恢复局部有效FLRW数据，数值端点之外由精确解析局部有效FLRW解定义。

Result: 构造的切片无残余分布层，满足重建的弱、零和主导能量条件（weak, null, and dominant energy conditions）；在有限半径处，其几何与物质变量在声明容差内回归局部有效FLRW数据，且约束残差随分辨率细化而收敛。

Conclusion: 该有限宽度构造避免了尖锐匹配产生的负表面层问题，在经典GR的局部有效框架内实现了自洽的初始数据切片；GCT框架仅提供全局动机的时钟解释，本计算不涉及径向GCT幅度或常数插值。

Abstract: Within classical general relativity, we compare a sharp timelike junction and a smooth finite-width spacelike initial-data embedding of a positive-excess virialized halo in a homogeneous Lambda-FLRW environment. On the ordinary branch, timelike Israel matching at the virial boundary produces a negative surface layer: a distributional representation of the environmental compensation omitted by the sharp construction. We replace this zero-width source by a finite-width, background-relative underdensity whose local rest-frame energy density remains positive. The resulting conformally flat, constant-mean-curvature ADM initial slice satisfies the Hamiltonian and momentum constraints, with the residuals converging under resolution refinement. At finite radius, its geometric and matter variables return to the local-effective FLRW data within the declared tolerances: the constructed slice contains no residual distributional layer and satisfies the reconstructed weak, null, and dominant energy conditions. For radii beyond the numerical endpoint, the exterior is defined by the exact analytic local-effective FLRW solution. The GCT framework supplies the global motivation clock interpretation, while the present calculations are local-effective classical-GR calculations with the bound-sector constants c0 and G0 held fixed: no radial GCT lapse or constant interpolation is constructed.

</details>


### [104] [On the Stability of the Euler-Poisson Dark-Fluid Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12434)
*Balázs Endre Szigeti,Imre Ferenc Barna,Gergely Gábor Barnaföldi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对一种暗流体（dark fluid）模型进行了稳定性分析。该模型为非相对论、旋转、无黏性、自引力流体，假设球对称并采用多方物态方程（polytropic equation of state），通过Guderley–Landau–Stanyukovich问题的自相似（self-similar）假设求解耦合非线性偏微分方程组。结果显示四个Lyapunov指数中三个为负、一个为正且幅值较小，表明该自相似解仅呈弱不稳定，在考察区间内具有动态稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对暗流体模型的动力学稳定性进行定量刻画，以判断其自相似解在相空间中的长期行为是否可靠。

Method: 将暗流体描述为非相对论、旋转、无黏性、自引力流体，在球对称假设下采用多方物态方程建模，并借助Guderley–Landau–Stanyukovich问题已知的自相似ansatz求解耦合非线性偏微分方程组，进而计算Lyapunov指数以评估稳定性。

Result: 四个Lyapunov指数中有三个为负、仅一个为正，且该正指数数值相对较小，表明相空间中存在一个不稳定方向与三个收缩方向。

Conclusion: 自相似解整体仅呈弱不稳定，在考察的区间内保持了较好的动力学稳健性。

Abstract: We present a stability analysis of a dark-fluid model described as a non-relativistic, rotating, non-viscous, self-gravitating fluid. We assume spherical symmetry and model the matter by a polytropic equation of state. The resulting coupled nonlinear partial differential equation system is solved using a self-similar ansatz known from the Guderley-Landau-Stanyukovich problem. We find that three of the four Lyapunov exponents are negative, while only one is positive. This indicates one unstable direction and three contracting directions in phase space. The single positive exponent is relatively small, suggesting that the self-similar solution is only weakly unstable and remains dynamically robust over the investigated interval.

</details>


### [105] [Thermal false vacuum decay near black holes is aspherical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12469)
*D. S. Gorbunov,D. G. Levkov,V. E. Maslov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个标量场（scalar field）假真空在（3+1）维史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）附近、与霍金温度（Hawking temperature）热平衡环境下的衰变问题。研究发现，当黑洞不是太小时，假真空会发生以黑洞中心为参照的非球对称衰变，并通过真空气泡（true vacuum bubble）在事件视界（event horizon）外侧形成并扩张的方式实现。作者进一步识别出三种衰变区间：最冷最大的黑洞以量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）为主导，中间质量黑洞由热涨落（thermal fluctuations）产生非球对称临界气泡主导，而最小黑洞则由热涨落驱动的球对称临界气泡覆盖整个视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假真空衰变是粒子物理和宇宙学中的核心问题，但现有研究多局限于平坦时空。该工作旨在理解弯曲时空（特别是黑洞背景）如何影响假真空衰变的机制与拓扑结构，并探讨标量场模型与标准模型希格斯扇区（Higgs sector）在大场极限下的相似性。

Method: 作者采用一个具有负四次幺正自耦合（negative quartic self-coupling）的标量场模型，将其置于被环境加热至霍金温度的（3+1）维史瓦西黑洞背景中，分析假真空的衰变动力学。通过区分黑洞质量/温度区间，分别考察量子隧穿与热涨落两种衰变通道，并分析临界气泡的对称性特征。

Result: 研究识别出三种假真空衰变区间：在最大最冷黑洞情况下，衰变由位于视界上的无穷薄瞬子（infinitesimally thin bounce）主导的量子隧穿机制完成；中间质量区间内，热涨落驱动视界附近非球对称临界气泡的形成；在最小黑洞情形下，热涨落仍然主导衰变，但主导临界气泡变为球对称并覆盖整个视界。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：只要黑洞不是太小，假真空会以黑洞中心为参照发生非球对称衰变，真空气泡在事件视界外侧形成并扩张。衰变机制随黑洞质量呈现三阶段转变，反映了黑洞引力与热环境对真空衰变拓扑和对称性的显著影响。

Abstract: We study decay of a scalar field false vacuum near a (3+1)-dimensional Schwarzschild black hole equilibrated at Hawking temperature with the environment. Our scalar field model has negative quartic self-coupling and thereby resembles Higgs sector of the Standard Model in the large-field limit. We demonstrate that if the black hole is not too small, the false vacuum in this model decays aspherically with regard to the black hole center: via formation of expanding true vacuum bubbles emerging on the outer side of the event horizon. More specifically, we identify three regimes of the decay. For the largest and coldest black holes, the main mechanism is quantum tunneling described by an infinitesimally thin bounce sitting at some point of the horizon. In the intermediate-mass regime, the vacuum is destroyed by thermal fluctuations creating aspherical critical bubbles in the horizon vicinity. Finally, near the smallest black holes thermal fluctuations still guide the decay but the dominant critical bubble is spherically symmetric and covers the entire horizon.

</details>


### [106] [Dymnikova Black Hole Tidal Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12495)
*M. H. Macêdo,A. A. M. Silva,R. R. Landim*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Dymnikova规则黑洞（regular black hole）的潮汐性质及其对径向自由下落大质量粒子的影响，证明de Sitter核心（de Sitter core）在内部区域对潮汐动力学起到正则化作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究规则黑洞内部潮汐力的行为，以理解时空非奇异性对大质量粒子动力学的具体影响。

Method: 从Dymnikova静态球对称解出发，推导类时径向测地线方程，构造适应自由落体观测者的正交标准标架，计算潮汐张量的径向和角向分量，并求解测地偏离方程。

Result: 在大径向距离处潮汐力恢复Schwarzschild行为；在规则中心附近两个分量均保持有限。径向和角向潮汐力在事件视界内特征半径处消失并改变符号，表明拉伸与压缩模式之间的转变。从事件视界外静止释放的粒子在柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）内到达转向点而非规则中心。测地偏离向量分量在内部区域保持有限直至转向点，而Schwarzschild情形中对应径向分量在奇点处发散。

Conclusion: de Sitter核心对延伸下落体的潮汐动力学起到正则化作用，消除了Schwarzschild黑洞内侧的奇异发散行为。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the tidal properties of the Dymnikova regular black hole and their ef fects on massive particles in radial free-fall. Starting from Dymnikova static, spherically symmetric solution, we derive the equations governing timelike radial geodesics and construct an orthonormal tetrad adapted to a free-falling observer. We then obtain the radial and angular components of the tidal tensor and analyze their dependence on the black hole mass and the characteristic length scale of the de Sitter core. At large radial distances, tidal forces recover Schwarzschild behavior, whereas near the regular center, both components remain finite, reflecting the non-singular nature of the spacetime. We show that the radial and angular tidal forces vanish and change sign at characteris tic radii inside the event horizon, indicating transitions between stretching and compression regimes. A particle released from rest outside the event horizon reaches a turnaround point located inside the Cauchy horizon, rather than reaching the regular center. We also solve the geodesic deviation equations for two sets of initial conditions and examine the evolution of the radial and transverse components of the deviation vector. Although the solutions asymptotically reproduce Schwarzschild behavior, they differ significantly in the inner region: the deviation vector components remain finite up to the turnaround point, whereas the corresponding radial component in the Schwarzschild case diverges at the singularity. These results demonstrate how the de Sitter core regularizes the tidal dynamics of extended falling bodies.

</details>


### [107] [Probing the Distribution and Nature of Dark Matter Around Supermassive Black Holes from EMRI and IMRI Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12540)
*Jared Fier,Farah Abdelshahed,Lilly Kowalczyk,Zhengfei Lyu,Anzhong Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在广义相对论框架下发展了一套完全相对论性的方法，用于研究超大质量黑洞周围无碰撞暗物质晕（dark-matter halos）产生的引力波信号。以模型I为示例应用，发现相对论性暗物质晕可对EMRIs/IMRIs的引力波周期累积、波形相位、信噪比和波形失配产生可测量的修正，且这些可观测信号主要由时空几何的保守性修正主导，相对论性动力学摩擦的耗散效应可忽略不计，表明LISA等未来引力波观测可探测超大质量黑洞周围暗物质的相对论性分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大质量黑洞附近暗物质的分布对星系演化和引力精确检验至关重要，但目前仍缺乏深入理解。未来的空间引力波天文台为探测这一相对论性区域提供了独特机会，因此需要开发能够在广义相对论框架下描述该物理过程的完整理论工具。

Method: 基于作者此前在广义相对论中构造的精确爱因斯坦-云解（Einstein-cloud solutions），作者构建了一个完全相对论性的框架，对极端/中等质量比旋进（EMRIs/IMRIs）的轨道动力学、绝热旋进、累积引力波周期、波形相位演化、信噪比和波形失配进行统一处理；并将该形式体系专门化到模型I（Model I）作为代表性应用。

Result: 研究表明，相对论性暗物质晕可以对累积引力波周期、波形相位、信噪比和波形失配产生可测量的修正，且可探测性判断在各指标间一致。通过将时空几何的保守性修正与相对论性动力学摩擦（dynamical friction）导致的耗散效应分离，发现可观测特征主要由保守性修正主导，而耗散效应在所考虑的晕模型下可忽略。

Conclusion: 该结果为LISA及类似任务提供了利用引力波观测探测超大质量黑洞周围暗物质相对论性分布与物理性质的有力途径，表明这些未来观测可成为检验该区域暗物质性质的重要工具。

Abstract: The distribution of dark matter in the immediate vicinity of supermassive black holes remains poorly understood despite its importance for galaxy evolution and precision tests of gravity. Future space-based gravitational-wave observatories offer a unique opportunity to probe this relativistic regime through the inspiral of compact objects into supermassive black holes. Building upon our previously constructed exact Einstein-cloud solutions within General Relativity, we develop a fully relativistic framework to investigate the gravitational-wave signatures of collisionless dark-matter halos surrounding supermassive black holes. The framework provides a unified treatment of orbital dynamics, adiabatic inspiral, accumulated gravitational-wave cycles, waveform phase evolution, signal-to-noise ratio, and waveform mismatch for extreme- and intermediate-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs/IMRIs). As a representative application, we specialize the formalism to Model I. We show that relativistic dark-matter halos can produce measurable modifications to the accumulated gravitational-wave cycles, waveform phase, signal-to-noise ratio, and waveform mismatch, leading to consistent conclusions regarding detectability. By separating conservative modifications of the spacetime geometry from dissipative effects due to relativistic dynamical friction, we find that the observable signatures are dominated by the former, while the latter remains negligible for the halo models considered. These results demonstrate that future gravitational-wave observations by LISA and similar missions may provide a powerful probe of the relativistic distribution and physical nature of dark matter around supermassive black holes.

</details>


### [108] [Two-scale magnetically charged regular black holes from nonlinear electrodynamics and a T-duality-inspired zero-point length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12541)
*Ali Ovgun,Reggie C. Pantig,Joel Saavedra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦引力框架下，基于磁非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics, NED）构造了一个双标度、静态、球对称的规则黑洞。零长（zero-point length）ℓ与渐近磁荷q共同控制质量与电荷分布的规则化，该几何在远处趋于Reissner-Nordström解，在特定参数下可还原为零点长度解或与Ayón-Beato-García解重合。文中系统推导了能量条件、极值曲线、热容量、Smarr关系、光学度规及阴影性质，并给出了弱场可观测修正与等离子体环境下的阴影参数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准爱因斯坦引力下的黑洞解普遍存在中心奇异性（singularity），而物理上要求引力理论在极端条件下具备自洽性。作者试图通过引入磁NED与零点长度ℓ，构造无奇异的规则黑洞，同时确保其在远处与经典Reissner-Nordström解一致，从而在避免奇异性的同时保持与经典引力理论的兼容。

Method: 采用逆构（inverse reconstruction）方法，由度规与电磁场形式反推出单值磁Lagrangian；分析不同参数符号下中心几何的de Sitter/Minkowski/anti-de Sitter性质，并检验弱能量条件（weak energy condition）。通过热力学第一定律与Smarr恒等式推导极值曲线与精确热容量，保留Wald面积熵。对于光学性质，定义了异常NED光学度规（extraordinary NED optical metric），通过全局最小化光学碰撞参数（optical impact parameter）确定捕获阴影（capture shadow），并进行弱场展开以计算近心点、偏转角、时间延迟与红移修正。在等离子体情形下，对幂律密度分布求严格参数阴影关系，并结合Hamilton射线追踪与Novikov-Thorne盘模型进行数值计算。

Result: 所得黑洞家族的中心正则性由2Mℓ−q²的符号决定，可呈de Sitter、局部Minkowski或anti-de Sitter形态；弱能量条件成立当且仅当3Mℓ≥2q²。文中导出了极值曲线、精确热容量、齐次视界变分关系与Smarr恒等式。证明了该家族中每个带电荷黑洞在外通信域上具有非简并的异常NED光学度规，其捕获阴影由光学碰撞参数函数的全局最小值决定，一般不同于背景测地阴影。弱场修正中，ℓ首次出现在标准一阶后牛顿（post-Newtonian）参数之外。在冷透明等离子体中，对幂律密度分布获得了精确参数化阴影关系，Hamilton射线追踪与Novikov-Thorne盘模型给出了可比较的观测预言。

Conclusion: 该双标度NED黑洞族在正则性、渐近相容性与可观测预言上均具备自洽性，为研究规则黑洞的引力与光学效应提供了一个完整框架。特别地，零点长度ℓ对可观测量的贡献从后牛顿展开的高阶开始显现，而阴影选取机制与材料等离子体的特殊延续则揭示了NED光学度规的共形歧义（conformal ambiguity）及其在物质环境中的限制。

Abstract: We construct a two-scale, static, spherically symmetric regular black hole in Einstein gravity sourced by magnetic nonlinear electrodynamics (NED). The zero-point length $\ell$ regularizes the mass and charge profiles, whereas $q$ is the asymptotic magnetic charge. The geometry approaches Reissner-Nordström at large radius, reduces to the neutral zero-point-length solution for $q=0$, and coincides geometrically with the Ayón-Beato-García solution for $\ell=|q|$. For $q\neq0$, inverse reconstruction gives a single-valued magnetic Lagrangian with Maxwell asymptotics and a finite strong-field limit. The center is regular and is de Sitter, locally Minkowski, or anti-de Sitter according to the sign of $2M\ell-q^2$; the weak energy condition holds globally if and only if $3M\ell\geq2q^2$. We derive the extremality curve, the exact heat capacity, and homogeneous horizon-variation and Smarr identities while retaining the Wald area entropy. We also prove that every charged black hole in this family has a nondegenerate extraordinary NED optical metric throughout the domain of outer communication. The associated capture shadow is selected by the global minimum of the optical impact-parameter function and generally differs from the background-geodesic shadow. Weak-field calculations yield the periapsis, bending, time-delay, and redshift corrections; in particular, $\ell$ first appears beyond the standard first-post-Newtonian parameters. Finally, for minimally coupled test radiation in a cold transparent plasma, we obtain exact parametric shadow relations for power-law density profiles and combine Hamiltonian ray tracing with a Novikov-Thorne disk model. A separate extraordinary NED-plasma continuation is displayed only as a phenomenological prescription because a material plasma breaks the conformal ambiguity of the vacuum characteristic metric.

</details>


### [109] [Quasitopological Gravity with Matter: Modified Double-Copy Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12596)
*Valeri P. Frolov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将近期提出的修正双拷贝形式（modified double-copy formalism）推广到与物质场耦合的准拓扑引力（quasitopological gravity），通过构造平坦（D+1）维时空中的辅助非线性规范场，将球对称构型的QTG场方程映射为辅助场方程，并验证了麦克斯韦、非线性电动力学及球对称杨-米尔斯场可作为兼容的物质源，最终得到满足广义Birkhoff定理的静态解或Vaidya型解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展双拷贝方法到更为一般的引力理论——准拓扑引力，并使其能够与物质场耦合，从而扩大该形式论的应用范围。

Method: 采用修正双拷贝形式，将D维弯曲时空中带物质源的QTG场方程映射为平坦(D+1)维时空中的辅助非线性规范场方程；辅助场的非线性电动力学完全由刻画QTG模型的生成函数h(p)决定，电流由物质能动张量决定；通过将辅助解限制到D维超平面并应用修正双拷贝规则，得到求解QTG方程的Kerr-Schild度规。

Result: 证明了麦克斯韦理论、非线性电动力学以及一大类球对称杨-米尔斯场均可作为兼容的物质源；在无零电流（null current）情形下，解满足广义Birkhoff定理且为静态的，而带零电荷电流则自然生成Vaidya型解；在爱因斯坦极限h(p)=p下，辅助非线性电动力学退化为麦克斯韦理论。

Conclusion: 修正双拷贝形式成功地推广至与物质耦合的准拓扑引力，为构建该类引力理论中带物质的精确解提供了系统性的框架，并自然恢复爱因斯坦极限中的已知结果。

Abstract: We extend the recently proposed modified double-copy formalism to quasitopological gravity (QTG) coupled to matter. For spherically symmetric configurations, the QTG field equations in $D-$dimensional curved spacetime with a broad class of matter sources are mapped to equations for an auxiliary nonlinear gauge field in a flat $(D+1)$-dimensional spacetime. The nonlinear electrodynamics governing this auxiliary field is determined entirely by the generating function $h(p)$ that specifies the QTG model, while the corresponding current is determined by the matter stress-energy tensor. Restricting the auxiliary solution to a $D$-dimensional hyperplane and applying the modified double-copy prescription yields the Kerr--Schild metric solving the QTG equations. We show that Maxwell and nonlinear electrodynamics, as well as a broad class of spherically symmetric Yang--Mills fields, provide physical matter sources compatible with this construction. In the absence of null currents, the resulting solutions satisfy a generalized Birkhoff theorem and are static, whereas null charged currents naturally generate Vaidya-type solutions. In the Einstein limit, $h(p)=p$, the auxiliary nonlinear electrodynamics reduces to Maxwell theory.

</details>


### [110] [Horizon-regular cross-focusing and inner-horizon obstructions in spherical f(R) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12651)
*Maickol Muñoz-Palma,Francisco S. N. Lobo,Jean Báez Cuevas,Francisco Tello-Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在球对称度规 $f(R)$ 引力中提出了一个正则内边缘视界的“视界正则双零判据”（horizon-regular double-null criterion），利用归一化的出射和入射径向零矢量导出了面积权重出射膨胀的精确演化定律，并给出了外-内边缘视界对存在的充分条件和积分平衡条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘视界判据往往依赖陷获区假设（trapped-region assumption），对于 $f(R)$ 引力的正则内边缘视界的判别缺乏一个不依赖此类假设的普适判据。本文旨在构建一个在球对称 $f(R)$ 引力下可直接判定内边缘视界存在性及其与外部视界配对关系的框架。

Method: 构造归一化的出射与入射径向零矢量 $\ell^\mu$ 与 $n^\mu$（后者仿射参数化），推导面积权重出射膨胀 $r^2\theta_{(\ell)}$ 的精确演化定律；其源项由标量场（scalaron）$F\equiv f_R>0$ 与包含物质、标量子导数和曲率势的混合量 $\mathcal{P}_{\ell n}$ 控制。由此建立不等式条件判别正则内/外边缘视界，并推广到静态极限与简并情形。

Result: 证明若沿从非简并未来外边缘视界发出的正则内倾零截线上满足 $\mathcal{P}_{\ell n}\leq F/r^2$，则出射膨胀不可能再次归零，同一生成线不会出现第二个同族正则边缘视界；而非简并未来内边缘视界则要求反向不等式，因此外-内视界对有赖于源反转与精确积分平衡。该判据不需陷获区假设，可复现Reissner–Nordström分类，并在一个带电非恒定曲率 $f(R)$ 黑洞解中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 所得的柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）结论是有条件的，仅当候选边界同时也是正则非简并未来内边缘视界时才成立，体现了该判据对视界正则性与非简并性的明确依赖。

Abstract: We formulate a horizon-regular double-null criterion for regular inner marginal horizons in spherically symmetric metric $f(R)$ gravity. Using normalized outgoing and ingoing radial null vectors $\ell^μ$ and $n^μ$, with $n^μ$ affinely parametrized, we derive an exact evolution law for the area-weighted outgoing expansion $r^2θ_{(\ell)}$. Its source is controlled by the scalaron $F\equiv f_R>0$ and by a mixed quantity $\mathcal{P}_{\ell n}$ containing matter, scalaron derivatives, and the curvature potential. If $\mathcal{P}_{\ell n}\leq F/r^2$ along a regular ingoing null segment issuing from a nondegenerate future outer marginal sphere, then the outgoing expansion cannot return to zero, and no second regular marginal sphere of the same family can occur on that generator. Conversely, a nondegenerate future inner marginal sphere requires the reverse inequality, so an outer--inner pair necessarily entails a source reversal and an exact integral balance. No trapped-region assumption is required. In the static limit, the criterion reduces to a horizon-regular relation involving the radial derivative of the metric function and remains valid in the degenerate case under the stated regularity conditions. It reproduces the Reissner--Nordström classification and is verified in an exact charged, nonconstant-curvature $f(R)$ black hole with a nonconstant scalaron. The resulting Cauchy-horizon statement is conditional and applies only when the candidate boundary is also a regular nondegenerate future inner marginal horizon.

</details>


### [111] [Cosmological Vacuum Decays from Schwinger-Keldysh Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12736)
*Zi-Yan Yuwen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从Schwinger-Keldysh形式论出发，建立了一个系统框架来描述辐射主导的FLRW宇宙中的真空衰变。通过将相变场Φ分解为平均场φ和短波模式σ，并将后者作为环境求迹，得到平均场的经典Langevin型运动方程，其中量子效应编码在非马尔可夫记忆核和非高斯噪声中。以多项式势为例研究了记忆核结构与噪声关联函数，并讨论了进一步扩展的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学一级相变（first-order phase transition）中的真空衰变动力学在辐射主导（radiation-dominated）的FLRW宇宙背景下尚无系统完备的描述框架，需要建立能够编码量子效应的有效动力学方程。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh形式论（Schwinger-Keldysh formalism），将相变场Φ分裂为平均场（mean field）φ与短波模式σ，对后者作为环境求迹，推导出平均场的经典Langevin型运动方程（Langevin-type equation-of-motion）。以多项式势为现象学实例分析记忆核结构与非高斯噪声关联函数。

Result: 获得了平均场的Langevin型方程，其中量子效应体现为非马尔可夫记忆核（non-Markov memory kernel）和非高斯噪声（non-Gaussian noise）两项；针对多项式势具体解析了记忆核与噪声关联函数的结构。

Conclusion: 该框架可进一步放宽尺度划分限制，允许φ携带空间依赖性，从而通过数值模拟描述宇宙学一级相变的完整动力学过程。

Abstract: In this work, we establish a systematic framework to describe the vacuum decays in a radiation-dominated FLRW universe from the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism. By splitting the phase transition field $Φ$ into the mean field $φ$ and the short-wavelength modes $σ$ and tracing over the latter as the environment, we obtain a classical Langevin-type equation-of-motion for the mean field $φ$, where the quantum effects are encoded in a non-Markov memory kernel and a non-Gaussian noise. As a phenomenological example, we consider a polynomial potential and study the structure of the memory kernel as well as the correlation functions of the noise term. With a less restrictive scale split by allowing $φ$ to carry spatial dependence, further extensions remain possible to describe the whole dynamics of cosmological first-order phase transitions via numerical simulations.

</details>


### [112] [A Minkowski-core black hole with cosmological constant and electric charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12800)
*Wen-Zheng Chen,Shulan Li,Jian-Pin Wu,Cong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文在协变有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian）框架下，通过逆构造方法推导出具有闵可夫斯基核心（Minkowski-core）的规则黑洞引力约束，并将其推广至带电及含宇宙学常数情形，获得规范无关的Reissner–Nordström–AdS/dS解，证明电荷和宇宙学常数保持核心正则性，并在AdS背景下揭示了相对RN-AdS基准改变的热力学相结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在无需引入奇异物质（exotic matter）的情况下构造核心正则的黑洞解，并探讨闵可夫斯基核心正则化如何影响AdS黑洞的热力学相结构。

Method: 在协变有效哈密顿量框架下采用逆构造（inverse construction）方法推导引力哈密顿量约束；将该约束与球对称约化的麦克斯韦场（Maxwell field）耦合，并引入宇宙学常数（cosmological constant），构建带电AdS与dS解。

Result: 所得带电AdS/dS解规范无关，在正则化参数为零时退化为RN-AdS黑洞；电荷与宇宙学常数保持闵可夫斯基核心，中心处克里奇曼标量（Kretschmann scalar）为零，并呈现多视界结构。在AdS背景下，当正则化参数低于临界值时，模型表现出相变（phase transition），各热力学量给出一致信号。

Conclusion: 闵可夫斯基核心正则化可保持中心正则性，同时相对于RN-AdS基准修正AdS热力学相结构，为核心规则黑洞的构造与热力学研究提供了一致框架。

Abstract: Within a covariant effective Hamiltonian framework, we employ an inverse construction to derive the gravitational Hamiltonian constraint for a Minkowski-core regular black hole without invoking exotic matter. We then extend the constraint by coupling it to a spherically reduced Maxwell field and including a cosmological constant. The resulting charged anti-de Sitter (AdS) and de Sitter (dS) solution is gauge independent and reduces to the Reissner--Nordström--AdS (RN-AdS) black hole when the regularization parameter vanishes. Electric charge and a cosmological constant preserve the Minkowski core: the metric approaches the Minkowski geometry at the center, the Kretschmann scalar vanishes there, and the spacetime exhibits a multi-horizon structure. Focusing on AdS backgrounds, we investigate the black hole thermodynamics. For a regularization parameter below a critical value, the model exhibits a phase transition with consistent signatures across these thermodynamic quantities, demonstrating that Minkowski-core regularization can preserve center regularity while modifying the AdS thermodynamic phase structure relative to the RN-AdS benchmark.

</details>


### [113] [Reeb-Wolf Improved Landauer Principle for Hairy Black Holes via Gravitational Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12810)
*J. Andrade,J. O. Cedeño,M. Zeeshan Gul,D. Santana,Jiří Svozilík*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过扩展几何形变（extended geometric deformation）生成的毛状黑洞（hairy black hole）事件视界处不可逆信息擦除的热力学与量子信息代价，发现引力毛（gravitational hair）倾向于降低霍金温度（Hawking temperature）和最小兰道尔擦除代价（Landauer erasure cost），同时增加视界面积和贝肯斯坦-霍金熵（Bekenstein-Hawking entropy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究引力毛对黑洞视界信息擦除的兰道尔代价的影响，揭示引力毛对黑洞热力学性质及量子信息处理代价的调制作用。

Method: 以史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）为种子解，通过扩展几何形变构造满足强能量条件（SEC）和主导能量条件（DEC）的两族毛状几何；利用兰道尔原理将擦除一位信息的最小能量与变形视界的霍金温度关联，并推导视界半径、霍金温度、贝肯斯坦-霍金熵及归一化兰道尔代价作为解耦参数、毛长度尺度和有效电荷的函数；引入Reeb-Wolf有限尺寸修正并量化学其互信息（mutual information）与相对熵（relative entropy）贡献。

Result: 在分析的参数范围内，引力毛降低霍金温度和最小兰道尔擦除代价，同时增大视界面积与贝肯斯坦-霍金熵；Reeb-Wolf有限尺寸修正对擦除代价提供额外正贡献但处于次主导地位；互信息贡献由面积间隔参数控制，相对熵贡献由相邻视界能级间实际能隙控制；量化的兰道尔谱随视界量子数增大而减小，但SEC与DEC分支中的变化方式不同。

Conclusion: 引力毛的存在改变了黑洞视界的热力学性质，并由此在连续区间和面积量子化后均影响了与信息擦除相关的兰道尔代价。

Abstract: We investigated the thermodynamic and quantum-information cost of irreversible information erasure at the event horizon of hairy black holes generated through extended geometric deformation. Starting from the Schwarzschild black hole seed solution, we considered two families of hairy geometries satisfying the strong and dominant energy conditions. Lan-dauer's principle was used to relate the minimum energy required to erase one bit of information to the Hawking temperature of deformed horizon. We derived corresponding horizon radii, Hawking temperatures, Bekenstein-Hawking entropies and normalised Landauer costs as functions of decoupling parameter, the hair length scale and the effective charge associated with the additional gravitational sector. For the parameter ranges analyzed, our results show that gravitational hair tends to reduce the Hawking temperature and the minimum Landauer erasure cost while simultaneously increasing the horizon area and the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. The Reeb-Wolf finite-size correction provides an additional positive contribution to the erasure cost but remains subdominant due to the large effective dimension of the horizon reservoir. We further separated the Reeb-Wolf correction into mutual-information and relative-entropy contributions, showing that the former is governed by the area-spacing parameter, whereas the latter is controlled by the actual energy gap between neighboring horizon levels. Furthermore, the Landauer spectrum obtained by quantizing the area decreases as the quantum number associated with the horizon increases, although this variation does not occur in the same way in the SEC and DEC branches. Overall, the presence of gravitational hair alters the thermodynamic properties of the horizon and, consequently, the Landauer cost associated with information erasure, both in the continuous regime and after quantizing the area.

</details>


### [114] [Temperature effects on white dwarfs in modified gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12992)
*Sofía Vidal,Aneta Wojnar,Laur Järv*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了有质量Brans-Dicke理论以及对称子（symmetron）和膨胀子（dilaton）屏蔽机制下，有限温度状态方程（finite temperature equation of state）对白矮星平衡结构的影响，并数值计算了质量-半径关系、有效引力常数以及星体内部标量场、压强和度规的径向分布。结果表明非零温度会导致星体半径增大，而总质量基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在与引力修正理论相关的致密星体研究中，此前工作通常采用零温状态方程，但真实白矮星具有有限温度，理解温度效应对星体平衡结构的影响对于验证修改引力理论（modified theories of gravity）及其屏蔽机制（screening mechanisms）至关重要。

Method: 采用数值计算方法，在有质量Brans-Dicke理论以及对称子和膨胀子屏蔽机制框架下，引入有限温度状态方程，求解白矮星的平衡结构方程，计算质量-半径关系、有效引力常数以及星内标量场、压强和度规的径向剖面。

Result: 数值结果表明，非零温度会使白矮星的有效半径增大，而星体的总质量几乎不受温度影响；同时研究还揭示了有效引力常数、中心密度与星体半径之间的相互作用关系。

Conclusion: 有限温度效应对修改引力理论中白矮星的平衡结构有显著影响，主要体现在半径变化上，而总质量保持稳定；这一结果对理解温度与修改引力效应在致密天体中的耦合具有重要意义。

Abstract: In this article we analyze the effects of a finite temperature equation of state on the equilibrium structure of white dwarfs in massive Brans-Dicke theory as well as the symmetron and dilaton screening mechanisms. We compute and present the numerically obtained mass-radius relation, effective gravitational constant as well as radial profiles of the scalar field, pressure and metric within the star. We show that assuming a non-zero temperature effectively results in a larger radius while leaving the total mass of the star essentially unchanged, and discuss the interplay between the effective gravitational constant, central density, and radius of the star.

</details>


### [115] [Long-term 3+1 simulations of primordial black hole formation during radiation domination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13206)
*Zhuan Ning,Rong-Gen Cai,Shao-Jiang Wang,Chul-Moon Yoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个高效的三维数值相对论框架，用于研究辐射主导宇宙中由超视界曲率扰动产生的原初黑洞（primordial black hole, PBH）形成过程，通过引入宇宙学标度Gamma-driver大幅提升计算效率，并验证了与球对称结果一致的坍缩阈值和临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞形成是涉及非球对称效应的重要物理过程，但此前缺乏高效的三维数值相对论工具来模拟长时程演化，尤其是在辐射主导宇宙中的超视界曲率扰动情形。

Method: 在自适应网格加密（adaptive mesh refinement）代码GRChombo中实现通量守恒的相对论流体力学（flux-conservative relativistic hydrodynamics），并引入宇宙学标度的Gamma-driver使宇宙时间步长随尺度因子（scale factor）成比例增长；同时构造共形时间的moving-puncture规范作为独立验证。

Result: 标度driver在保持视界质量演化和约束行为的同时，将粗网格推进次数减少约94倍；针对球对称高斯（Gaussian）曲率剖面的模拟得到坍缩阈值0.79578 < μ_c < 0.79580和临界指数γ ≃ 0.3559，与先前球对称结果一致；后期PBH质量增长符合Zel'dovich–Novikov吸积定律。

Conclusion: 该框架能够同时追踪近临界坍缩和形成后的长时程演化，为未来超越球对称的原初黑洞形成研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop an efficient three-dimensional numerical-relativity framework for primordial black-hole (PBH) formation from superhorizon curvature perturbations in a radiation-dominated Universe. We implement flux-conservative relativistic hydrodynamics in the adaptive-mesh-refinement code \textsc{GRChombo} and introduce a cosmologically scaled Gamma-driver that allows the cosmic-time step to grow in proportion to the scale factor. For a representative long-term simulation, the scaled driver preserves the apparent-horizon mass evolution and constraint behavior while reducing the number of coarse-level advances by a factor of approximately $94$ relative to the standard driver. We also construct a conformal-time version of the moving-puncture gauge as an independent check. Applying the framework to a spherical Gaussian curvature profile, we find a collapse threshold $0.79578 < μ_c < 0.79580$ and a critical exponent $γ\simeq 0.3559$, consistent with previous spherically symmetric results. We further fit the late-time PBH mass growth to the Zel'dovich--Novikov accretion law, demonstrating that the code can follow both near-critical collapse and long-term post-formation evolution in three dimensions. The framework provides a foundation for future studies of PBH formation beyond spherical symmetry.

</details>


### [116] [White dwarfs in minimal dilatonic gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13236)
*Denitsa Staicova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极小膨胀引力（minimal dilatonic gravity, MDG）——一种固定耦合常数α²=1/3、自由康普顿长度λ_Φ的Brans-Dicke理论——中的静态球对称白矮星。研究发现MDG白矮星对于任意λ_Φ都是严格亚钱德拉塞卡（sub-Chandrasekhar）的：最大质量从广义相对论中的1.425 M_⊙降至200 km处的1.27 M_⊙和500 km处的1.09 M_⊙。结合旋转和引力红移观测可将λ_Φ分别限制在≲300 km和≲720 km，而太阳系侧的量级约束则来自Kepler反演的GM_⊕的百分比级高度依赖。文章引入了一种带线性化外域Robin条件的自由边界搭配法（collocation method）来处理该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDG作为Brans-Dicke理论的一个特例，其白矮星性质尚未被系统研究。理解该理论对白矮星最大质量的修正，并通过观测数据约束自由参数λ_Φ，是检验该引力理论的重要途径。

Method: 求解完整的相对论性结构方程（full relativistic structure equations），并对刚体旋转和引力红移情况进行一致分析。数值上采用一种自由边界搭配法，配合线性化外域Robin边界条件，以消除打靶法（shooting method）的指数刚性（exponential stiffness）问题，从而能在双精度下访问屏蔽区（screened regime）。

Result: MDG白矮星在所有λ_Φ取值下均为严格亚钱德拉塞卡，最大质量从GR的1.425 M_⊙分别降至λ_Φ=200 km时的1.27 M_⊙和λ_Φ=500 km时的1.09 M_⊙。MDG对最大质量的压降比例对方程状态（equation of state）稳健。质量脱落（mass shedding）附近的刚体旋转分析以及引力红移观测分别给出λ_Φ≲300 km和λ_Φ≲720 km的约束；太阳系侧，由于膨胀子质量与密度无关，同一耦合产生Kepler反演GM_⊕的百分比级高度依赖性。

Conclusion: MDG理论对白矮星最大质量产生显著的、与状态方程无关的压降，实测白矮星与太阳系观测共同将λ_Φ限制在一个较窄范围（约300 km以内）。文中提出的自由边界搭配法为类似具备指数刚性困难的天体物理标量-张量问题提供了可行数值工具。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric white dwarfs in minimal dilatonic gravity (MDG) -- a Brans-Dicke theory with fixed coupling $α^2=1/3 $ and free Compton length $λ_Φ$. Solving the full relativistic structure equations we find that MDG white dwarfs are strictly sub-Chandrasekhar for every $λ_Φ$: the maximum mass falls from $1.425\,M_\odot$ in GR to $1.27\,M_\odot$ at $200$ km and $1.09\,M_\odot$ at $500$ km. The ratio by which MDG reduces the maximum mass is robust against the equation of state. Including rigid rotation near mass shedding, consistency with the most massive observed white dwarfs restricts $λ_Φ \lesssim 300$ km. An independent gravitational-redshift bound gives $λ_Φ \lesssim 720$ km. Because the dilaton mass is density-independent the same coupling produces a percent-level altitude dependence of the Kepler-inferred $ GM_\oplus$, constraining it from the Solar-System side. To tackle the problem, we introduce a free-boundary collocation method with a linearized-exterior Robin condition that eliminates the exponential stiffness of shooting methods and gives access to the screened regime in double precision.

</details>


### [117] [Gravitational Wave Standard Sirens as Probes of Lorentz Violation in Bumblebee Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13282)
*Ayan Banerjee,Bobur Turimov,Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Sulton Usanov,Farkhod Turaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文利用引力波标准警报器（standard sirens）预测爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET）对Bumblebee引力中洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz violation）的灵敏度，并结合Ia型超新星数据评估改善效果。结果显示超新星数据大幅改善背景参数（如H₀、Ωm），但洛伦兹破缺参数ℓ₀仍受先验主导，最佳预测精度比GW170817给出的界限弱约4.7×10¹²倍，主要结论是一个量化的灵敏度差距而非预测探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波标准警报器可直接测量光度距离，为在宇宙学尺度上检验引力理论提供新途径。本文旨在利用这一工具评估爱因斯坦望远镜对Bumblebee引力中洛伦兹破缺的探测能力，并检验加入Ia型超新星数据后的影响。

Method: 研究分别考虑常数场和演化场两种情形，利用包含10³事件的模拟ET目录以及Pantheon+类超新星样本进行预测。在演化场情形中，Bumblebee真空期望值随红移演化，同时影响宇宙膨胀和引力波传播振幅。预测结果还映射到现象学的(Ξ, n)描述框架中，以便与其他引力模型的标准警报器研究进行直接比较。

Result: 超新星数据显著改善背景参数：常数场情形下H₀和Ωm的不确定度分别减少约4.4倍；演化场情形下分别减少约1.6倍和6.2倍。然而洛伦兹破缺参数ℓ₀仍受先验主导，演化指数β仅由引力波扇区约束。最佳预测精度Δℓ₀≈0.028，比GW170817给出的界限弱约4.7×10¹²倍，说明核心结果为灵敏度差距而非探测能力。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了ET对Bumblebee引力中洛伦兹破缺的量化灵敏度上界，明确了相较于GW170817存在的巨大灵敏度差距。通过在(Ξ, n)描述框架中表达预测，为与其他引力模型的标准警报器研究提供了直接比较基础。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave standard sirens provide a direct measurement of luminosity distance and therefore offer a new way to test gravity over cosmological scales. We use this idea to forecast the sensitivity of the Einstein Telescope (ET) to Lorentz violation in Bumblebee gravity, and we examine how the forecast changes when Type~Ia supernova information is added. A timelike Bumblebee vacuum expectation value can affect the cosmic expansion and, when it evolves with redshift, the propagation amplitude of gravitational waves. We study a constant-field case and an evolving-field case using mock ET catalogues with $10^3$ events together with a Pantheon+-like supernova sample. The supernova data substantially improve the background parameters: in the constant-field case the uncertainties in $H_0$ and $Ω_m$ decrease by a factor of about $4.4$, while in the evolving-field case they decrease by factors of about $1.6$ and $6.2$, respectively. By contrast, the Lorentz-violating parameter $\ell_0$ remains prior dominated, and the evolution index $β$ is constrained only by the gravitational-wave sector. The best forecast precision, $Δ\ell_0\simeq0.028$, is about $4.7\times10^{12}$ times weaker than the bound implied by GW170817. The principal result is therefore a quantified sensitivity gap rather than a forecast detection. We also express the prediction in the phenomenological $(Ξ,n)$ description of modified gravitational-wave propagation, allowing direct comparison with standard-siren studies of other gravity models.

</details>


### [118] [Differential Obstructions to Curvature-Dependent Conformal Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13289)
*David S. Pereira,Francisco S. N. Lobo,José Pedro Mimoso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了依赖于曲率的共形规则 $G_{μν}=F(R[g])g_{μν}$（非退化类 $F>0$, $F_R\neq0$）的数学结构，证明了其前向映射可借由辅助标量场局部化，但恢复原始度规需满足微分约束；完整逆切向映射包含非多项式投影算子，因而不存在开集上的有限次导数逆映射，仅在有边界或柯西数据的条件下可分支性地定义函数逆。研究以度规 $f(R)$ 引力为实例，推导了拉回Hessian、离壳嵌入项及标量子非局域核，澄清了曲率依赖的标架变换对有效作用量与量子标架等价性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力（modified gravity）理论中，曲率依赖的共形变换（如从 Jordan 标架到 Einstein 标架）被广泛使用，但其前向映射并非一般意义下度规变量的局域变化，这给有效作用量计算和量子标架等价性分析带来了根本性的概念困难。本文旨在为这类变换提供一个精确的数学框架，明确其局域性阻碍的来源和适用范围。

Method: 作者引入独立的辅助标量场（auxiliary scalar）来实现前向映射的局域化，并通过微分约束恢复原始度规。通过分析完整逆切向映射中出现的非多项式投影子 $ξ^μξ^ν/ξ^2$，论证了不存在开集上的有限次导数逆映射（finite-jet inverse）。以度规 $f(R)$ 引力为具体例证，推导了拉回经典Hessian、离壳嵌入项，并在二次模型中借助约束高斯消去法（constrained Gaussian elimination）得到标量子非局域核、法向行列式及零模相容条件。

Result: 结果表明：曲率依赖共形规则的前向映射可通过辅助标量实现局域参数化，但想要恢复度规本身则必须引入由法向算子（normal operator）控制的微分截面。完整逆映射必含非多项式投影项，因而不存在仅依赖同点有限阶导数的可微逆；但给定边界或柯西数据后，分支性的函数逆仍然可以定义。精确的父理论与度规解保持等效，阻礙仅体现在局域性及离壳变分和涨落域层面。

Conclusion: 本文为修正引力中的曲率依赖标架变换提供了精确的数学框架，明确了局域性阻碍的几何根源，并澄清了其在有效作用量、半经典分析和量子标架等价性中的含义。

Abstract: Curvature-dependent conformal rules are local forward assignments on known metrics, but they are not generically local changes of metric variables. We study the nondegenerate class $\widetilde g_{μν}=F(R[g])g_{μν}$, with $F>0$ and $F_R\neq0$. Introducing an independent auxiliary scalar localizes the forward map, while recovering the original metric requires a differential constraint. The complete inverse metric tangent map contains the nonpolynomial projector $ξ^μξ^ν/ξ^2$; hence no differentiable finite-jet inverse, i.e., a formula involving only finitely many derivatives at the same point, exists on an open set of unrestricted metrics. Branchwise functional inverses may nevertheless exist after boundary or Cauchy data are specified. Metric $f(R)$ gravity gives an explicit realization: its local Einstein-frame scalar--tensor representation is a parent theory, whereas a metric-only Einstein-side description requires a differential section governed by a normal operator. We derive the pulled-back classical Hessian and its off-shell embedding term, and in the quadratic model show how constrained Gaussian elimination produces the scalaron nonlocal kernel, the corresponding normal determinant for the displayed measure, and the zero-mode compatibility condition. Exact parent and metric solutions remain equivalent; the obstruction concerns locality and the off-shell variational and fluctuation domains. These results provide a precise framework for assessing curvature-dependent frame transformations in modified gravity and clarify their implications for effective actions, semiclassical analyses, and quantum frame equivalence.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum-Corrected Thermodynamics and Phase Structure of AdS Euler-Heisenberg Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13378)
*Siddhartha Sankar Borah,Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Kalyan Bhuyan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由非线性电动力学修正引力（nonlinear electrodynamics-corrected gravity）导出的AdS黑洞在热涨落（thermal fluctuations）影响下的热力学行为，在扩展相空间中分析了修正后的热力学量，发现比热容出现多重发散与符号变化，表明存在真实的二级相变，且热涨落可定性地重构黑洞的相空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子效应对AdS黑洞热力学稳定性的影响，特别是通过热涨落修正理解黑洞在扩展相空间中的相变行为与稳定性机制。

Method: 基于爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡（Einstein–Euler–Heisenberg）框架构造修正黑洞解，在扩展相空间（extended phase space）中利用对数项与逆面积项修正熵，进而推导焓、内能、亥姆霍兹自由能和吉布斯自由能的修正表达式，并分析修正后比热容的奇异性与相变特征。

Result: 修正后的比热容出现多重发散与符号变化，表明系统经历真实的二级相变；结果揭示了一个由量子效应稳定的微观相以及随后出现的普适宏观失稳阶段。

Conclusion: 热涨落能够定性地重构黑洞的热力学相空间，量子修正在决定黑洞稳定性方面起主导作用。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic behaviour of an AdS black hole arising from nonlinear electrodynamics-corrected gravity, incorporating quantum effects through thermal fluctuations. From the Einstein--Euler--Heisenberg framework, we consider the modified black hole solution and analyse its thermodynamic properties in the extended phase space. Logarithmic and inverse-area corrections to the entropy are obtained, leading to modified expressions for enthalpy, internal energy, Helmholtz free energy and Gibbs free energy. The corrected specific heat exhibits multiple divergences and sign changes, signalling genuine second-order phase transitions and revealing a quantum-stabilized microscopic phase followed by universal macroscopic instability. Our results demonstrate that thermal fluctuations qualitatively restructure the thermodynamic phase space and highlight the dominant role of quantum corrections in governing the black hole stability.

</details>


### [120] [Quadratic effective energy--momentum tensor on uniform-density hypersurfaces during slow-roll inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13498)
*Inyong Cho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了慢滚暴胀期间均匀密度超曲面上的标量宇宙学扰动在二次阶有效能动张量（2EMT）的行为。作者施加完整标量规范条件δρ=0和E=0，将所有扰动用Bardeen势Ψ表达，并在长波和短波区域评估傅里叶空间中的2EMT，揭示了严格红外与中间红外极限、严格紫外与中间紫外极限下的规范依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学微扰理论中，二次阶有效能动张量（2EMT）用于描述标量扰动对背景动力学的反向作用。由于2EMT非规范不变，其在不同规范选取下的行为差异需要被系统梳理，尤其在与新重算的纵向量规范（longitudinal gauge）、平空间规范（spatially-flat gauge）和共动规范（comoving gauge）结果比较时，规范依赖的结构性规律有待阐明。

Method: 采用均匀密度规范（uniform-density gauge），施加完整的标量规范条件δρ=0和E=0，将线性和暴胀子扰动全部以Bardeen势Ψ表示。在长波长与短波长两个区域分别对傅里叶空间的2EMT进行评估，区分严格（strict）红外/紫外极限与中间（intermediate）红外/紫外区域，并与纵向量、平空间和共动规范的结果进行重新比较。

Result: 在严格红外极限下，均匀密度与共动规范结果一致；在中间红外区域，主导领头阶保持不变，但显式有限梯度修正区分了两种规范。在紫外区域，2EMT因缓慢变化的物质时钟ρ₀'∝ε而获得1/ε增强，均匀密度最小领头项还因拉普拉斯项获得额外的1/σ₂²增强。均匀密度与共动切片在绝热超哈勃模上一致，而纵向量与平空间规范在严格红外下受慢滚抑制，并在中间红外区域转为梯度主导。

Conclusion: 2EMT的规范依赖具有清晰的结构：绝热超哈勃模上均匀密度与共动规范等价，而纵向量与平空间规范的表现则从慢滚抑制过渡到梯度主导。中间紫外展开在不改变领头项的前提下使次领头梯度层级结构变得显式。

Abstract: We investigate the quadratic-order effective energy--momentum tensor (2EMT) of scalar cosmological perturbations on uniform-density hypersurfaces during slow-roll inflation. The 2EMT is constructed from terms quadratic in the linear metric and inflaton perturbations, and is therefore a gauge-fixed effective source rather than a gauge-invariant observable. We impose the complete scalar gauge conditions $δρ=0$ and $E=0$, express all perturbations in terms of the Bardeen potential $Ψ$, and evaluate the Fourier-space 2EMT in the long- and short-wavelength domains. We distinguish the ``strict'' infrared and ultraviolet limits from the ``intermediate'' regimes. The uniform-density and comoving results agree in the strict infrared limit. In the intermediate infrared regime, the dominant leading order remains the same, while explicit finite-gradient corrections distinguish the two gauges. In the ultraviolet, the 2EMT is enhanced by $1/ε$ due to the slowly varying matter clock, $ρ_0'\proptoε$, and the leading uniform-density 2EMT terms exhibit an additional enhancement by $1/σ_2^2$ $(σ_2\equiv \cal{H}/k)$ from the Laplacian term. The intermediate ultraviolet expansion makes the subleading gradient hierarchy explicit without changing the leading terms. We compare these results with newly recalculated longitudinal, spatially-flat, and comoving expressions, displayed in a more explicit form than in the earlier analysis. The comparison shows that the gauge dependence is structured: uniform-density and comoving slicings coincide for adiabatic super-Hubble modes, whereas the longitudinal and spatially-flat gauges are {\it slow-roll} suppressed in the strict infrared and become {\it gradient} dominated in the intermediate infrared.

</details>


### [121] [Vector Perturbations in Ghost-Free Quasidilaton Massive Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13529)
*Ekapob Kulchoakrungsun,Daris Samart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了无鬼扩展准膨胀大质量引力（extended quasidilaton massive gravity）中横向矢量扰动（transverse vector perturbations）的行为，发现在自加速分支（Branch J=0）上引力矢量模式的动力学系数 KV 在真空和存在最小物质（标量场、麦克斯韦或普洛卡场）时均为零，说明这些模式在线性阶无限强耦合，普通最小物质不足以修复该分支的矢量扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在考察扩展准膨胀大质量引力理论中，最小物质（minimal matter）能否修复无动力学项的引力矢量模式。

Method: 在无准膨胀动能项的鬼自由扩展准膨胀大质量引力框架下，引入正则标量场（canonical scalar field）和单一阿贝尔矢量场（麦克斯韦或普洛卡场），积分掉辅助移位（auxiliary shift）场后计算矢量模的动力学系数，并在二次阶检查标量/矢量物质与引力矢量的耦合。

Result: 真空情形复现了已知结果：自加速分支 J=0 上 KV=0。加入物质后 KV 仍为零。标量物质的横向扰动不存在，其贡献项在使用 Friedmann 方程后抵消；无各向同性背景的麦克斯韦或普洛卡场在二次阶不与引力矢量混合。因此最小物质不改变 Branch II 上 KV=0 的结论。

Conclusion: 普通最小物质不足以使 Branch II 的矢量扇区在线性阶上保持理论健康（perturbatively healthy），若需健康的引力矢量模，应使用 Branch I。

Abstract: We study transverse vector perturbations in ghost-free extended quasidilaton massive gravity without a quasidilaton kinetic term, in the presence of minimal matter. In vacuum, we recover the known result that the kinetic coefficient $K_V$ of the gravitational vector modes vanishes on the self-accelerating branch $J=0$, so those modes are infinitely strongly coupled at linear order. We then add a canonical scalar field and a single Abelian vector (Maxwell or Proca). After integrating out the auxiliary shift, we find the same $K_V$ as in vacuum. The scalar matter has no transverse perturbation; it enters the unsimplified shift constraint, but those terms cancel once the Friedmann equation is used. A Maxwell or Proca field with vanishing isotropic background does not mix with the gravitational vectors at quadratic order. Minimal matter therefore leaves $K_V=0$ on Branch II. We do not claim that the modes are absent from the nonlinear theory. We do conclude that ordinary minimal matter is not enough to make the vector sector perturbatively healthy on this branch. If we need healthy gravitational vector modes at the linear level, Branch I is the branch to use.

</details>
