### [122] [Quantum-Geometric Bound on Dynamical Instability in Bosonic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12406)
*A. M. Tishin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模二次玻色系统中量子几何速度（Fubini-Study速度）与动力学不稳定性速率之间的关系，证明在裸模真空态参考下，Fubini-Study速度等于流动的对称拉伸部分的Frobenius范数，并由此导出一个由共振纯压缩器饱和的尖锐不等式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何速度与动力学不稳定性速率是驱动量子系统中两种自然的速率度量，但即使在可精确求解的动力学中，二者的关系也尚未明确。

Method: 针对任意多模二次玻色系统（bare-mode真空作为参考态），将Fubini-Study速度与流动的对称拉伸部分的Frobenius范数相关联，进而推导相应的不等式。

Result: 证明Fubini-Study速度v_FS等于流动中对称拉伸部分的Frobenius范数，并获得不等式λ_max ≤ √2 v_FS，该界由共振纯压缩器饱和；该界不可逆，在失谐参量放大器中可固定一个速率而改变另一个。

Conclusion: 量子几何速度与动力学不稳定性速率之间存在由共振纯压缩器饱和的尖锐上界关系，但该关系并非双向可逆。

Abstract: Quantum-geometric speed and dynamical instability are two natural rates for a driven quantum system, and their relation is unsettled even for exactly solvable dynamics. Here we show that for any multimode quadratic bosonic system referred to the bare-mode vacuum the Fubini-Study speed v_FS is the Frobenius norm of the symmetric, stretching part of the flow. This yields a sharp bound, lambda_max <= sqrt(2) v_FS, saturated by a resonant pure squeezer. The bound is not invertible: in a detuned parametric amplifier we hold either rate fixed while varying the other.

</details>


### [123] [Is the Aharonov-Casher phase geometrical or dynamical?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12427)
*Igor Kuzmenko,Y. B. Band,Yshai Avishai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究二维电子系统（包括普通二维导体的薛定谔方程描述和单层石墨烯的狄拉克方程描述）在垂直均匀电场产生的Rashba自旋轨道相互作用（RSOI）下的Aharonov-Casher（AC）相位。研究发现Rashba矢量势无法通过规范变换消除，但对于平面波解，可借助AC相位因子（在薛定谔方案中为几何相位，在狄拉克方案中为动力学相位）来处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望厘清Rashba自旋轨道相互作用（RSOI）在不同二维电子系统中的规范性质，并揭示Aharonov-Casher（AC）相位在薛定谔方程（SE）与狄拉克方程（DE）两种描述下的本质差异。

Method: 采用二维薛定谔方程描述无相互作用电子系统，采用二维狄拉克方程描述无质量费米子的单层石墨烯系统；两种情形中RSOI均通过SU(2) Rashba矢量势A_R表示。通过规范变换分析和平面波解，构造酉矩阵（即AC相位因子）来消除A_R，并分别考察薛定谔方案和狄拉克方案中的AC相位特性。

Result: 结果表明，A_R无法通过规范变换从二维SE或二维DE中消除；但平面波解中存在一个酉矩阵（AC相位因子）可在1D SE中消除A_R。在DE平面波解中，A_R分解为波矢方向分量A_{R,k}和法向分量A_{R,n}：后者产生无法从DE中消除的有效电子质量，前者产生可由含时幺正变换消除的AC相位。狄拉克AC相位是含时的，即动力学相位（dynamical phase），与薛定谔AC相位为几何相位（geometrical phase）形成对比。

Conclusion: 薛定谔方案中的AC相位本质上是几何相位，而狄拉克方案中的AC相位本质上是动力学相位，两种描述下的AC相位具有根本不同的物理属性。

Abstract: We consider two two-dimensional (2D) electronic systems in the presence of a perpendicular homogeneous electric field that generates a Rashba spin-orbit interaction (RSOI): a system of non-interacting electrons in a 2D conductor, modeled using the 2D Schrödinger equation (SE), and a single-layer graphene system, modeled using a 2D Dirac equation (DE) for massless fermions. In both cases the RSOI is expressed via an $SU(2)$ Rashba vector potential ${\bf A}_{R}$. We demonstrate that ${\bf A}_{R}$ cannot be eliminated from either the 2D SE or the 2D DE via a gauge transformation. Nevertheless, for a plane wave solution, an $SU(2)$ matrix exists that eliminates ${\bf A}_{R}$ from the resulting 1D SE. This unitary matrix is an Aharonov-Casher (AC) phase factor, and facilitates the calculation of the AC phase in the Schrödinger scheme. The plane wave solution for the DE contains two components of ${\bf A}_{R}$: $A_{R, k}$ in the direction of the wave vector ${\bf k}$, and $A_{R, n}$ normal to ${\bf k}$. The latter generates an effective electron mass that cannot be eliminated from the DE. The former generates an AC phase that can be eliminated by a time-dependent unitary transformation. Thus, the Dirac AC phase is time-dependent, i.e., it is a dynamical phase. This is in contradistinction to the Schrödinger AC phase which is geometrical.

</details>


### [124] [Exceptional activated mode theory for generalized real-complex transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12475)
*Mengjie Yang,Alexander N. Poddubny,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一个非微扰的"激活模式"原理（activated-mode principle），用于普遍性地刻画非厄米系统（non-Hermitian systems）中实-复谱转变（real-to-complex spectral transitions）的阈值，并给出闭合形式的例外激活条件（exceptional-activation condition），适用于任意强度的扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米系统中实-复谱转变标志着放大的出现，但其阈值以往常被视为依赖于具体模型的量，缺乏普适性的理论框架。

Method: 基于一个核心洞察：转变起始时仅有一个较小的希尔伯特子空间被"激活"，该子空间可通过谱失谐（spectral detuning）与模式级投影非厄米耦合（mode-level projected non-Hermitian couplings）之间的竞争进行变分确定；由此推导出对任意大小的扰动均成立的闭合形式例外激活条件，而非微扰近似。

Result: 该框架应用于三个对比性示例，得到：(i) 在所有系统尺寸下均闭合的临界非厄米趋肤放大（critical non-Hermitian skin amplification）阈值；(ii) 杂质隧穿阈值与例外点切换（exceptional point switching）之间的新关联；(iii) 不存在底层拓扑相变（topological phase transition）情况下仍发生的激活通道切换。

Conclusion: 本文重新将实-复谱转变刻画为通用的模式选择（mode-selection）问题，不依赖于任何特定对称性，为跨系统类别的放大型非厄米现象提供了统一理解框架。

Abstract: Real-to-complex spectral transitions mark the onset of amplification in non-Hermitian systems, but their thresholds are often treated as model-specific quantities. Here we develop a general, non-perturbative activated-mode principle that governs the real-to-complex threshold across broad classes of non-Hermitian systems. A central insight is that only a small Hilbert subspace is ``activated" at the transition onset, which can be variationally determined through the competition between spectral detuning and mode-level projected non-Hermitian couplings. The result is a closed-form exceptional-activation condition for arbitrarily large ``disturbances", rather than a perturbative estimate. We apply our framework to three contrasting illustrative problems, establishing (i) a closed-form threshold for critical non-Hermitian skin amplification at \emph{all} system sizes; (ii) a new link between impurity tunneling threshold and exceptional point switching; and (iii) activation channel switching without underlying topological phase transition. Overall, our findings recast real-to-complex transitions as generic mode-selection problems independent of any specific symmetry.

</details>


### [125] [Emergent trans-moiré orbitals and topology in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12478)
*Yuqin Wang,Jian Xie,Yi-Jie Wang,Jiajun Zhang,Yiting Gao,Zaizhe Zhang,Da Yi,Yan Xie,Jingjing Shi,Guanqin Zhao,Chengyu Xiong,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Zhi-Da Song,Xiaobo Lu,Yi Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunnelling microscopy）成像，在菱面体六层石墨烯中首次观察到了此前未知的"跨莫尔轨道"（trans-moiré orbitals），揭示了小扭转角莫尔界面与电子远离界面这两个看似矛盾条件背后的统一微观机制，为分数量子反常霍尔效应（fractional quantum anomalous Hall effect, FQAHE）的物理解释提供了关键缺失环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子反常霍尔效应在菱面体石墨烯/六方氮化硼莫尔超晶格中已被实验证实，但其背后的微观机制尚不明确，核心难题在于该体系中存在两个看似矛盾的实验条件：显著的微小扭转角莫尔界面，以及电子需远离该界面。理解这一矛盾的物理根源是推进拓扑量子计算应用的前提。

Method: 在同时满足小扭转角和电子远离界面两个条件的前提下，采用扫描隧道显微成像技术观察菱面体六层石墨烯的电子结构；结合数值模拟分析相互作用驱动的电荷重分布机制，并对比不同扭转角下跨莫尔轨道的存在与否与量子反常霍尔（QAHE）平台的关系。

Result: 观察到跨莫尔轨道在莫尔界面的远端形成，但在所有测量的填充数下均强制施加莫尔周期性；被掺杂电子按序占据的轨道呈现空间与能量上的层级结构，其中最低能轨道呈空心笼状形态，与小填充数下的FQAHE直接相关；当扭转角≥1°时这些轨道消失，相应的QAHE平台也一并消失。模拟表明这一现象由相互作用驱动的电荷重分布机制造成。

Conclusion: 两个看似矛盾的条件获得自然解释：电子并非简单地远离小扭转角莫尔界面，而是在该条件下被迫进入拓扑跨莫尔轨道中。这一发现补全了微观机制的关键缺失环节，并为设计更广泛的"合成"FQAHE平台打开了新途径。

Abstract: The fractional quantum anomalous Hall effect (FQAHE) exhibited in fractional Chern insulators has recently been demonstrated in twisted MoTe2 and rhombohedral graphene/hBN moiré superlattices, promising new routes toward topological quantum computation. Central to realizing this promise is the understanding of the underlying microscopic mechanism. This, however, remains elusive in the case of rhombohedral graphene, with the crux being its two seemingly paradoxical conditions: a pronounced small-twist-angle (θ) moiré interface, yet only when electrons are kept distant from it. Here, by scanning tunnelling microscopic imaging with both conditions fulfilled, we capture dramatic electronic structure reshaping in rhombohedral hexalayer graphene by unforeseen 'trans-moiré orbitals', which emerge on the other, distant side of the moiré interface but nevertheless enforce the moiré periodicity at all measured fillings. We visualize a hierarchy of spatially and energetically distinct trans-moiré orbitals which doped electrons must sequentially occupy--the lowest-energy orbital, expectedly responsible for the FQAHE at small fillings, carries a hollow-cage-like shape. Remarkably, these trans-moiré orbitals vanish at θ {\gtrsim} 1°, and so do QAHE plateaus in similar devices. Simulations reveal an interaction-driven charge-redistribution mechanism which shapes the trans-moiré orbitals and corresponding Chern minibands. With our findings providing the missing microscopic link, the paradoxical conditions find a natural explanation: electrons are not simply kept distant from a small-θ moiré interface; they are forced into topological trans-moiré orbitals, forged precisely under such conditions. Our microscopic diagnostics unlocks a wide range of possible 'synthetic' FQAHE platforms.

</details>


### [126] [Resonant Far-Infrared Spectroscopy of Flat-Band Fermions in Magic Angle Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12553)
*Ayelet J. Uzan-Narovlansky,Ipsita Das,Juan F. Mendez Valderrama,Yue Tang,Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman,Haoyu Hu,Pengjie Wang,Zhaoyi Joy Zheng,Haosen Guan,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,B. Andrei Bernevig,Sanfeng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用新开发的毫开尔文远红外（FIR）光学平台，首次观测到魔角扭曲双层石墨烯（MATBG）中平带电子的特征远红外共振，揭示了所谓"轻"和"重"费米子之间相互作用的强可调谐光谱特征，并通过拓扑重费米子模型解释了这些现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔工程学极大改变了扭曲二维材料的低能带、相互作用和拓扑量子态，但特征远红外波段下相互作用的莫尔带光学光谱由于极端实验挑战一直未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，实现对该波段平带电子的直接光学观测。

Method: 作者开发了一种全新的毫开尔文温度远红外测量平台；基于该平台对MATBG进行光学光谱测量，并利用拓扑重费米子模型（topological heavy-fermion model, THF）进行理论建模，揭示光学场与平带电子系统的耦合机制及光学选择定则。

Result: 观测到MATBG平带中相互作用的轻、重费米子的高度可调谐光谱特征；建立了MATBG的光学选择定则，揭示了支配轻-重费米子杂化（hybridization）的关键对称性；在电荷中性点观测到低于局域库仑能的显著共振，暗示新多体模式的涌现。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了对MATBG中光-物质相互作用的基本理解，证明了可将莫尔带共振光学光谱学扩展到毫开尔文温度，为未来研究相互作用的莫尔平带体系开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Moiré engineering in twisted two-dimensional (2D) materials radically alters low-energy bands, interactions and topological quantum states. Despite extensive studies, optical spectroscopy of interacting moiré bands in the characteristic far-infrared (FIR) regime has remained largely unexplored due to extreme experimental challenges. Using a newly developed millikelvin FIR platform, we report the observation of the long-sought-after characteristic FIR resonances of flat-band electrons in magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene (MATBG). We observe highly tunable spectroscopic signatures of interacting light and heavy fermions that constitute the flat bands in MATBG. Using the topological heavy-fermion model (THF), we show that itinerant topological electrons act as an "antenna" that couples strongly to the optical field, with resonant frequencies renormalized by the hybridization with localized heavy electrons. We establish optical selection rules of MATBG which uncovers the key symmetry governing light-heavy fermion hybridization. At charge neutrality, we observe pronounced resonances at energies below the on-site Coulomb energy, implying the emergence of new many-body modes. Our experiments and modeling provide a fundamental understanding of light-matter interactions in MATBG and enable resonant optical spectroscopy of moiré bands down to millikelvin temperatures.

</details>


### [127] [Spin Vector Control for Heisenberg-Inspired Probabilistic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12568)
*Yuanqiu Tan,Rahul Tripathi,Saleh Bunaiyan,Ryan Wagner,Neil Dilley,Kerem Camsari,Joerg Appenzeller,Zhihong Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于单层石墨烯沟道、利用双铁磁自旋注入实现实空间矢量求和的自旋电子学平台，通过独立控制注入电流连续调控自旋累积矢量的幅度与方向，为基于二维材料的概率性自旋电路和经典海森堡模型的硬件映射奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率位（p-bits）是概率计算的核心，但超越二元概率位的关键挑战在于实现和操控矢量自旋信息，这对于映射诸如海森堡哈密顿量（Heisenberg Hamiltonian）等复杂基于能量的模型至关重要。

Method: 采用双铁磁自旋注入到单层石墨烯沟道的自旋电子学平台，通过独立控制的注入电流电学调控自旋极化，实现对所产生的自旋累积矢量幅度与方向的连续控制；同时借助理论矢量求和模型和自旋电路（spin-circuit）模拟加以验证。

Result: 实验观测揭示了相干矢量相互作用和自旋状态的角向可调性，实现了基于矢量的自旋逻辑的直接实现，并为利用随机低势垒磁体映射经典海森堡模型铺平了道路。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于二维材料实现概率性自旋电路的可扩展路径，为低功耗、非布尔计算架构提供了新的机遇。

Abstract: Probabilistic bits (p-bits) have emerged as a cornerstone of probabilistic computing, enabling energy-efficient hardware implementation for probabilistic inference and combinatorial optimization. A critical challenge in advancing this field beyond binary p-bits lies in realizing and manipulating vector spin information, essential for mapping complex energy-based models such as the Heisenberg Hamiltonian.Here, we demonstrate a spintronic platform capable of real-space vector summation by using dual ferromagnetic spin injections into a monolayer graphene channel. By electrically tuning the spin polarization through independently controlled injection currents, we achieve continuous control over the magnitude and direction of the resulting spin accumulation vector. Experimental observations, supported by theoretical vector summation models and spin-circuit simulations, reveal coherent vector interactions and angular tunability of the spin state. This approach enables direct implementation of vector-based spin logic and lays the groundwork for mapping classical Heisenberg models using stochastic low-barrier magnets. Our results establish a scalable pathway for realizing probabilistic spin circuits based on two-dimensional materials, offering new opportunities for low-power, non-Boolean computing architectures.

</details>


### [128] [Thermal Hall tomography of chiral superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12586)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出通过低温热霍尔电导（thermal Hall conductance）直接读出手性超导体中的Bogoliubov–de Gennes Chern数，避免了对复杂正常态逐口袋重构的困难。对菱方石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）材料，作者展示了该平台数值Chern数在525个参数点下保持不变，并给出了Bogoliubov费米面破坏量子化的判据，热测量所需的热量计分辨率在毫开尔文温度下已能满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性超导体（chiral superconductor）边缘携带手性马约拉纳模（chiral Majorana modes），其数量由单个整数——Bogoliubov–de Gennes（BdG）Chern数决定，但三十年来候选材料均未能直接测量该整数，因为常用的磁学信号不受拓扑保护。菱方石墨烯的发现使这一问题既紧迫又可能被解答：其磁成像可分辨超导相内可重写的破时间反演畴，而量子振荡揭示的正常态过于复杂，无法逐口袋重建。

Method: 作者证明了低温热霍尔电导可直接返回该整数，无需逐口袋重建。对于带投影配对（band-projected pairing），该电导等于被动量空间占据区域所包围的配对涡旋缠绕数（pairing-vortex winding）。将带内哈密顿量分解为对称与反对称部分，分离出真实材料中的三角翘曲（trigonal warping）和有限库珀对动量（Cooper pair momentum），其中反对称部分在拓扑上是惰性的。

Result: 对525个参数点的直接Chern数计算表明拓扑不变量保持不变；一个不等式刻画了Bogoliubov费米面（Bogoliubov Fermi surface）出现并破坏量子化的边界。平台公式κ_{xy}/T=(π²k_B²/6h)C_BdG可读出该整数，其符号随磁成像观察到的畴改变而反转，写入的畴壁应携带2|C_BdG|条马约拉纳通道。所需的温度测量分辨率在毫开尔文温度下封装石墨烯中已经能够分辨单个热量子。

Conclusion: 热霍尔测量为读取手性超导体中的BdG Chern数提供了直接且拓扑稳健的方法，克服了磁学信号不受拓扑保护的根本缺陷。菱方石墨烯成为首个有望实现该整数直接测量的候选体系，其平台值与磁成像畴结构及畴壁马约拉纳通道数形成自洽的完整验证链条。

Abstract: A chiral superconductor carries chiral Majorana modes along its edges, and a single integer, the Bogoliubov--de Gennes Chern number, counts them. Thirty years of candidate materials have not yielded a measurement of that integer, because the magnetic signatures usually invoked are not topologically protected. Rhombohedral graphene makes the question both urgent and answerable: magnetic imaging resolves rewritable time-reversal-breaking domains inside the superconducting phase, while quantum oscillations reveal a normal state too intricate to reconstruct pocket by pocket. We show that the low-temperature thermal Hall conductance returns the integer directly, with no such reconstruction. For band-projected pairing it equals the pairing-vortex winding enclosed by the occupied regions of momentum space. Splitting the intravalley Hamiltonian into symmetric and antisymmetric parts isolates the trigonal warping and finite Cooper pair momentum of the real material: the antisymmetric part is topologically inert, direct Chern calculations across $525$ parameter points show the invariant preserved, and one inequality marks where a Bogoliubov Fermi surface removes quantization. The plateau $κ_{xy}/T=(π^2k_B^2/6h)\,C_{\rm BdG}$ then reads out the integer, its sign reverses with the imaged domain, a written domain wall should carry $2|C_{\rm BdG}|$ Majorana channels, and the thermometry required already resolves single thermal quanta in encapsulated graphene at millikelvin temperatures.

</details>


### [129] [Gate Control of g-factor in Germanium Quantum Dots: A Strain-Based Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12718)
*Mu Niu,Adrian Culver,Johnathan Bryan,Chris Anderson,Mark Gyure,HongWen Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于应变诱导的g张量（g-tensor）调制，定量解释了锗量子点（germanium quantum dots）中由小幅度栅极电压变化引起的巨大g因子变化，建立了几何应变、静电控制和自旋量子比特（spin qubit）性能之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体自旋量子比特的行为由g因子直接决定，它同时影响量子比特频率及其对电噪声和磁噪声的敏感度。锗量子点的最新实验表明，当栅极电压有微小变化时会出现巨大g因子变化，表明静电学与自旋特性之间存在强耦合，然而该现象尚缺乏定量解释。

Method: 研究结合了非均匀应变的有限元模拟（finite-element simulation）与空穴波函数的量子学计算（quantum calculation of hole wavefunctions）。通过这两种方法的联合使用来量化器件诱导应变如何产生空间可变的g张量，并进一步分析栅极电压如何移动量子点在g张量景观中的位置。

Result: 研究表明器件诱导的应变会产生空间变化的g张量，而当栅极电压使量子点在该g张量景观中移动时，将导致有效g因子的显著改变。该结果可能解释实验中观察到的g因子可调性，并强调了面内（in-plane）g张量变化的重要性。

Conclusion: 这项工作在锗自旋量子比特中建立了应变、静电控制和量子比特性能之间的直接关联，为理解应变与g因子耦合的物理机制提供了定量框架。

Abstract: The g-factor is a key parameter governing the behavior of semiconductor spin qubits, as it directly determines the qubit frequency and its sensitivity to electrical and magnetic noise. Recent experiments in germanium quantum dots have revealed large g-factor variations under small gate voltage changes, indicating a strong coupling between electrostatics and spin properties. Here, we present a quantitative explanation based on strain-induced g-tensor modulation. By combining finite-element simulations of inhomogeneous strain with quantum calculations of hole wavefunctions, we show that device-induced strain produces spatially varying g-tensors. Gate voltages shift the quantum dot within this landscape, leading to substantial changes in the effective g-factor. Our results may account for the experimentally observed tunability and highlight the importance of in-plane g-tensor variations. This work establishes a direct link between strain, electrostatic control, and qubit performance in germanium spin qubits.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum Divergence and Topological Edge Diagnostics via Levitov Full Counting Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12726)
*Maolin Bo,Xiang Chen,Siyu Liu,Han Lu,Yunhu Zhu,Zhongkai Huang,Chuang Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出用于介观输运的微分全计数统计（differential full counting statistics）方案，通过比较不同栅极构型在匹配k1下的端子法诺因子（Fano factor）和噪声累积量，避免了从单一绝对值F推断体发散传感器的缺陷；并通过二维晶格非平衡格林函数计算和低温量子点接触校准进行了分析验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过单一绝对值F推断体发散传感器存在局限性，需要更可靠的协议来研究介观输运中的噪声特性。

Method: 提出一种微分全计数统计方案，在匹配k1条件下比较不同栅极构型之间的端子法诺因子和噪声累积量；通过零温度几何扫描对双通道因子化进行解析演示，并利用二维晶格非平衡格林函数（non-equilibrium Green's function）计算及Kumar低温量子点接触（quantum point contact）校准作为次级基准测试。

Result: 二维晶格非平衡格林函数计算得到次泊松（sub Poissonian）法诺因子，Kumar低温量子点接触校准验证了数值实现的正确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的微分统计协议能够更有效地比较不同构型下的输运噪声特性，并为介观输运的体发散诊断提供了更稳健的分析框架。

Abstract: We propose a differential full counting statistics protocol for mesoscopic transport. Additionally, we compare terminal Fano factors and noise cumulants between gate configurations at matched k1, instead of inferring a bulk divergence sensor from a single absolute F. it is illustrated analytically for a two channel factorization via a zero temperature geometry scan. Secondary benchmarks show that a two dimensional lattice non equilibrium Greens function calculation yields sub Poissonian Fano factors, whereas Kumars low temperature quantum point contact calibration validates the numerical implementation.

</details>


### [131] [The principle of stationary action and Lagrangian for dissipative dynamics with velocity-proportional frictional force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13413)
*Georgii Koniukov,Dmitry Nerukh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分数阶微积分（fractional calculus）的方法，用于推导耗散线性动力学系统（dissipative linear dynamical systems）的运动方程，解决了经典最小作用量原理（principle of stationary action）无法直接处理含速度项方程的长期问题，并给出了欧拉-拉格朗日方程（Euler-Lagrange equation）、哈密顿方程（Hamilton equations）、能量变化及能耗的几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，常系数耗散线性动力学系统的运动方程无法从经典最小作用量原理推导，因为方程中与速度成正比的项导致拉格朗日量中出现1/2阶时间导数。已有的分数阶方法在数学和物理层面均存在缺陷，因此需要提出更完善的方法。

Method: 作者呈现了自己的分数阶微积分方法，该方法能够正确推导出欧拉-拉格朗日方程，并进一步得到哈密顿方程，同时推导出运动物体的能量变化表达式，并尝试对能量耗散过程进行几何解释。

Result: 该方法成功获得正确的欧拉-拉格朗日方程和哈密顿方程，给出了运动物体能量变化的表达式，并提出了一种关于能量如何耗散的几何解释。

Conclusion: 所提出的分数阶微积分方法克服了已有分数阶方法的数学与物理缺陷，为耗散动力学系统的变分原理推导提供了可行的理论框架，并初步建立了能量耗散的几何图像。

Abstract: It has been known for a long time that the equation of motion for dissipative linear dynamical systems with constant coefficients cannot be derived from the classical principle of stationary action because the term proportional to velocity in the equation of motion leads to the time derivative of order one half in the Lagrangian. Thus, approaches utilising fractional calculus have been used; however, they suffer from deficiencies both from mathematical and physical points of view. We here present our version of such fractional calculus based approach that provides correct Euler-Lagrange and, ultimately, the Hamilton equations, energy change of the moving body, and an attempt for a geometric interpretation of how energy dissipates.

</details>


### [132] [Observation of Time-Domain Braiding of Non-Abelian Anyons at $ν= 5/2$ State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12897)
*Tomer Alkalay,Jinhong Park,Minseong Oh,Changki Hong,Jun-Young M. Lee,Priya Tiwari,Tapas Senapati,Vladimir Umansky,Moty Heiblum,H. -S. Sim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本论文通过测量ν=5/2分数量子霍尔态中下游带电模式和上游中性模的分配噪声，报道了非阿贝尔任意子（non-Abelian anyons）的"时域编织"（time-domain braiding）特征，为粒子-空穴Pfaffian拓扑序中非阿贝尔任意子的存在提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与服从玻色子或费米子交换统计的基本粒子不同，任意子（anyons）被预测展现出阿贝尔或非阿贝尔编织统计。其中，非阿贝尔编织（non-Abelian braiding）可在简并态子空间内实现态的幺正变换，但到目前为止实验证据仍然难以获得，因此寻找非阿贝尔任意子的实验证据是该领域的核心目标。

Method: 作者将先前在ν=1/3阿贝尔任意子中验证的方法扩展到ν=5/2分数量子霍尔态，基于高稀释一维边缘模式的弱分割产生的电流涨落进行测量。实验独立探测了下游带电模式的分割噪声以及上游中性模的分割噪声，并将这些独立测量结果与"时域编织"的理论预测进行比较。

Result: 下游阿贝尔任意子和上游非阿贝尔任意子的独立测量结果均与"粒子-空穴Pfaffian"（particle-hole Pfaffian）拓扑序中"时域编织"的理论预测一致，为ν=5/2态中非阿贝尔任意子的存在提供了实验证据。

Conclusion: 这些测量结果共同为ν=5/2分数量子霍尔态中存在非阿贝尔任意子提供了证据，也表明"时域编织"方法可成为探测非阿贝尔拓扑序的有效实验手段。

Abstract: Unlike elementary particles, which obey either bosonic or fermionic exchange statistics, certain quasiparticles, known as anyons, are predicted to exhibit Abelian or non-Abelian braiding statistics. While braiding Abelian anyons modifies the wavefunction by a 'statistical phase', braiding non-Abelian anyons implements a unitary transformation of the state within a degenerate subspace of states. Experimental evidence of non-Abelian braiding has thus far remained elusive. Here, we report a 'time-domain braiding' signature of non-Abelian anyons in the $ν= 5/2$ fractional quantum Hall state, by extending our previously demonstrated approach with Abelian anyons at $ν= 1/3$. Our approach is based on measurements of the current fluctuations arising from weak partitioning of a highly dilute one-dimensional edge mode. We independently probe the partition noise of the downstream charged mode and also that of the upstream neutral mode. These independent measurements agree with our theoretical predictions for 'time-domain braiding' of the downstream Abelian and the upstream non-Abelian anyons, respectively, in the 'particle-hole Pfaffian' topological order. Together, these results provide evidence for the presence of non-Abelian anyons.

</details>


### [133] [Acoustic Tweezers for Magnetic Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13055)
*Chongzhou Wang,Weichao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种“声学镊子（acoustic tweezer）”，利用空间扩展声束实现单个磁斯格明子（skyrmion）的确定性捕获与路由操控。研究发现空间受限纵波携带非平凡声子自旋（phonon spin），产生手性锁定的磁弹性场，进而诱导极性选择性辐射力，将斯格明子吸引至局部声子自旋极大值处。正交声束相交可构建可重构吸引点，实现绝热、确定性操控，为非破坏性、片上高精度拓扑自旋电子学（topological spintronics）提供新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驱动磁斯格明子的方法主要将整个系综（ensemble）作为一个整体平移，缺乏对单个粒子的选择性操控能力，这是本文拟解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出“声学镊子”方案，利用空间扩展声束对单个斯格明子进行确定性捕获与路由。方法依托空间受限纵波携带的非平凡声子自旋，其产生手性锁定的磁弹性场（magnetoelastic field），进而形成不同于保守梯度力（conservative gradient forces）的极性选择性辐射力（polarity-selective radiation forces），将斯格明子吸引至局部声子自旋极大值；通过正交声束相交构建可重构吸引点，实现绝热操控。

Result: 揭示了空间受限纵波中存在非平凡声子自旋及其与斯格明子极性锁定的磁弹性手性耦合机制，实现了单个斯格明子的极性选择性辐射力捕获，并验证了正交声束构成的交叉吸引点可对斯格明子进行可重构、确定性的绝热操控。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种“全局场–局部相互作用”（global-field-local-interaction）范式，为高精度、非破坏性、片上集成的拓扑自旋电子学操控提供了新的可行路线。

Abstract: Current methods for driving magnetic skyrmions predominantly translate ensembles as a whole, lacking single-particle selectivity. Here, we propose an "acoustic tweezer" that deterministically traps and routes individual skyrmions using spatially extended acoustic beams. We reveal that spatially confined longitudinal waves carry nontrivial phonon spin, inducing a magnetoelastic field whose chirality is locked to the acoustic spin texture. This generates polarity-selective radiation forces, distinct from conservative gradient forces, that attract skyrmions to local phonon spin maxima. Intersecting orthogonal beams create reconfigurable attractive points for adiabatic, deterministic manipulation. Our global-field-local-interaction paradigm establishes a non-destructive, on-chip route for high-precision topological spintronics.

</details>


### [134] [Emergence of moiré magnetic chaos in twisted bilayer CrI3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13062)
*Gyuyoung Park,OukJae Lee,Kyoung-Min Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在无外部驱动的扭转双层CrI₃中发现了新型磁混沌——莫尔磁混沌（moiré magnetic chaos），它由中间尺度磁畴变量产生，通过层间交换阻挫稳定，表现出对初始微扰的极端敏感性、有限时间李雅普诺夫指数显著，且最终磁畴构型具有随机性和两两不相关性，将非线性磁性扩展至传统驱动范式之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁混沌研究集中于外部驱动下的宏观变量，此前的框架无法涵盖无驱动条件下介观尺度磁畴动力学中可能存在的自发性混沌行为，有必要探索新的混沌类型以扩展非线性磁性研究的边界。

Method: 采用微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation）研究扭转双层CrI₃在无外部驱动条件下向莫尔磁织构弛豫的过程，通过计算有限时间李雅普诺夫指数（finite-time Lyapunov exponents）定量表征动力学敏感度，并结合统计分析评估最终磁畴构型的随机性与相关性。

Result: 弛豫过程对初始状态的微小局域扰动极为敏感，有限时间李雅普诺夫指数显著；初始扰动幅度跨越五个数量级时仍保持最终态敏感性；统计结果表明所得磁畴构型呈随机且两两不相关的分布。

Conclusion: 研究确认了扭转磁体中一种新的微观无驱动混沌形式，表明层间交换阻挫提供的多重动力学自由度足以支撑自发混沌，从而将非线性磁性推广至传统外部驱动框架之外。

Abstract: The study of magnetic chaos has traditionally focused on macroscopic variables under external driving. Here we demonstrate a new type of magnetic chaos, termed moiré magnetic chaos, associated with mesoscopic magnetic domain variables in twisted bilayer CrI3 without external driving. The domains are stabilized by a characteristic interlayer exchange frustration, which supplies the multiple dynamical degrees of freedom required for autonomous chaos. Through micromagnetic simulations, we show that relaxation toward moiré magnetic textures is extremely sensitive to minute local perturbations of the initial state, characterized by substantial finite-time Lyapunov exponents and a final-state sensitivity that persists over five decades of perturbation amplitude. Statistical analysis further reveals that the resulting domain configurations are stochastic and pairwise uncorrelated. Our results identify a form of microscopic, undriven chaos in twisted magnets that extends nonlinear magnetism beyond the conventional driven regime.

</details>


### [135] [Topological Superconductors in Doubly-Coupled Nanowires with Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13265)
*Hongfa Pan,Haoyang Wang,Wenguang Zhu,Zhenhua Qiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了在双重耦合纳米线（doubly coupled nanowires）中通过邻近效应感应超导性（proximity-induced superconductivity）与交变磁性（altermagnetism）相结合来构建拓扑超导电性（topological superconductivity）的可能性，发现四种不同的拓扑超导相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在双重耦合纳米线体系中设计拓扑超导电性的可行性，利用交变磁性诱导拓扑超导电性，并寻找更多可实验调控的参数来实现对拓扑性质的灵活控制。

Method: 通过理论分析，在双重耦合纳米线体系中引入邻近效应超导性与交变磁性，系统调节实验上可访问的参数，并对所获得的拓扑超导相进行分类和对称性分析。

Result: 发现四种不同的拓扑超导相：称其为D类（class D）、BDI类（class BDI）以及两种每端承载两个马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero modes）的相，分别由自旋群对称性（spin-group symmetry）和磁性点群对称性（magnetic point-group symmetry）所保护。

Conclusion: 该系统不仅继承了交变磁性诱导拓扑超导电性的优点，还提供了多个调控旋钮（如超导相位差和线间耦合）来控制拓扑性质，为实验实现和调控拓扑超导态提供了更多自由度。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the possibility of engineering topological superconductivity in doubly coupled nanowires integrating proximity-induced superconductivity and altermagnetism. By tuning experimentally accessible parameters, we find four distinct topological superconducting phases: class D, class BDI, and two phases hosting two Majorana zero modes per end, protected respectively by spin-group and magnetic point-group symmetries. Beyond inheriting the advantages of alternating?magnetism induced topological superconductivity, our system provides multiple tuning knobs (e.g., superconducting phase difference and inter-wire coupling) to control topological properties.

</details>


### [136] [Inductively-protected Andreev (IPA) spin qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13530)
*J. L. del Olmo N.,F. J. Matute-Cañadas,A. Levy Yeyati,R. Seoane Souto,R. Aguado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"电感保护的安德烈夫自旋量子比特"（inductively protected Andreev spin qubit, IPA），通过在安德烈夫自旋量子比特（ASQ）上并联线性电感，使自旋量子比特态在相空间中分离到不同势阱，几乎消除波函数重叠，从而延长弛豫时间，兼具受保护超导量子比特的长相干时间、低频基态流形和大非谐性，以及自旋自由度的操作优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安德烈夫自旋量子比特基于量子点约瑟夫森结中准粒子的自旋，但其弛豫时间受限，需要一种方法来增强其相干性能而不牺牲自旋自由度的操作优势。

Method: 对安德烈夫自旋量子比特并联一个线性电感（linear inductor），通过将自旋量子比特态分离到相空间中不同的势阱来几乎消除波函数重叠，从而增强弛豫时间。该方案被证明等价于两个处于重流区（heavy regime）的通量量子比特（fluxonium），每个自旋对应一个。

Result: 并联电感后形成的电感保护安德烈夫自旋量子比特（IPA qubit）显著延长了弛豫时间，并同时具备受保护超导量子比特的长相干时间、低频基态流形和大非谐性，以及自旋自由度的操作优势。

Conclusion: 与线性电感并联的安德烈夫自旋量子比特形成了一种双通量（fluxonium）结构，实现了电感保护的自旋量子比特，在相干时间与操作便利性之间达到理想结合。

Abstract: The spin of a quasiparticle trapped in a quantum dot Josephson junction forms the basis of an Andreev spin qubit (ASQ): a semiconductor-superconductor device where the interplay between a localized spin degree of freedom and superconductivity leads to a spin-resolved Josephson potential. In this work, we show that shunting an ASQ with a linear inductor enhances its relaxation time by separating the spin-qubit states into distinct potential wells in phase space, nearly eliminating wavefunction overlap. The resulting inductively protected Andreev (IPA) spin qubit is equivalent to two fluxoniums in the heavy regime, one for each spin. Thus, the IPA qubit combines the long coherence times, low-frequency ground-state manifold, and large anharmonicity of a protected superconducting qubit with the operational advantages of a spin degree of freedom.

</details>

### [137] [Singlet-Doublet fermion origin of dark matter, neutrino mass and inverse first-order electroweak phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12483)
*Debasish Borah,Indrajit Saha,Sujit Kumar Sahoo,Narendra Sahu,Shashwat Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在苏格兰型（scotogenic-type）辐射中微子质量模型中研究了逆一阶电弱相变（inverse first-order electroweak phase transition, IFOEWPT）及其产生的可观测引力波（gravitational waves, GW）。模型引入两代SU(2)_L单态和双重态费米子、一个单态标量及三个右手中微子（right-handed neutrinos, RHNs），其中较轻的单态-双重态（singlet-doublet, SD）费米子中性组分为暗物质（dark matter, DM）候选者，较重的SD费米子与希格斯强耦合从而触发IFOEWPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探索一个能够同时解释中微子质量、暗物质和宇宙一阶相变（first-order phase transition）的统一框架，并评估该模型中由IFOEWPT产生的引力波信号在现有及未来实验中的可探测性。通过将SD费米子与希格斯耦合，模型可实现宇宙从对称相到最终破缺希格斯相经历两次一阶相变的过程。

Method: 模型以标准模型（standard model, SM）为基础，扩展了两代SU(2)_L单态和双重态费米子、一个单态标量以及三个右手中微子。SD费米子与单态标量在单圈层次产生狄拉克（Dirac）中微子质量，轻的SD费米子中性分量由残留Z_2对称性稳定后充当暗物质。作者进一步从正确中微子质量、暗物质遗迹丰度和IFOEWPT三方面约束模型参数空间，并纳入现有实验限制。

Result: 模型最终确定了一个满足所有约束条件的允许参数空间。该参数空间可被对撞机、暗物质直接探测（direct-detection）、引力波以及宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）实验在近期内探测到。

Conclusion: 作者认为该模型提供了一个能够在同一框架内解释中微子质量、暗物质及IFOEWPT的可行方案，其允许参数空间对未来多重实验（包括引力波与CMB观测）具有明确的可检验预言。

Abstract: We study the possibility of an inverse first-order electroweak phase transition (IFOEWPT) and observable gravitational waves (GW) in a radiative neutrino mass model of scotogenic type where singlet-doublet (SD) fermions, the lightest of whom is the dark matter (DM) candidate, generate the necessary seesaw at one-loop level. Considering the possibility of light neutrinos being Dirac for simplicity and additional detection prospects, we extend the standard model (SM) with two generations of $SU(2)_L$ singlet and doublet fermions, one singlet scalar, and three right-handed neutrinos (RHNs). While RHNs provide the right chiral parts of light Dirac neutrinos, the SD fermions and the scalar singlet facilitate the one-loop neutrino mass diagram. The neutral component of the lighter SD fermion, stabilized under a residual $Z_2$ symmetry plays the role of DM while the heavier SD fermions strongly couple to the Higgs leading to an IFOEWPT where the Universe undergoes two different first-order phase transition as it goes from the symmetric to the final broken Higgs phase. We constrain the parameter space from the requirements of generating the correct neutrino mass, DM relic as well as IFOEWPT while incorporating the existing constraints from different experiments. The final allowed parameter space of the model can be probed at collider, direct-detection, GW and cosmic microwave background (CMB) experiments in near future.

</details>


### [138] [Newtrinos.jl: A Julia Package for Global Analysis of Neutrino Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12940)
*Philipp Eller,David Schultheiß*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Newtrinos.jl 是一个开源的 Julia 软件包，专用于中微子（neutrino）数据的全局分析。它采用模块化三层架构，将物理模型、实验描述和统计推断分离，支持灵活组合实验与理论模型，且所有模型均可自动微分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子数据的全局分析需要整合多个实验数据集和理论模型，传统工具难以灵活组合、扩展和维护。作者旨在提供一个模块化、可组合的开源框架，使研究者能够自由搭配实验与模型，并支持现代统计推断工作流。

Method: 采用三层模块化架构（three-layer architecture），分别承载物理模型、实验描述与统计推断。每个实验数据集均实现了包含全部相关系统不确定性的统计前向模型（statistical forward model），同时定义似然函数（likelihood）与数据生成过程（data-generating process）。物理参数与干扰参数（nuisance parameters）可在实验间合并、关联或去关联。支持频率学派（Frequentist）与贝叶斯（Bayesian）推断，并可在 CPU 线程或分布式工作节点上并行计算。由于完全基于 Julia 编写，所有模型均可自动微分（automatic differentiation）。

Result: 该框架具有可组合性（composable），能够便捷地构建跨多个数据集的联合似然（joint likelihood）。新实验与物理模型可在不修改核心代码的情况下添加，且支持精确梯度计算。

Conclusion: Newtrinos.jl 为全球中微子数据分析提供了一个灵活、可扩展且具备现代统计推断能力的高性能开源工具。

Abstract: Newtrinos.jl is an open-source Julia package for performing global analyses of neutrino data. It provides a modular, three-layer architecture that separates physics models, experiment descriptions, and statistical inference into independent modules. This allows researchers to freely combine experiments and test them against a variety of theoretical models. New experiments and physics models can be added without modifying core code. Full statistical forward models including all relevant systematic uncertainties are implemented for each experimental dataset, defining both the likelihood and the data-generating process and enabling modern statistical inference workflows. The framework is composable, making it straightforward to construct joint likelihoods over multiple datasets. Physics and nuisance parameters can be merged, correlated, or decorrelated across experiments to ensure consistency in the joint fit. The package supports Frequentist and Bayesian inference and is parallelizable across CPU threads or distributed workers. Written entirely in Julia, all models are automatically differentiable, enabling exact gradient computation.

</details>


### [139] [Non-perturbative quark production and transport in the evolving glasma: the WAGASHI event generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13393)
*Nicholas J. Benoit,Chiho Nonaka,Hidetoshi Taya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本论文提出一种名为WAGASHI的新模型，结合经典Yang-Mills glasma演化、Wong方程输运和Schwinger对产生，研究了相对论重离子碰撞早期glasma阶段的非微扰夸克-反夸克对产生及其后续动力学。研究发现此阶段可产生与最终强子产额相当的大量夸克，并伴随显著的动量展宽和自旋随机化，提示对夸克-胶子等离子体早期热化有重要贡献，同时给出了LHC能量下Pb-Pb和O-O碰撞中重子数、电荷、奇异数和自旋极化的逐事件分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论重离子碰撞中，夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）的早期热化机制尚不清楚，尤其是glasma阶段非微扰夸克对产生的贡献及其对后续流体动力学演化的影响需要定量研究。

Method: 开发了新的WAGASHI模型，该模型将经典Yang-Mills glasma演化、Wong方程输运以及Schwinger对产生机制以逐事件（event-by-event）方式耦合，用于模拟夸克产生及其后续动力学。

Result: 在glasma阶段即可产生数量可观且与最终强子产额相当的夸克，这些夸克经历显著的动量展宽和自旋随机化；同时获得了LHC能量下Pb-Pb和O-O碰撞中重子数、电荷、奇异数和自旋极化的逐事件分布，其中较小的O-O体系显示特别大的涨落。

Conclusion: glasma阶段的夸克对产生及其动力学可能对夸克-胶子等离子体的早期热化产生显著贡献，该模型提供的逐事件动力学初值可用于后续的流体动力学（hydrodynamic）演化模拟。

Abstract: We study non-perturbative quark-antiquark pair production and the subsequent quark dynamics in the earliest glasma stage of relativistic heavy-ion collisions. For that, we develop a new model, WAGASHI (Wong-precessing Anisotropic Glasma And ScHwinger-produced Initial-conditions), which combines classical Yang-Mills glasma evolution, Wong-equation transport, and Schwinger pair production on an event-by-event basis. We find that a sizeable number of quarks, comparable to the final hadron yields, are produced already during the glasma stage and subsequently undergo substantial momentum broadening and spin randomization, suggesting a significant contribution toward the early equilibration of the quark-gluon plasma. We also determine the event-by-event distributions of baryon number, electric charge, strangeness, and spin polarization in Pb-Pb and O-O collisions at LHC energies, finding particularly large fluctuations in the smaller O-O system. These results provide dynamical initial conditions for the subsequent hydrodynamic evolution of the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [140] [Massive cold hybrid stars in a modified Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12653)
*Sk Md Adil Imam,Pedro Costa,Mariana Dutra,Odilon Lourenço,Renan Pereira,Constança Providência*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个修正的多项式圈-南布–约纳-拉西尼奥（mPNJL）模型，其中Polyakov势显式依赖夸克化学势，使其在零温下保持有限，从而能描述冷致密物质中的禁闭-退禁闭相变。通过与强子状态方程进行麦克斯韦（Maxwell）构造，该模型可产生从强子物质到禁闭（quarkyonic）夸克物质再到退禁闭夸克物质的两次相变，或直接从强子到退禁闭夸克物质（或quarkyonic夸克物质）的一次相变。研究系统考察了Polyakov势参数和矢量相互作用对相变位置的调控作用，发现排斥性矢量相互作用对获得稳定的夸克核心至关重要。具有quarkyonic和/或退禁闭核心的混合星最大质量可超过2倍太阳质量，且最大质量构型的核心声速可超过共形极限（c_s²=1/3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷致密物质中的禁闭-退禁闭相变是核天体物理和量子色动力学（QCD）相图研究的重要课题，但传统Polyakov圈模型在零温下无法直接描述该相变。本文旨在构建一个能在零温下处理相变的理论框架，并阐明各模型参数对混合星结构（hybrid star structure）的影响。

Method: 采用修正的Polyakov-loop Nambu–Jona-Lasinio（mPNJL）模型，将Polyakov势表达为对夸克化学势的显式依赖；将该修正的夸克扇区与强子状态方程通过麦克斯韦（Maxwell）构造结合，形成统一的物态方程；系统扫描模型参数并计算对应的混合星质量-半径关系及声速等结构性质。

Result: 模型根据参数选取可产生两种相变或一种相变的不同路径；获得了仅含quarkyonic和/或退禁闭夸克相的稳定大质量冷混合星；排斥性矢量相互作用是获得稳定夸克核心的必要条件；在低密度区采用足够刚硬的强子物态方程时，混合星最大质量可超过2倍太阳质量；最大质量构型中quarkyonic星核心的声速超过共形极限c_s²=1/3。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了每个模型参数在塑造混合星结构中的定性作用，为理解冷致密夸克物质中的相变行为及高质量混合星的存在条件提供了系统性的理论框架。

Abstract: We propose a modified Polyakov-loop Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (mPNJL) model in which the Polyakov potential is given by an explicit dependence on the quark chemical potential, allowing it to remain finite at zero temperature and thus to describe the confinement-deconfinement transition in cold dense matter. Combining this modified quark sector with hadronic equations of state via a Maxwell construction, we find that, depending on the model parameters, the equation of state can exhibit either two phase transitions, from hadronic matter to confined (quarkyonic) quark matter and subsequently to deconfined quark matter, or a single transition directly from hadronic to deconfined quark matter or from hadronic to quarkyonic quark matter. Stable massive cold hybrid stars with only quarkyonic and/or deconfined quark phase are obtained. We systematically examine how the parameters of the modified Polyakov potential and the quark vector interactions control the location of these transitions, and find that repulsive vector interactions are essential to obtain a stable quark core. Hybrid stars with quarkyonic and/or a deconfined core can reach maximum masses above $2M_\odot$, provided a sufficiently stiff hadronic equation of state is used at low density. In the core of the maximum-mass configurations, the speed of sound exceeds the conformal limit, $c_s^2 = 1/3$, for the quarkyonic core stars. This work establishes the qualitative role of each model parameter in shaping hybrid-star structure.

</details>


### [141] [Scalar induced gravitational waves as probes of dark QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12706)
*Wan-Zhe Feng,Ao Li,Jing-Zhi Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由标量扰动诱导的引力波（SIGW）作为探测暗QCD交叉相变（dark QCD crossover）的探针，证明暗QCD交叉在其特征尺度附近产生额外频移的畸变信号，可成为隐藏禁闭扇区的独特宇宙学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受孪生希格斯（twin Higgs）和不对称孪生重子暗物质（asymmetric twin baryon dark matter）等模型的启发，暗QCD扇区是理论上备受关注的隐藏禁闭扇区候选，但目前缺乏有效的宇宙学探测手段。本文旨在探索SIGW能否为这类隐藏扇区提供互补性的宇宙学观测信号。

Method: 作者构建了标准模型（Standard Model）叠加不同暗QCD扇区（含轻味或全六味暗夸克）的有效能量和熵自由度，利用由此得到的物态方程参数（equation of state parameter）和声速求解一阶标量扰动及二阶SIGW在SM和暗QCD交叉时期的演化，并采用单色原初曲率功率谱进行分析。

Result: 真实SM热历史会使SIGW谱相对理想辐射主导情形产生修正；暗QCD交叉则在增强标量模在暗禁闭尺度附近重新入视界时产生额外的频移畸变，且该特征相对SM QCD印记偏移至更高频率。

Conclusion: SIGW可作为隐藏禁闭扇区（hidden confining sectors）的互补宇宙学探针；该分析方法可推广至其他包含不同暗禁闭扇区的理论模型中。

Abstract: We investigate scalar induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) as probes of a dark QCD crossover. Motivated by twin Higgs and asymmetric twin baryon dark matter scenarios, we consider a dark QCD sector with a confinement scale approximately 5.5 times the Standard Model (SM) QCD scale. We construct the effective energy and entropy degrees of freedom for the SM supplemented by dark QCD sectors containing either three light dark quark flavors or all six dark quark flavors. The resulting equation of state parameter and sound speed are then used to solve the first-order scalar perturbations and the second-order SIGWs through the SM and dark QCD crossover epochs. For a monochromatic primordial curvature power spectrum, we first demonstrate that the realistic SM thermal history modifies the SIGW spectrum relative to the idealized radiation-dominated case. We then show that a dark QCD crossover generates an additional frequency-shifted distortion when the enhanced scalar mode reenters the horizon near the dark confinement scale. This distinctive feature can therefore serve as a characteristic signature of the dark QCD sector. Our results demonstrate that SIGWs provide a complementary cosmological probe of hidden confining sectors, with characteristic spectral features shifted to higher frequencies relative to the SM QCD imprint. The analysis developed in this work can also be extended to other well-motivated theories containing different dark confining sectors.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Dynamical uncertainty geometry for nonlinear transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12521)
*Giacomo Tommei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出动态不确定性几何（Dynamical Uncertainty Geometry, DUG）框架，将后验不确定性、未来观测与模型诱导的输运（transport）三个常被混淆的要素分离，通过共同的封闭概率坐标对后验可信集与输运条件子集进行索引，并利用质量秩化可信滤过、Fisher几何与有标输运骨架来追踪不同可信度水平下的物理上有意义的类别。作者建立了在密度与概率测度同时扰动下持续模块（persistence modules）的稳定性结果，并通过短弧轨道确定与地月平面圆型限制性三体问题两个基准问题验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力学系统中的不确定性往往受输运结构的组织，而这些结构无法仅由后验几何（posterior geometry）显式呈现。现有方法常将后验不确定性、未来观测和模型诱导输运三个概念混为一谈，且仅在单一阈值下考察可信集，难以追踪不同可信度水平下物理意义不同的结果类别。

Method: DUG框架由三部分组成：质量秩化可信滤过（mass-ranked credible filtration）描述后验几何；未来实验通过其诱导概率定律与Fisher几何（Fisher geometry）加以刻画；带有标签的输运骨架（labelled transport skeleton）表示动力学上不同的结果。这些组件由共同的封闭概率坐标索引，并在此基础上构建持续模块，同时建立连续、细化及有限原子三种表述下进行持久性（persistence）的稳定性分析。

Result: 在短弧轨道确定问题中，DUG在一个连通的不确定区域内识别出动力学上不同的回返类别，并揭示基于信息量与基于局部Fisher的观测设计准则之间的差异。在地月平面圆型限制性三体问题中，DUG揭示了连通可信区域内并存的多个首次命中输运结果，并提供了评估其数值分辨率的诊断。

Conclusion: 将后验几何的拓扑摘要与输运标签及未来实验相结合，能够比单独使用后验概率或动力学分类提供更丰富的不确定性描述。DUG框架为数值近似与加权网格下计算的持久性提供了定量控制保证。

Abstract: Uncertainty in nonlinear dynamical systems is often organized by transport structures that are not apparent from posterior geometry alone. We introduce Dynamical Uncertainty Geometry (DUG), a framework that separates three ingredients that are frequently conflated: posterior uncertainty, future observations, and model-induced transport. DUG indexes posterior credible sets and transport-conditioned subsets by a common enclosed-probability coordinate, allowing physically meaningful classes to be tracked across credibility levels rather than examined at a single threshold. The framework combines a mass-ranked credible filtration, a description of future experiments through their induced probability laws and Fisher geometry, and a labelled transport skeleton representing dynamically distinct outcomes. We establish stability results for the resulting mass-indexed persistence modules under simultaneous perturbations of posterior density and probability measure, including continuum, refinement, and finite atomic formulations. These results provide quantitative control of persistence computed from numerical approximations and weighted grids. Two benchmark problems illustrate the framework. In a short-arc orbit-determination setting, DUG identifies dynamically distinct return classes within a connected uncertainty region and highlights a difference between information-based and local Fisher-based observation-design criteria. In the Earth-Moon planar circular restricted three-body problem, DUG reveals multiple first-hit transport outcomes coexisting within a connected credible region and provides diagnostics for assessing their numerical resolution. Together, these examples show how topological summaries of posterior geometry, when coupled to transport labels and future experiments, yield a richer description of uncertainty than either posterior probabilities or dynamical classifications alone.

</details>


### [143] [Neveu--Schwarz Irregular Vertex Operators, Decomposition Theorems, and Bilinear Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12727)
*Hajime Nagoya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了Neveu–Schwarz（NS）代数的零级不规则顶点算子（rank-zero irregular vertex operator），将其定义为同秩不规则Verma模之间的线性映射，满足超共形对易关系和不规则渐近条件。在非退化假设Λ_{2p}≠0下证明了存在性和唯一性，并将分解定理推广到不规则情形。通过配对和模式插入，推导出关于Virasoro不规则共形块的（双线性微分方程（bilinear differential equations）），经过参数识别和规范变换后，所得算子与量子Painlevé V和IV tau函数方程中的算子一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不规则（irregular）顶点算子在高维共形场论和可积系统交界处的结构，特别是将NS代数的分解定理和顶点算子理论推广到不规则Verma模的设定中，并建立与量子Painlevé方程之间的联系。

Method: 构造零级不规则顶点算子作为不规则Verma模之间的线性映射；在Λ_{2p}≠0条件下证明其存在性与唯一性；将分解定理推广至不规则设定（自由费米子Fock模与秩-p NS不规则Verma模的张量积分解为Virasoro不规则Verma模的无穷直和）；利用合适的配对（pairing）和模式插入（mode insertion）推导加权和的（双线性微分算子（bilinear differential operator））。

Result: 成功构造并证明了NS代数零级不规则顶点算子的存在性和唯一性；建立了不规则分解定理及不规则顶点算子与Virasoro不规则顶点算子张量积分解的相容性；推导出类型(0,0,1)和(0,2)的Virasoro不规则共形块的加权和所满足的双线性微分方程；在Painlevé V情形下，经显式参数识别和规范变换后，所得双线性微分算子与量子Painlevé V和IV tau函数方程中出现的算子吻合。

Conclusion: 本文在数学上严格地建立了NS代数不规则顶点算子理论，填补了不规则Verma模设定下顶点算子构造的空白，并将该理论与量子Painlevé V和IV方程建立了具体的对应关系，表明了共形场论与可积系统之间深层的结构联系。

Abstract: We construct rank-zero irregular vertex operators for the Neveu--Schwarz algebra as linear maps between irregular Verma modules of the same rank satisfying the usual superconformal commutation relations and an irregular asymptotic condition. Under the nondegeneracy assumption $Λ_{2p}\neq0$, we prove their existence and uniqueness. We then extend the decomposition theorem for the Neveu--Schwarz algebra to the irregular setting: the tensor product of the free-fermion Fock module with a rank-$p$ Neveu--Schwarz irregular Verma module decomposes into an infinite direct sum of tensor products of two Virasoro irregular Verma modules. We also prove a compatible decomposition of the irregular vertex operators into tensor products of Virasoro irregular vertex operators. Using suitable pairings and mode insertions, we derive bilinear differential equations for weighted sums of products of Virasoro irregular conformal blocks of types $(0,0,1)$ and $(0,2)$. After explicit parameter identifications and a gauge transformation in the Painlevé V case, the resulting bilinear differential operators agree with those appearing in the quantum Painlevé V and IV tau-function equations.

</details>


### [144] [From Lie--Rinehart Algebras to $F$-Manifold Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12802)
*Yufeng Pei,Yunhe Sheng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为每个李-林哈特代数（Lie–Rinehart algebra）在其基代数与模的直和上构造了一个F-流形代数（F-manifold algebra），证明该结构通常并非泊松（Poisson）结构（与Loday–Vallette命题相反），并研究了相关对称泊松代数中正次数理想的幂何时为泊松理想，最后将莱布尼茨算符（Leibnizator）的迹与锚映射（anchor）联系起来，得到注入锚的刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者期望系统性地为李-林哈特代数构造F-流形代数结构，并纠正已有文献（Loday和Vallette的命题13.3.26）中断言该结构为泊松结构的错误结论，进而深入理解这种构造的代数与几何性质。

Method: 方法上，作者在基代数与模的直和上显式构造F-流形代数，系统检验其泊松性条件；通过分析对称泊松代数（symmetric Poisson algebra）中正次数理想的幂是否为泊松理想来确定结构性质；利用李-林哈特微分（Lie–Rinehart differential）刻画莱布尼茨算符，并对有限秩投射模计算莱布尼茨算符的迹以导出锚映射的相关结论。

Result: 证明该构造一般不是泊松结构，推翻了Loday–Vallette中的相关断言；确定了正次数理想幂成为泊松理想的条件；证明了有限常数秩投射模情形下莱布尼茨算符的迹可恢复锚映射，并由此获得注入锚的刚性结果；文末给出了代数和几何方面的实例。

Conclusion: 李-林哈特代数直和上的F-流形代数结构与泊松代数有本质区别，其性质可通过莱布尼茨算符与锚映射之间的联系进行刻画，该构造在代数与几何层面均有丰富的实例支撑。

Abstract: For every Lie--Rinehart algebra, we construct an $F$-manifold algebra on the
  direct sum of its base algebra and module. Contrary to the assertion in
  \cite[Proposition 13.3.26]{LodayVallette}, the resulting structure is generally
  not Poisson. We determine when powers of the positive-degree ideal in the
  associated symmetric Poisson algebra are Poisson ideals, and relate the
  Leibnizator to the Lie--Rinehart differential. For a finite projective module
  of constant rank, the trace of the Leibnizator recovers the anchor and yields a
  rigidity result for injective anchors. We conclude with algebraic and
  geometric examples.

</details>


### [145] [A discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz I superintegrable system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12899)
*Vutha Vichhea Chea,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维方格三角区域上构造了Smorodinsky–Winternitz I超可积系统的有限离散实现，并证明其在连续极限下恢复连续系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续可积系统（integrable system）实现为有限离散版本，是离散数学物理中的重要问题。本文旨在为Smorodinsky–Winternitz I超可积系统提供一种基于方格上的有限离散实现。

Method: 利用一对具有有限谱（finite spectrum）的对易数算符（number operators）及其伴随阶梯算符（ladder operators）结构，在二维方格三角区域上构造了该离散模型；并通过Tratnik型双变量对偶Hahn多项式（bivariate dual Hahn polynomials）精确求解谱问题。

Result: 所构造的模型是最大超可积（maximally superintegrable）的，其对称代数（symmetry algebra）具有Hahn代数表示；谱问题以Tratnik型双变量对偶Hahn多项式精确求解。连续极限恢复了连续Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统及其阶梯算符、本征函数和对称代数。

Conclusion: 该构造被确立为连续Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统的真正有限离散实现，表明连续系统的完整结构（算符、谱、对称代数）可在一有限离散框架中被复现。

Abstract: We construct a finite discrete realization of the Smorodinsky--Winternitz I superintegrable system on a triangular region of the two-dimensional square lattice. The construction is based on a pair of commuting number operators with finite spectrum together with an associated ladder-operator structure. We show that the resulting model is maximally superintegrable and that its symmetry algebra admits a Hahn-algebra presentation. Its spectral problem is solved exactly in terms of the bivariate dual Hahn polynomials of Tratnik type. Finally, we show that the continuum limit recovers the continuous Smorodinsky--Winternitz I system together with its ladder operators, eigenfunctions and symmetry algebra, thereby establishing the present construction as a genuine finite discrete realization of the continuous model.

</details>


### [146] [Notes on Electrostatics in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12900)
*Vladimir Gol'dshtein,Wolfgang H. Müller,Reuven Segev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种静电学（electrostatics）的紧凑表述形式，在不依赖本构关系（constitutive relations）的前提下推导出带电区域在势函数（potential function）作用下的势能表达式，并基于虚功原理（virtual work）推导出力学力泛函，其中包含非对称主动应力场 D_i E_j 的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一种不依赖本构关系（包括以太关系）的静电学紧凑表述，从而更一般化地描述带电体系的力学行为。

Method: 通过电荷势场（charge potential field）刻画电荷分布，提出带电区域在势函数下的势能表达式；假设电荷虚位移（virtual motion）过程中场做功等于势能时间导数的相反数，据此推导出力学力泛函（force functional）。

Result: 推导出的力泛函包含非对称主动应力场 D_i E_j 的作用，其中该应力场的反对称部分（skew-symmetric part）即机械力矩密度（mechanical couple density）。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑表述能够在无需本构关系的条件下统一描述静电体系的势能、力与力矩密度，为非对称应力场的力学意义提供了理论基础。

Abstract: A compact formulation of electrostatics is presented. Without using constitutive relations, including the aether relations, an expression for the potential energy of a charged region under a potential function is proposed, where the charge distribution is specified by the charge potential field. Assuming that during a virtual motion of the charge, the virtual work expended by the field is equal to minus the time derivative of the potential energy, an expression for the mechanical force functional is derived. The force functional contains the action of an asymmetric active stress field D_{i}E_{j}, the skew-symmetric part of which is the mechanical couple density.

</details>


### [147] [A discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz II superintegrable system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12909)
*Pierre-Antoine Bernard,Vutha Vichhea Chea,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维正方格子的三角区域上构造了一个离散的Smorodinsky–Winternitz II超可积系统，其对称代数具有Hahn代数表示，谱问题由混合Krawtchouk和双Hahn型双变量正交多项式精确求解，且连续极限恢复连续系统及其Hermite–Laguerre本征函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散格点背景下构造超可积系统，并将其与连续极限下的已知超可积结构相衔接，是量子可积性与特殊函数理论中的核心问题之一。

Method: 利用一对对易的有限差分数算子（finite-difference number operators）及其伴随阶梯算子（ladder operators）结构构造离散模型，并通过因子化A2-Leonard对关联的双变量正交多项式求解谱问题。

Result: 该系统被证明是最大超可积的，其对称代数具有Hahn代数（Hahn algebra）表示；谱问题精确求解为混合Krawtchouk与双Hahn型双变量正交多项式。连续极限恢复连续SW II系统及其Hermite–Laguerre本征函数，同时Hahn代数表示在极限下出现奇异性，代数结构由与笛卡尔和抛物分离变量相关的Laguerre–Heun代数描述。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了离散超可积系统与连续极限之间代数结构的过渡关系，揭示了Hahn代数和Laguerre–Heun代数在两种情形下的对应与奇异化机制。

Abstract: We construct a discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz II superintegrable system on a triangular region of the two-dimensional square lattice. The model is built from a pair of commuting finite-difference number operators with finite spectrum together with an associated ladder-operator structure. We show that it is maximally superintegrable and that its symmetry algebra admits a Hahn-algebra presentation. The spectral problem is solved exactly in terms of bivariate orthogonal polynomials of mixed Krawtchouk and dual Hahn type associated with the factorized $A_2$-Leonard pair. Finally, we show that the continuum limit recovers the continuous Smorodinsky--Winternitz II system together with its Hermite--Laguerre eigenfunctions. We further explain how the Hahn presentation of the discrete symmetry algebra becomes singular in this limit, while the limiting algebraic structure is naturally described by the Laguerre--Heun algebra associated with Cartesian and parabolic separation of variables.

</details>


### [148] [Transparent Boundary Conditions for the Heat Equation on Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12937)
*Jasur Matrasulov,Jambul Yusupov,Matthias Ehrhardt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对分支准一维度量星形图上的时变热方程，提出了透明边界条件（TBCs）的精确顶点条件，通过导出扩散系数的求和规则消除顶点处的热回流，并采用Crank-Nicolson有限差分法验证了该模型的数值有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分支准一维网络结构上控制热扩散需要确保热量在结点处无阻碍地流通，而现有方法缺乏在度量图网络上精确建立透明边界条件（TBCs）的理论框架。

Method: 将经典透明边界条件概念与度量图（metric graph）上的偏微分方程（PDE）理论相结合，推导出一个精确的顶点条件，并导出扩散系数的求和规则以消除顶点处的热回流；随后采用Crank-Nicolson有限差分法对解析模型进行数值验证。

Result: 解析推导出确保热流在结点处无阻碍流通的精确顶点条件及扩散系数求和规则；数值实验确认了热量在网络中光滑、无阻碍的传播行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为低温维结构中热扩散的可调控制与优化提供了一个实用的数学框架，可作为度量图网络上热扩散的控制工具。

Abstract: We study transparent boundary conditions (TBCs) for the time-dependent heat equation in a branching, quasi-one-dimensional domain modeled as a metric star graph. By combining the classical concept of TBCs with the theory of partial differential equations (PDEs) on networks, we derive an exact vertex condition that ensures unobstructed thermal flow across junctions. Specifically,we derive a sum rule for the diffusion coefficients that eliminates thermal backflow at the vertex.We demonstrate the validity of this analytical model numerically using a Crank-Nicolson finite-difference method, which confirms smooth, unobstructed heat propagation through the network.These results provide a practical mathematical framework for tunable control and optimization of thermal diffusion in low-dimensional structures. In particular, combining the well-known concept of the TBCs and theory for PDE on metric graphs, we propose a mathematical model providing a control tool for thermal diffusion in networks.

</details>


### [149] [Exact analytical solution for the non-selfadjoint problem of Maxwell--Cattaneo--Vernotte heat conduction with heat-transfer boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13164)
*Mátyás Szücs,Tamás Fülöp*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对带有热交换边界条件的Maxwell–Cattaneo–Vernotte方程进行了解析求解，通过建立伴随特征值问题与双正交函数系，获得了任意平方可积初始条件下的无限和解析解，并与数值差分结果进行了系统比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越傅里叶（beyond-Fourier）热传导模型在带热交换边界条件下因微分算符非自伴而难以解析求解，该问题在实验中难以直接观测到热波传播现象，因此需要一个解析基准。

Method: 通过选取合适的标量积建立伴随（adjoint）特征值问题，利用左右特征函数构成的双正交（biorthogonal）系统，将任意平方可积初始条件展开为无穷级数，从而得到解析解。

Result: 给出了平衡态与闪光脉冲（flash pulse）两类初始条件下的级数解，详细分析了特征值的实、虚及复共轭根的谱结构及其对无量纲弛豫时间和Biot数的依赖，数值验证了特征函数系的完备性，并与有限差分解吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了非自伴双曲型热传导的解析基准，揭示了边界热交换与弛豫时间对傅里叶扩散与热波传播之间过渡的影响，并开启了通过热交换实验研究超越傅里叶热传导的可能性。

Abstract: The most well-known beyond-Fourier heat conduction model, the Maxwell--Cattaneo--Vernotte equation is solved analytically in the presence of heat transfer boundary condition. In contrast to the corresponding Fourier problem, this boundary condition renders the underlying differential operator non-selfadjoint. With a suitable scalar product, the adjoint eigenvalue problem is established. The resulting left and right eigenfunctions constitute a biorthogonal system, allowing the expansion coefficients to be determined from arbitrary square-integrable initial conditions. This enables a convenient infinite-sum analytical solution, which is presented and thoroughly investigated for various values of the model parameters (including near-Fourier and highly hyperbolic regimes) and for two practically important initial conditions (equilibrium and flash pulse initiated). The spectral structure is analyzed in detail, including the occurrence of real, imaginary, and complex-conjugate eigenvalue roots, their asymptotic distribution, and their dependence on the dimensionless relaxation time and Biot number. We find good agreement with corresponding finite-difference numerical solutions. The comparison also illustrates the effects of spectral truncation (i.e., the Gibbs phenomenon) and numerical dissipation near propagating thermal-wave fronts. Completeness of the eigenfunction set is numerically demonstrated. The initial condition induced by the flash pulse is derived analytically. The results provide an analytical benchmark for non-selfadjoint hyperbolic heat conduction, unveil how boundary heat transfer and relaxation time influence the transition between Fourier-like diffusion and thermal-wave propagation, and open the possibility to find and investigate beyond-Fourier heat conduction via heat transfer in experiments and practical applications.

</details>


### [150] [On Toeplitz determinants with slow Fourier decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13182)
*Nedialko Bradinoff,Maurice Duits*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究傅里叶系数满足 f_k=O(|k|^{-1}) 的符号函数的 Toeplitz 行列式（Toeplitz determinant）det T_n(e^f)，该函数类超越 H^{1/2} 空间并包含具有 Fisher-Hartwig 奇点的符号。作者发展了一种基于 Baker–Campbell–Hausdorff 公式的算子理论方法，将 log det T_n(e^{tf}) 展开中的二次项与高阶项分离，证明二次项负责可能的增长，而所有固定阶高阶系数保持有界，并由此获得了 Fisher-Hartwig 类型符号的统一高阶系数界以及相关 CUE 线性统计量的中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Toeplitz 行列式在可积系统、随机矩阵理论和统计力学中具有核心地位，但传统结果通常限于 H^{1/2} 正则性假设。本文旨在将分析扩展到更广泛的符号类，涵盖具有 Fisher-Hartwig 奇点的情形，以理解行列式增长行为的本质来源。

Method: 作者采用算子理论方法，利用 Baker–Campbell–Hausdorff 公式对 log det T_n(e^{tf}) 进行展开，将二次项 ∑_{k=1}^{∞} min(k,n) f_k f_{-k} 与所有更高阶项分离，并分别对二者进行估计。

Result: 证明二次项完全解释了行列式随 n 的可能增长，而每个固定高阶系数保持有界。对于正负傅里叶部分均有界的符号，去掉二次贡献后得到行列式的双边估计；对于包含 Fisher-Hartwig 类型符号的更广可容许类，获得了统一的高阶系数界及 CUE 线性统计量的中心极限定理，此外还得到了 CUE 导出随机场在特征多项式之外混合指数矩的界。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 Toeplitz 行列式在 Fisher-Hartwig 奇点存在下的系统性估计框架，澄清了行列式增长的主导项结构，并为 CUE 线性统计量提供了新的概率极限定理。

Abstract: We study Toeplitz determinants $\det T_n(e^f)$ for $f$ whose Fourier coefficients satisfy $f_k=O(|k|^{-1})$. This regime extends beyond $H^{1/2}$ and includes symbols with Fisher-Hartwig singularities. We develop an operator-theoretic approach based on the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula that separates the quadratic term \[ \sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\min(k,n)f_kf_{-k} \] from the higher-order terms in the expansion of $\log\det T_n(e^{tf})$. We show that this quadratic term accounts for the possible growth with $n$, while every fixed higher-order coefficient remains bounded. For symbols with bounded positive and negative Fourier parts, our estimates yield two-sided bounds for the determinant after removal of the quadratic contribution. For a broader admissible class, including Fisher-Hartwig-type symbols, we obtain uniform higher-order coefficient bounds and a central limit theorem for the associated CUE linear statistics. We also obtain bounds on mixed exponential moments for CUE-derived random fields beyond the characteristic polynomial.

</details>


### [151] [Proper condensates and the Onsager-Penrose condition: A friendly debate on Bose-Einstein condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13402)
*Detlev Buchholz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过两位概念支持者之间的辩论形式，对比了正常凝聚体（proper condensate）与Onsager-Penrose判据在玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensation）中的定义差异，并在尾声部分讨论了求逆代数（resolvent algebra）的相关物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的定义标准存在两种不同概念——正常凝聚体与Onsager-Penrose判据，本文旨在厘清二者的区别与优劣，以辩论形式呈现两种观点的对立与论据。

Method: 采用辩论（debate）的文体形式，由两位分别代表各自概念的支持者展开讨论；序言部分介绍两种判据的基本定义，尾声部分则从求逆代数（resolvent algebra）的性质出发补充相关物理背景与评论。

Result: 文章以辩论形式系统呈现了正常凝聚体概念与Onsager-Penrose判据之间的对比，并在尾声指出求逆代数中与此语境物理相关的若干性质。

Conclusion: 两种凝聚判据各有其概念依据与适用场景，求逆代数的性质为理解这一争论提供了额外的物理视角。

Abstract: This article contrasts the concepts of a proper condensate and the Onsager-Penrose criterion for Bose-Einstein condensation in the form of a debate between two proponents of the respective concepts. The prologue briefly introduces the two criteria. The epilogue contains remarks on properties of the underlying resolvent algebra that are physically relevant in this context.

</details>


### [152] [Hidden Dyson Universality in Inverse-Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13475)
*Momo Hayashi,Kazumitsu Sakai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示Dyson普适性（Dyson universality）并非仅限于局部本征值统计，而是能在非线性逆谱重建后幸存下来，并重新出现在重建算子的矩阵几何中。通过dressing变换将展开谱映射为固定谐振子的形变，并用距离壳矩（distance-shell moments）刻画矩阵元权重的分布，该诊断指标能够区分GOE、GUE和GSE三个系综，并据此将黎曼zeta函数非平凡零点重建算子定位在GUE扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往认为Dyson普适性只体现在局部本征值统计中，本文旨在探索普适性是否能超越直接本征值统计，在非线性逆谱重建所得到的算子几何结构中留下可识别的印记，从而为量子混沌与随机矩阵理论提供新的诊断路径。

Method: 利用dressing变换将每个展开谱（unfolded spectrum）映射为固定谐振子（harmonic oscillator）的形变$f(x)$，并在公共谐振子基下用矩阵元$F_{mn}=\langle m|f|n\rangle$表示该算子。将矩阵元权重按固定距离$d=|m-n|$分解为壳层，每个壳层对应参考谐振子的能量转移通道，并用距离壳矩刻画其分布特征。

Result: 该方法在Gaussian $\beta$-系综（Gaussian $\beta$-ensembles）上独立标定后，发现距离壳矩随$\beta$平滑变化，能够有效区分GOE、GUE和GSE。将该诊断应用于黎曼zeta函数的非平凡零点时，重建算子被定位于GUE扇区。

Conclusion: 黎曼zeta函数零点的GUE特征并非通过输入能级的直接统计恢复，而是从重建算子基于距离分辨的几何结构中体现出来，说明Dyson普适性的印记能够穿透非线性逆谱重建并在矩阵几何层面重新显现。

Abstract: Dyson universality typically manifests itself in local eigenvalue statistics. Here we show that its signature survives a nonlinear inverse-spectral reconstruction and reappears in the matrix geometry of the reconstructed operator. Using a dressing transformation, we map each unfolded spectrum to a deformation $f(x)$ of a fixed harmonic oscillator and represent it in the common oscillator basis by $F_{mn}=\bra m|f|n\ket$. We resolve the matrix-element weight into shells of fixed distance $d=|m-n|$, corresponding to the energy-transfer channels of the reference oscillator, and characterize the resulting distribution by distance-shell moments. Independently calibrated on Gaussian $β$-ensembles, these moments vary smoothly with $β$ and distinguish the GOE, GUE, and GSE. With this calibration fixed, applying the same diagnostic to the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function places the reconstructed operators in the GUE sector. Thus, the GUE character of the zeros is recovered not through direct statistics of the input levels, but from the distance-resolved geometry of the reconstructed operator.

</details>


### [153] [Symmetry Emergence in Self-Organized Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13500)
*Ernesto Lupercio,Mikhail Shkolnikov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文描述了一维自组织临界性原型模型在最大密度区域中的仿射对称涌现（affine symmetry emergence）机制，并证明其倾倒函数（toppling function）的缩放极限是满足Dirichlet边界条件的Monge-Ampère方程的唯一凹解，该方程与最优传输和微分几何密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组织临界性（self-organized criticality）模型在最大密度区域中的行为尚缺乏严格的数学描述，特别是仿射对称如何涌现以及密度偏离如何精确估计的问题需要理论刻画。

Method: 论文在底层晶格网格倒数的平方远大于按指定概率测度分布的随机扰动点数的条件下，分析倾倒函数（即每个位点的操作计数）的缩放极限，将其归结为具有Dirichlet边界条件、以概率测度为势能的Monge-Ampère方程；仿射对称涌现机制基于对倾倒函数缩放极限的唯一凹解特性的归纳论证，并已升级为严格证明。

Result: 证明了倾倒函数的缩放极限是满足Dirichlet边界条件的Monge-Ampère方程在凸域上的唯一凹解；同时利用该方程可精确估计任何宏观窗口内密度偏离其最大值的偏差。该结论在归纳论证基础上升级为严格的数学证明。

Conclusion: 自组织临界性模型的仿射对称涌现由倾倒函数缩放极限满足Monge-Ampère方程这一事实所决定，该方程作为最优传输与微分几何中的已知非线性偏微分方程，为密度偏差的精确估计提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We describe a mechanism of affine symmetry emergence in the maximal density regime of the prototypical model of self-organized criticality when the inverse square of the mesh of the underlying lattice is much larger than the number of random perturbation points distributed according to a prescribed probability measure supported in the interior of the ambient convex domain. Moreover, an appropriate scaling limit of the toppling function (aka odometer), which counts the number of operations per site, is a solution to a non-linear partial differential equation well known in the context of optimal transport and differential geometry, making it possible to accurately estimate the deviation of the density from its maximal value in any macroscopic window. The mechanism for the affine symmetry emergence is due to the novel empirical fact, supported in addition by inductive arguments that have recently being upgraded to a rigorous proof, that the scaling limit of the toppling function is the unique concave solution of the Monge-Ampère equation with Dirichlet boundary condition on the convex domain with the potential given by the probability measure used above as the infinite-perturbation profile.

</details>
